============================================================================
 PluginSample.txt - reference framework this oscilloscope library was modeled on.

 This is a single-file dump of the in-house ELoad electronic-load plugin
 framework (shivarampb/ELoad_R2), kept for reference exactly as the ELoad repo
 keeps a dump of the PowerSupply framework it was itself modeled on. A new
 model author can read this end-to-end to see the whole pattern - core manager,
 plugin interface, a complete self-contained model plugin, the virtual model,
 the MockVisa emulator, and the console/GUI testers - then follow the scope
 equivalents in this repository and docs/Adding_A_New_Model.md.
============================================================================

-- ELoad.pro

# =============================================================================
#  ELoad.pro - top-level build for the ELoad electronic-load framework.
#
#  Mirrors the in-house PowerSupply framework: a core library (CELoadManager)
#  plus runtime-discovered, self-contained per-model plugin DLLs, a VISA
#  library (MockVisa here; a real NI-VISA on a bench) and the tester app.
#
#  Recommended (shadow) build:
#      mkdir build && cd build && qmake ../ELoad.pro && make -j
#  Outputs land in build/lib, build/plugins and build/bin.
# =============================================================================
TEMPLATE = subdirs
CONFIG  += ordered

SUBDIRS += \
    ELoadCore \
    MockVisa \
    PluginPLZ1005WH2 \
    PluginPLZ2005WH2 \
    PluginPLZ4005WH2 \
    PluginPLZ12005WH2 \
    PluginPLZ20005WH2 \
    PluginSimLoad \
    AutoTest \
    GuiTester

# Each folder holds a matching <name>.pro, so no .file overrides are needed.
MockVisa.depends           = ELoadCore
PluginPLZ1005WH2.depends   = ELoadCore MockVisa
PluginPLZ2005WH2.depends   = ELoadCore MockVisa
PluginPLZ4005WH2.depends   = ELoadCore MockVisa
PluginPLZ12005WH2.depends  = ELoadCore MockVisa
PluginPLZ20005WH2.depends  = ELoadCore MockVisa
PluginSimLoad.depends      = ELoadCore
AutoTest.depends           = ELoadCore
GuiTester.depends            = ELoadCore

-- ELoadCore/ELoadCore.pro

# =============================================================================
#  ELoadCore - the framework core library (CELoadManager + ELoadError).
#  Mirrors PowerSupplyCore.pro.
# =============================================================================
QT       -= gui
QT       += core

TEMPLATE  = lib
CONFIG   += c++11
DEFINES  += ELOADCORE_LIBRARY

TARGET    = ELoadCore
DESTDIR   = ../lib
INCLUDEPATH += ../include

HEADERS += \
    ../include/IELoadPlugin.h \
    ../include/ELoadManager.h \
    ../include/ELoadError.h \
    ../include/ELoadTypes.h \
    ../include/VisaHelper.h \
    ../include/visa.h

SOURCES += \
    ELoadManager.cpp \
    ELoadError.cpp \
    VisaHelper.cpp

-- ELoadCore/ELoadManager.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  ELoadManager.cpp - CELoadManager implementation.
 *
 *  Discovers model plugins with QPluginLoader, maps a load number to a
 *  plugin instance and forwards every operation. Mirrors CPowerSupplyManager.
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "ELoadManager.h"

#include <QDir>
#include <QFileInfo>

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Forwarding helper: resolve the plugin owning the load number and call it.
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
#define ELD_FWD(load, call)                                                     \
    do {                                                                        \
        CIELoadPlugin* pPlugin = getPlugin(load);                               \
        if (pPlugin == nullptr) {                                               \
            return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_LOAD_NUMBER,                 \
                QStringLiteral("no instance for load number %1").arg(load));    \
        }                                                                       \
        return pPlugin->call;                                                   \
    } while (0)

/*============================================================================
 *  Singleton / lifecycle
 *==========================================================================*/
CELoadManager& CELoadManager::instance()
{
    static CELoadManager s_instance;
    return s_instance;
}

CELoadManager::CELoadManager()
{
}

CELoadManager::~CELoadManager()
{
    m_mapInstances.clear();
    for (auto it = m_mapPlugins.begin(); it != m_mapPlugins.end(); ++it)
    {
        if (it.value().m_pLoader != nullptr)
        {
            it.value().m_pLoader->unload();
            delete it.value().m_pLoader;
        }
    }
    m_mapPlugins.clear();
}

CIELoadPlugin* CELoadManager::getPlugin(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const
{
    if (!m_mapInstances.contains(in_u32LoadNumber))
    {
        return nullptr;
    }
    const QString strName = m_mapInstances.value(in_u32LoadNumber);
    if (!m_mapPlugins.contains(strName))
    {
        return nullptr;
    }
    return m_mapPlugins.value(strName).m_pPlugin;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Plugin management
 *==========================================================================*/
void CELoadManager::loadPlugins(const QString& in_strPluginPath)
{
    QDir dirPlugins(in_strPluginPath);
    QStringList lstFilters;
#ifdef Q_OS_WIN
    lstFilters << QStringLiteral("*.dll");
#else
    lstFilters << QStringLiteral("*.so") << QStringLiteral("*.so.*");
#endif
    dirPlugins.setNameFilters(lstFilters);

    const QStringList lstFiles = dirPlugins.entryList(QDir::Files);
    for (const QString& strFileName : lstFiles)
    {
        const QString strFullPath = dirPlugins.absoluteFilePath(strFileName);
        QPluginLoader* pLoader = new QPluginLoader(strFullPath);

        QObject* pObject = pLoader->instance();
        if (pObject == nullptr)
        {
            emit pluginLoadFailed(strFileName, pLoader->errorString());
            delete pLoader;
            continue;
        }

        CIELoadPlugin* pPlugin = qobject_cast<CIELoadPlugin*>(pObject);
        if (pPlugin == nullptr)
        {
            emit pluginLoadFailed(strFileName, QStringLiteral("not an ELoad plugin"));
            pLoader->unload();
            delete pLoader;
            continue;
        }

        const S_ELoad_PluginInfo sInfo = pPlugin->getPluginInfo();
        if (!sInfo.isCompatible(getCoreVersion()))
        {
            emit pluginLoadFailed(strFileName, QStringLiteral("version incompatible"));
            pLoader->unload();
            delete pLoader;
            continue;
        }

        const QString strName = QString::fromLatin1(sInfo.m_szName);
        if (m_mapPlugins.contains(strName))
        {
            // already registered; keep the first, drop this loader
            pLoader->unload();
            delete pLoader;
            continue;
        }

        S_PluginData sData;
        sData.m_pLoader = pLoader;
        sData.m_pPlugin = pPlugin;
        sData.m_sInfo   = sInfo;
        sData.m_sCaps   = pPlugin->getCapabilities();
        m_mapPlugins.insert(strName, sData);
        emit pluginLoaded(strName);
    }
}

QStringList CELoadManager::getAvailablePlugins() const
{
    return m_mapPlugins.keys();
}

S_ELoad_PluginInfo CELoadManager::getPluginInfoByName(const QString& in_strPluginName) const
{
    return m_mapPlugins.value(in_strPluginName).m_sInfo;
}

S_ELoad_Capabilities CELoadManager::getPluginCapabilities(const QString& in_strPluginName) const
{
    return m_mapPlugins.value(in_strPluginName).m_sCaps;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Instance management
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CELoadManager::createInstance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const QString& in_strPluginName)
{
    if (in_u32LoadNumber < 1)
    {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_LOAD_NUMBER,
                          QStringLiteral("load number must be >= 1"));
    }
    if (m_mapInstances.contains(in_u32LoadNumber))
    {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::ALREADY_CONNECTED,
                          QStringLiteral("load number %1 already in use").arg(in_u32LoadNumber));
    }
    if (!m_mapPlugins.contains(in_strPluginName))
    {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND,
                          QStringLiteral("plugin \"%1\" not found").arg(in_strPluginName));
    }
    m_mapInstances.insert(in_u32LoadNumber, in_strPluginName);
    emit instanceCreated(in_u32LoadNumber, in_strPluginName);
    return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::SUCCESS);
}

ELoadError CELoadManager::destroyInstance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber)
{
    if (!m_mapInstances.contains(in_u32LoadNumber))
    {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_LOAD_NUMBER,
                          QStringLiteral("no instance for load number %1").arg(in_u32LoadNumber));
    }
    CIELoadPlugin* pPlugin = getPlugin(in_u32LoadNumber);
    if (pPlugin != nullptr && pPlugin->isConnected(in_u32LoadNumber))
    {
        pPlugin->disconnect(in_u32LoadNumber);
    }
    m_mapInstances.remove(in_u32LoadNumber);
    emit instanceDestroyed(in_u32LoadNumber);
    return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::SUCCESS);
}

bool CELoadManager::instanceExists(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const
{
    return m_mapInstances.contains(in_u32LoadNumber);
}

QString CELoadManager::getInstancePlugin(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const
{
    return m_mapInstances.value(in_u32LoadNumber);
}

S_ELoad_Capabilities CELoadManager::getCapabilities(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const
{
    CIELoadPlugin* pPlugin = getPlugin(in_u32LoadNumber);
    if (pPlugin == nullptr)
    {
        return S_ELoad_Capabilities();
    }
    return pPlugin->getCapabilities();
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Connection
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CELoadManager::connect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig& in_sConfig)
{ ELD_FWD(in_u32LoadNumber, connect(in_u32LoadNumber, in_sConfig)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::disconnect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber)
{ ELD_FWD(in_u32LoadNumber, disconnect(in_u32LoadNumber)); }
bool CELoadManager::isConnected(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const
{
    CIELoadPlugin* pPlugin = getPlugin(in_u32LoadNumber);
    return (pPlugin != nullptr) && pPlugin->isConnected(in_u32LoadNumber);
}
ELoadError CELoadManager::reset(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber)
{ ELD_FWD(in_u32LoadNumber, reset(in_u32LoadNumber)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::setTimeout(U32BIT l, U32BIT ms)
{ ELD_FWD(l, setTimeout(l, ms)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::clearStatus(U32BIT l)
{ ELD_FWD(l, clearStatus(l)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::getIdentification(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ ELD_FWD(l, getIdentification(l, o)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::selfTest(U32BIT l, S32BIT& o)
{ ELD_FWD(l, selfTest(l, o)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::getOptions(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ ELD_FWD(l, getOptions(l, o)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::waitOperationComplete(U32BIT l)
{ ELD_FWD(l, waitOperationComplete(l)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::getScpiVersion(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ ELD_FWD(l, getScpiVersion(l, o)); }
ELoadError CELoadManager::getParameterRange(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_ParamId p, S_ELoad_ParameterRange& o)
{ ELD_FWD(l, getParameterRange(l, c, p, o)); }

/*==== status / system =====================================================*/
ELoadError CELoadManager::readErrorStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus& o)
{
    CIELoadPlugin* pPlugin = getPlugin(l);
    if (pPlugin == nullptr)
    {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_LOAD_NUMBER);
    }
    ELoadError err = pPlugin->readErrorStatus(l, c, o);
    if (err.isSuccess() && o.hasError())
    {
        emit errorOccurred(l, c, o);
    }
    return err;
}

-- ELoadCore/ELoadError.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  ELoadError.cpp - implementation of the ELoadError result type.
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "ELoadError.h"

ELoadError::ELoadError()
    : m_eCode(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::SUCCESS)
    , m_strDescription(QStringLiteral("Success"))
{
}

ELoadError::ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode in_eCode, const QString& in_strDescription)
    : m_eCode(in_eCode)
    , m_strDescription(in_strDescription)
{
    if (m_strDescription.isEmpty())
    {
        m_strDescription = errorCodeToString(in_eCode);
    }
}

QString ELoadError::toString() const
{
    return QStringLiteral("[%1] %2").arg(static_cast<int>(m_eCode)).arg(m_strDescription);
}

QString ELoadError::errorCodeToString(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode in_eCode)
{
    switch (in_eCode)
    {
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::SUCCESS:                 return QStringLiteral("Success");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND:        return QStringLiteral("Plugin not found");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PLUGIN_LOAD_FAILED:      return QStringLiteral("Plugin load failed");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PLUGIN_VERSION_MISMATCH: return QStringLiteral("Plugin version mismatch");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_LOAD_NUMBER:     return QStringLiteral("Invalid load number");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::ALREADY_CONNECTED:       return QStringLiteral("Already connected");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED:           return QStringLiteral("Not connected");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_CHANNEL:         return QStringLiteral("Invalid channel");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::CONNECTION_FAILED:       return QStringLiteral("Connection failed");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::DISCONNECTION_FAILED:    return QStringLiteral("Disconnection failed");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::COMMUNICATION_TIMEOUT:   return QStringLiteral("Communication timeout");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::COMMUNICATION_ERROR:     return QStringLiteral("Communication error");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_RESPONSE:        return QStringLiteral("Invalid response");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE:  return QStringLiteral("Parameter out of range");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER:       return QStringLiteral("Invalid parameter");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_SUPPORTED:           return QStringLiteral("Operation not supported");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INSTRUMENT_ERROR:        return QStringLiteral("Instrument error");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::OVER_VOLTAGE:            return QStringLiteral("Overvoltage protection");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::OVER_CURRENT:            return QStringLiteral("Overcurrent protection");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::OVER_POWER:              return QStringLiteral("Overpower protection");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::OVER_TEMPERATURE:        return QStringLiteral("Over-temperature protection");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::UNDER_VOLTAGE:           return QStringLiteral("Undervoltage protection");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::WATCHDOG_TRIP:           return QStringLiteral("Watchdog protection");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::REVERSE_VOLTAGE:         return QStringLiteral("Reverse voltage detected");
    case Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::UNKNOWN_ERROR:           return QStringLiteral("Unknown error");
    default:                                   return QStringLiteral("Undefined error");
    }
}

QString ELoadError::deviceStatusToString(ELoad_DeviceStatus in_status)
{
    QStringList lst;
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverVoltage)     lst << QStringLiteral("Overvoltage");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverCurrent)     lst << QStringLiteral("Overcurrent");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverPower)       lst << QStringLiteral("Overpower");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverTemperature) lst << QStringLiteral("Over-temperature");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::Watchdog)        lst << QStringLiteral("Watchdog");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::CurrentLimiting) lst << QStringLiteral("Current limiting");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::PowerLimiting)   lst << QStringLiteral("Power limiting");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::UnderVoltage)    lst << QStringLiteral("Undervoltage");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::ExternalError)   lst << QStringLiteral("External error");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::ReverseVoltage)  lst << QStringLiteral("Reverse voltage");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::ConnectionError) lst << QStringLiteral("Connection error");
    if (in_status & Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::LoadOn)          lst << QStringLiteral("Load on");
    return lst.isEmpty() ? QStringLiteral("No error") : lst.join(QStringLiteral(", "));
}

QString ELoadError::operatingModeToString(Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode in_eMode)
{
    switch (in_eMode)
    {
    case Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantCurrent:    return QStringLiteral("CC");
    case Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantResistance: return QStringLiteral("CR");
    case Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantVoltage:    return QStringLiteral("CV");
    case Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantPower:      return QStringLiteral("CP");
    default:                                           return QStringLiteral("Unknown");
    }
}

QString S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus::toString() const
{
    QStringList lst;
    lst << QStringLiteral("Mode: %1").arg(ELoadError::operatingModeToString(m_EnumMode));
    lst << QStringLiteral("Status: %1").arg(ELoadError::deviceStatusToString(m_statusFlags));
    if (!m_StrErrorMessage.isEmpty())
    {
        lst << QStringLiteral("Message: %1").arg(m_StrErrorMessage);
    }
    return lst.join(QStringLiteral(" | "));
}

-- ELoadCore/VisaHelper.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  VisaHelper.cpp - translation unit for the inline VisaHelper header.
 *  (S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig::toVisaResourceString is defined inline in the header;
 *  this file gives the core library a compilation unit for it, mirroring the
 *  PowerSupply framework layout.)
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "VisaHelper.h"

-- include/ELoadTypes.h

/*============================================================================
 *  ELoadTypes.h
 *
 *  Shared value types for the ELoad plugin framework. Mirrors the structure
 *  of the PowerSupply framework's PowerSupplyTypes.h, adapted for electronic
 *  loads. Included by the core, every model plugin and the application.
 *
 *  Naming convention: because several of these types mirror the PowerSupply
 *  framework, every ELoad framework structure is named  S_ELoad_<Name>  and
 *  every enumeration  Enum_ELoad_<Name>  so the two frameworks never collide
 *  when both are used from the same application.
 *
 *  \author  ELoad framework
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef ELOADTYPES_H
#define ELOADTYPES_H

#include <QtGlobal>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>
#include <cstring>

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Import/export decoration for the public core types (ELoadCore library).
 * Defined here (the lowest-level header) so ELoadError, S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus
 * and CELoadManager all share one definition: a model plugin that links the
 * core imports these symbols on every platform (MSVC and MinGW alike), so
 * constructing an ELoadError in a plugin never becomes an unresolved symbol.
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
#if defined(ELOADCORE_LIBRARY)
#  define ELOADCORE_EXPORT Q_DECL_EXPORT
#else
#  define ELOADCORE_EXPORT Q_DECL_IMPORT
#endif

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Primitive typedefs (house style, matching the PowerSupply framework).
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
typedef unsigned int   U32BIT;
typedef int            S32BIT;
typedef unsigned short U16BIT;
typedef short          S16BIT;
typedef unsigned char  U8BIT;
typedef char           S8BIT;
typedef double         FDOUBLE;
typedef float          FFLOAT;

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Fixed field sizes for the POD plugin-info / connection structs.
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
#define PLUGIN_INFO_NAME_SIZE           32
#define PLUGIN_INFO_VERSION_SIZE        16
#define PLUGIN_INFO_MANUFACTURER_SIZE   32
#define PLUGIN_INFO_MODEL_NAME_SIZE     32
#define PLUGIN_INFO_SERIES_SIZE         32
#define PLUGIN_INFO_DESCRIPTION_SIZE    128
#define PLUGIN_INFO_MIN_CORE_VERSION    16
#define PLUGIN_INFO_MAX_CORE_VERSION    16

#define CONN_RES_STR_SIZE       128  // main VISA resource string
#define CONN_PORT_NAME_SIZE     16   // COM port or /dev/tty name
#define CONN_IP_ADDR_SIZE       16   // IPv4 address
#define CONN_USB_ID_SIZE        8    // Vendor / Product ID
#define CONN_USB_SN_SIZE        64   // USB serial number

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Communication protocol for a connection.
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
enum class Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol
{
    RS232,
    RS485,
    USB,
    GPIB,
    ETHERNET,
    LXI,
    TCPIP,
    VXI11
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * E-Load operating mode (FUNC).
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
enum class Enum_ELoad_Mode
{
    m_enumCC,     // constant current
    m_enumCR,     // constant resistance (conductance controlled)
    m_enumCV,     // constant voltage
    m_enumCP,     // constant power
    m_enumARB,    // arbitrary I-V characteristic
    m_enumUnknown
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Regulation state read back from the instrument (STAT:OPER).
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
enum class Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode
{
    m_enumConstantCurrent,      // CC
    m_enumConstantResistance,   // CR
    m_enumConstantVoltage,      // CV
    m_enumConstantPower,        // CP
    m_enumUnknown
};

enum class Enum_ELoad_CRRange       { m_enumLow, m_enumHigh };
enum class Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed { m_enumNormal, m_enumFast };
enum class Enum_ELoad_ProtAction    { m_enumLimit, m_enumTrip };
enum class Enum_ELoad_PowerOnState  { m_enumRST, m_enumRCL0, m_enumAuto };
enum class Enum_ELoad_RemoteState   { m_enumLocal, m_enumRemote, m_enumRWLock };
enum class Enum_ELoad_Transition    { m_enumImmediate, m_enumRamp };
enum class Enum_ELoad_IntegGate     { m_enumNone, m_enumLoadOn, m_enumProgRun };

enum class Enum_ELoad_AcqTrigSource {
    m_enumImmediate, m_enumBus, m_enumDigital2, m_enumTALink, m_enumMSync, m_enumLoadOff
};
enum class Enum_ELoad_TranTrigSource {
    m_enumImmediate, m_enumBus, m_enumDigital2, m_enumMSync
};
enum class Enum_ELoad_SeqState {
    m_enumIdle, m_enumWaiting, m_enumRunning, m_enumSuspended, m_enumPulse, m_enumSine
};
enum class Enum_ELoad_DataItem {
    m_enumCurrent, m_enumVoltage, m_enumPower, m_enumETime, m_enumCapacity, m_enumEnergy
};
enum class Enum_ELoad_Beeper {
    m_enumAll, m_enumKey, m_enumProtection, m_enumScpi
};
enum class Enum_ELoad_CutoffType {
    m_enumVoltage, m_enumCapacity, m_enumEnergy, m_enumTime
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig - how to reach one instrument (protocol + parameters).
 * POD-ish; provides toVisaResourceString() (see VisaHelper.h).
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
struct S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig
{
    Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol m_enumProtocol;

    S8BIT  m_szResourceString[CONN_RES_STR_SIZE];   // explicit VISA resource

    S8BIT  m_szPortName[CONN_PORT_NAME_SIZE];
    U32BIT m_u32BaudRate;

    S8BIT  m_szIpAddress[CONN_IP_ADDR_SIZE];
    U32BIT m_u32Port;

    U32BIT m_u32GpibAddress;
    U32BIT m_u32GpibBoard;

    S8BIT  m_szUsbVendorId[CONN_USB_ID_SIZE];
    S8BIT  m_szUsbProductId[CONN_USB_ID_SIZE];
    S8BIT  m_szUsbSerialNumber[CONN_USB_SN_SIZE];

    U32BIT m_u32DeviceAddress;    // daisy-chain address (0 = not used)
    U32BIT m_u32Timeout;          // communication timeout [ms]

    S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig()
        : m_enumProtocol(Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::USB)
        , m_u32BaudRate(19200)
        , m_u32Port(5025)
        , m_u32GpibAddress(0)
        , m_u32GpibBoard(0)
        , m_u32DeviceAddress(0)
        , m_u32Timeout(3000)
    {
        memset(m_szResourceString, 0, sizeof(m_szResourceString));
        memset(m_szPortName, 0, sizeof(m_szPortName));
        memset(m_szIpAddress, 0, sizeof(m_szIpAddress));
        memset(m_szUsbVendorId, 0, sizeof(m_szUsbVendorId));
        memset(m_szUsbProductId, 0, sizeof(m_szUsbProductId));
        memset(m_szUsbSerialNumber, 0, sizeof(m_szUsbSerialNumber));
    }

    // Convenience: build from an explicit VISA resource string.
    void setResourceString(const QString& in_strResource)
    {
        const QByteArray aby = in_strResource.toLatin1();
        qstrncpy(m_szResourceString, aby.constData(), CONN_RES_STR_SIZE);
    }

    QString toVisaResourceString() const;   // implemented in VisaHelper.h
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * S_ELoad_PluginInfo - static plugin metadata reported by getPluginInfo().
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
struct S_ELoad_PluginInfo
{
    S8BIT m_szName[PLUGIN_INFO_NAME_SIZE];
    S8BIT m_szVersion[PLUGIN_INFO_VERSION_SIZE];
    S8BIT m_szManufacturer[PLUGIN_INFO_MANUFACTURER_SIZE];
    S8BIT m_szModelName[PLUGIN_INFO_MODEL_NAME_SIZE];
    S8BIT m_szSeries[PLUGIN_INFO_SERIES_SIZE];
    S8BIT m_szDescription[PLUGIN_INFO_DESCRIPTION_SIZE];
    S8BIT m_szMinCoreVersion[PLUGIN_INFO_MIN_CORE_VERSION];
    S8BIT m_szMaxCoreVersion[PLUGIN_INFO_MAX_CORE_VERSION];
    QStringList m_StrlstSupportedProtocols;
    QStringList m_StrlstSupportedModes;

    S_ELoad_PluginInfo()
    {
        memset(m_szName, 0, sizeof(m_szName));
        memset(m_szVersion, 0, sizeof(m_szVersion));
        memset(m_szManufacturer, 0, sizeof(m_szManufacturer));
        memset(m_szModelName, 0, sizeof(m_szModelName));
        memset(m_szSeries, 0, sizeof(m_szSeries));
        memset(m_szDescription, 0, sizeof(m_szDescription));
        memset(m_szMinCoreVersion, 0, sizeof(m_szMinCoreVersion));
        memset(m_szMaxCoreVersion, 0, sizeof(m_szMaxCoreVersion));
    }

    bool isCompatible(const QString& in_strCoreVersion) const
    {
        const QString strMin = QString::fromLatin1(m_szMinCoreVersion);
        const QString strMax = QString::fromLatin1(m_szMaxCoreVersion);
        return (in_strCoreVersion >= strMin && in_strCoreVersion <= strMax);
    }
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Per-channel capabilities. For the PLZ-5WH2 single-input loads there is one
 * channel (channel 1).
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
struct S_ELoad_ChannelCapabilities
{
    U32BIT  m_u32ChannelNumber;
    FDOUBLE m_dMaxVoltage;
    FDOUBLE m_dMaxCurrent;
    FDOUBLE m_dMaxPower;
    FDOUBLE m_dMaxConductance;   // CR high-range maximum [S]
    FDOUBLE m_dMinVoltage;
    FDOUBLE m_dMinCurrent;
    bool    m_bHasOCP;
    bool    m_bHasOPP;
    bool    m_bHasUVP;
    bool    m_bHasOVP;           // programmable OVP (fixed hardware on PLZ-5WH2)
    bool    m_bHasARB;
    bool    m_bHasSequence;
    bool    m_bHasPulseSine;

    S_ELoad_ChannelCapabilities()
        : m_u32ChannelNumber(1)
        , m_dMaxVoltage(0.0), m_dMaxCurrent(0.0), m_dMaxPower(0.0)
        , m_dMaxConductance(0.0), m_dMinVoltage(0.0), m_dMinCurrent(0.0)
        , m_bHasOCP(false), m_bHasOPP(false), m_bHasUVP(false), m_bHasOVP(false)
        , m_bHasARB(false), m_bHasSequence(false), m_bHasPulseSine(false)
    {}
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Whole-instrument capabilities reported by getCapabilities().
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
struct S_ELoad_Capabilities
{
    U32BIT  m_u32NumberOfChannels;
    FDOUBLE m_dMaxVoltage;
    FDOUBLE m_dMaxCurrent;
    FDOUBLE m_dMaxPower;
    bool    m_bHasOCP;
    bool    m_bHasOPP;
    bool    m_bHasUVP;
    bool    m_bHasOVP;
    bool    m_bSupportsParallel;
    U32BIT  m_u32MaxParallelUnits;
    QList<S_ELoad_ChannelCapabilities> m_QlistChannels;

    S_ELoad_Capabilities()
        : m_u32NumberOfChannels(1)
        , m_dMaxVoltage(0.0), m_dMaxCurrent(0.0), m_dMaxPower(0.0)
        , m_bHasOCP(false), m_bHasOPP(false), m_bHasUVP(false), m_bHasOVP(false)
        , m_bSupportsParallel(false), m_u32MaxParallelUnits(1)
    {}

    S_ELoad_ChannelCapabilities getChannelCapabilities(U32BIT in_u32Channel) const
    {
        for (const S_ELoad_ChannelCapabilities& ch : m_QlistChannels)
        {
            if (ch.m_u32ChannelNumber == in_u32Channel)
            {
                return ch;
            }
        }
        S_ELoad_ChannelCapabilities sDef;
        sDef.m_u32ChannelNumber = in_u32Channel;
        sDef.m_dMaxVoltage = m_dMaxVoltage;
        sDef.m_dMaxCurrent = m_dMaxCurrent;
        sDef.m_dMaxPower   = m_dMaxPower;
        sDef.m_bHasOCP = m_bHasOCP;
        sDef.m_bHasOPP = m_bHasOPP;
        sDef.m_bHasUVP = m_bHasUVP;
        sDef.m_bHasOVP = m_bHasOVP;
        return sDef;
    }
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Numeric range descriptor for a parameter (auto-ranging UIs).
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
struct S_ELoad_ParameterRange
{
    FDOUBLE m_dMin;
    FDOUBLE m_dMax;
    FDOUBLE m_dResolution;
    QList<FDOUBLE> m_QlistDiscreteValues;   // non-empty for discrete params

    S_ELoad_ParameterRange() : m_dMin(0.0), m_dMax(0.0), m_dResolution(0.0) {}
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Parameter identifiers for getParameterRange().
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
enum class Enum_ELoad_ParamId
{
    m_enumCurrent, m_enumVoltage, m_enumConductanceHigh, m_enumConductanceLow,
    m_enumPower, m_enumSlewRate, m_enumSoftStart, m_enumOcpLevel, m_enumOppLevel,
    m_enumUvpLevel, m_enumWatchdogDelay, m_enumCutoffVoltage, m_enumCutoffCapacity,
    m_enumCutoffEnergy, m_enumCutoffTime, m_enumTimedInput, m_enumPulseFrequency,
    m_enumPulseDuty, m_enumSineFrequency, m_enumArbResponse, m_enumSenseAperture,
    m_enumBufferSize, m_enumAcqTrigCount, m_enumAcqTrigDelay, m_enumAcqIntervalTime,
    m_enumTranTrigDelay, m_enumProgramLoop, m_enumProgramStepCount, m_enumStepDwell,
    m_enumStepSlewRate, m_enumScreenSaverDelay
};

#endif // ELOADTYPES_H

-- include/ELoadError.h

/*============================================================================
 *  ELoadError.h
 *
 *  Error/result type for the ELoad framework. Mirrors PowerSupplyError.h:
 *  a returned ELoadError object carrying an Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode plus a descriptive
 *  string, the device status flags and the S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus aggregate.
 *
 *  \author  ELoad framework
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef ELOADERROR_H
#define ELOADERROR_H

#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>
#include <QFlags>
#include <QDebug>

#include "ELoadTypes.h"

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode - result codes returned through the ELoadError object.
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
enum class Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode
{
    SUCCESS = 0,

    // framework / plugin
    PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND      = 1000,
    PLUGIN_LOAD_FAILED    = 1001,
    PLUGIN_VERSION_MISMATCH = 1002,
    INVALID_LOAD_NUMBER   = 1003,
    ALREADY_CONNECTED     = 1004,
    NOT_CONNECTED         = 1005,
    INVALID_CHANNEL       = 1006,

    // communication
    CONNECTION_FAILED     = 2000,
    DISCONNECTION_FAILED  = 2001,
    COMMUNICATION_TIMEOUT = 2002,
    COMMUNICATION_ERROR   = 2003,
    INVALID_RESPONSE      = 2004,

    // parameter / usage
    PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE = 3000,
    INVALID_PARAMETER      = 3001,
    NOT_SUPPORTED          = 3002,

    // instrument-reported
    INSTRUMENT_ERROR       = 4000,
    OVER_VOLTAGE           = 4001,
    OVER_CURRENT           = 4002,
    OVER_POWER             = 4003,
    OVER_TEMPERATURE       = 4004,
    UNDER_VOLTAGE          = 4005,
    WATCHDOG_TRIP          = 4006,
    REVERSE_VOLTAGE        = 4007,

    UNKNOWN_ERROR          = 9999
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Decoded protection / device status flags (from STAT:QUES:COND).
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
enum class Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag
{
    NoError          = 0x0000,
    OverVoltage      = 0x0001,
    OverCurrent      = 0x0002,
    OverPower        = 0x0004,
    OverTemperature  = 0x0008,
    Watchdog         = 0x0010,
    CurrentLimiting  = 0x0020,
    PowerLimiting    = 0x0040,
    UnderVoltage     = 0x0080,
    ExternalError    = 0x0100,
    ReverseVoltage   = 0x0200,
    ConnectionError  = 0x0400,
    LoadOn           = 0x0800
};
Q_DECLARE_FLAGS(ELoad_DeviceStatus, Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag)
Q_DECLARE_OPERATORS_FOR_FLAGS(ELoad_DeviceStatus)

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Aggregate error/status snapshot filled by readErrorStatus().
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
struct ELOADCORE_EXPORT S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus
{
    ELoad_DeviceStatus       m_statusFlags;
    Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode m_EnumMode;
    QString            m_StrErrorMessage;
    S32BIT             m_iStandardEventStatus;   // *ESR?
    S32BIT             m_iQuestionableStatus;    // STAT:QUES?
    S32BIT             m_iOperationStatus;       // STAT:OPER?

    S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus()
        : m_statusFlags(Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::NoError)
        , m_EnumMode(Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumUnknown)
        , m_iStandardEventStatus(0)
        , m_iQuestionableStatus(0)
        , m_iOperationStatus(0)
    {}

    bool hasError() const
    {
        return static_cast<int>(m_statusFlags) != 0;
    }

    QString toString() const;
};

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * ELoadError - the value returned by every framework/plugin operation.
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
class ELOADCORE_EXPORT ELoadError
{
public:
    ELoadError();
    ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode in_eCode, const QString& in_strDescription = QString());

    Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode code() const { return m_eCode; }
    QString description() const { return m_strDescription; }
    QString toString() const;
    bool isSuccess() const { return m_eCode == Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::SUCCESS; }

    static QString errorCodeToString(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode in_eCode);
    static QString deviceStatusToString(ELoad_DeviceStatus in_status);
    static QString operatingModeToString(Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode in_eMode);

private:
    Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode m_eCode;
    QString     m_strDescription;
};

#endif // ELOADERROR_H

-- include/IELoadPlugin.h

/*============================================================================
 *  IELoadPlugin.h
 *
 *  Abstract plugin interface for an electronic-load model. Mirrors the
 *  PowerSupply framework's CIPowerSupplyPlugin: a Qt plugin interface loaded
 *  with QPluginLoader and resolved with qobject_cast. Each model plugin is a
 *  QObject deriving from CIELoadPlugin and declaring:
 *      Q_PLUGIN_METADATA(IID ELoadPlugin_iid)
 *      Q_INTERFACES(CIELoadPlugin)
 *
 *  Every operation takes the logical load number (1-based) and, where it
 *  applies, the channel (1-based; the PLZ-5WH2 loads are single-channel).
 *  Non-mandatory operations default to NOT_SUPPORTED, so a plugin overrides
 *  only what its model provides (e.g. the PLZ-5WH2 family does not override
 *  setOVP/readOVP because its OVP is fixed hardware).
 *
 *  \author  ELoad framework
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef IELOADPLUGIN_H
#define IELOADPLUGIN_H

#include <QtPlugin>
#include <QVector>

#include "ELoadError.h"
#include "ELoadTypes.h"

#define ELoadPlugin_iid "com.automation.ELoadPlugin/1.0"

/* Default body for a not-overridden (unsupported) operation. Resolves to the
 * protected static CIELoadPlugin::NotSupported() in complete-class context. */
#define ELD_NS() return CIELoadPlugin::NotSupported()

/* The many default NOT_SUPPORTED stubs deliberately ignore their (named, for
 * documentation) parameters; silence -Wunused-parameter for this interface. */
#if defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__clang__)
#  pragma GCC diagnostic push
#  pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wunused-parameter"
#endif

class CIELoadPlugin
{
public:
    virtual ~CIELoadPlugin() {}

    /*==== plugin information (mandatory) ===================================*/
    virtual S_ELoad_PluginInfo        getPluginInfo() const = 0;
    virtual S_ELoad_Capabilities getCapabilities() const = 0;

    /*==== connection management (mandatory) ===============================*/
    virtual ELoadError connect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig& in_sConfig) = 0;
    virtual ELoadError disconnect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) = 0;
    virtual bool       isConnected(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const = 0;
    virtual ELoadError reset(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) = 0;

    virtual ELoadError setTimeout(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32TimeoutMs) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError clearStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getIdentification(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strIdn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError selfTest(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, S32BIT& out_iResult) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getOptions(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strOptions) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError waitOperationComplete(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getScpiVersion(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strVersion) { ELD_NS(); }

    virtual ELoadError getParameterRange(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel,
                                         Enum_ELoad_ParamId in_eParam, S_ELoad_ParameterRange& out_sRange) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== mode ============================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError setMode(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_Mode in_eMode) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getMode(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_Mode& out_eMode) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setCVOption(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getCVOption(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getOperatingMode(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode& out_eMode) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== setpoints =======================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError setCurrent(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dAmps) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readbackSetCurrent(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dAmps) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setVoltage(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dVolts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readbackSetVoltage(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dVolts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setConductance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dSiemens) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readbackSetConductance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dSiemens) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setResistance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dOhms) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readbackSetResistance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dOhms) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setPower(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dWatts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readbackSetPower(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dWatts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setCRRange(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_CRRange in_eRange) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getCRRange(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_CRRange& out_eRange) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setCVResponse(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed in_eResp) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getCVResponse(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed& out_eResp) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setCRResponse(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed in_eResp) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getCRResponse(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed& out_eResp) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setSlewRate(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dAmpsPerUs) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getSlewRate(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dAmpsPerUs) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setSoftStart(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getSoftStart(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setExtCurrentSum(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getExtCurrentSum(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setExtFullControl(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_Mode in_eMode, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getExtFullControl(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_Mode in_eMode, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== load input ======================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError enableInput(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError isInputEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError enableInputTimed(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, U32BIT in_u32Seconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setPowerOnState(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_PowerOnState in_eState) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getPowerOnState(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_PowerOnState& out_eState) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setExternalInputLogic(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bNegative) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getExternalInputLogic(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bNegative) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setMSyncAccept(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getMSyncAccept(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError sendMSyncLoad(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== cut-off =========================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError setCutoff(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType in_eType, FDOUBLE in_dValue) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getCutoff(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType in_eType, FDOUBLE& out_dValue) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError enableCutoff(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType in_eType, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError isCutoffEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType in_eType, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== protections =====================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError setOCP(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dAmps) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readOCP(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dAmps) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setOCPAction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ProtAction in_eAction) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getOCPAction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ProtAction& out_eAction) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setOPP(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dWatts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readOPP(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dWatts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setOPPAction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ProtAction in_eAction) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getOPPAction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ProtAction& out_eAction) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setUVP(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dVolts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readUVP(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dVolts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setUVPAction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ProtAction in_eAction) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getUVPAction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ProtAction& out_eAction) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setUVPEnable(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError isUVPEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setWatchdog(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError isWatchdogEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setWatchdogDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getWatchdogDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setOVP(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dVolts) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readOVP(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dVolts) { ELD_NS(); }

    virtual ELoadError clearProtection(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== measurements ====================================================*/

    virtual ELoadError measureCurrent(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dAmps) { ELD_NS(); }

    virtual ELoadError getSenseAperture(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setDisplayItem(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_DataItem in_eItem, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getDisplayItem(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_DataItem in_eItem, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== data logger and integration =====================================*/

    virtual ELoadError getSineFrequency(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dHertz) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError startSine(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError stopSine(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== ARB =============================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError setArbCount(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, U32BIT in_u32Count) { ELD_NS(); }

    virtual ELoadError listPrograms(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QStringList& out_lstNames) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setProgramLoop(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, S32BIT in_iLoop) { ELD_NS(); }         // -1 = INFinite
    virtual ELoadError getProgramLoop(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, S32BIT& out_iLoop) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setProgramStepCount(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Count) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getProgramStepCount(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Count) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setProgramProtection(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_Mode in_eWhich, FDOUBLE in_dLevel) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setStepLevel(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, FDOUBLE in_dLevel) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getStepLevel(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, FDOUBLE& out_dLevel) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setStepDwell(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getStepDwell(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setStepInput(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getStepInput(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setStepTransition(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, Enum_ELoad_Transition in_eTransition) { ELD_NS(); }

    virtual ELoadError setStepTriggerOut(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, U32BIT in_u32Flags) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getStepTriggerOut(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, U32BIT& out_u32Flags) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setStepTriggerWait(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, U32BIT in_u32Flags) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getStepTriggerWait(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Step, U32BIT& out_u32Flags) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError startSequence(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError abortSequence(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) { ELD_NS(); }

    virtual ELoadError getSequenceState(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_SeqState& out_eState, U32BIT& out_u32Step, U32BIT& out_u32Loop) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== triggers ========================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError setAcqTriggerSource(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_AcqTrigSource in_eSource) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getAcqTriggerSource(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_AcqTrigSource& out_eSource) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setAcqTriggerCount(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Count) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getAcqTriggerCount(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Count) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setAcqTriggerDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getAcqTriggerDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setAcqIntervalEnable(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getAcqIntervalEnable(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setAcqIntervalTime(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getAcqIntervalTime(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError initiateAcquire(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setTranTriggerSource(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_TranTrigSource in_eSource) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getTranTriggerSource(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_TranTrigSource& out_eSource) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setTranTriggerDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getTranTriggerDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError sendTrigger(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError abort(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError abortAcquire(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== memories ========================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError saveSettings(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Location) { ELD_NS(); }   // *SAV 0..19
    virtual ELoadError recallSettings(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Location) { ELD_NS(); } // *RCL 0..19
    virtual ELoadError savePreset(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Slot) { ELD_NS(); }         // MEM 1..3
    virtual ELoadError recallPreset(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Slot) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== status / system =================================================*/
    virtual ELoadError readErrorStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus& out_sStatus) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError clearErrorStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError queryErrorQueue(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strMessage) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getInstrumentErrorCount(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Count) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readStandardEventStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Status) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readStatusByte(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Status) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readQuestionableStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, U32BIT& out_u32Status) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError readOperationStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, U32BIT& out_u32Status) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setRemoteState(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_RemoteState in_eState) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getRemoteState(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_RemoteState& out_eState) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setKeyLock(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError isKeyLocked(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setKeyLockLevel(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Level) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getKeyLockLevel(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Level) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setBeeper(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_Beeper in_eWhich, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getBeeper(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_Beeper in_eWhich, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setRemoteSensing(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError isRemoteSensingEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setDigital2Direction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool in_bOutput) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getDigital2Direction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool& out_bOutput) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getMSyncIndex(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Index) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setScreenSaver(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool in_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError isScreenSaverEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool& out_bOn) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError setScreenSaverDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError getScreenSaverDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds) { ELD_NS(); }

    /*==== debug escape hatch (diagnostics only) ===========================*/
    virtual ELoadError writeScpi(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const QString& in_strCommand) { ELD_NS(); }
    virtual ELoadError queryScpi(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const QString& in_strCommand, QString& out_strResponse) { ELD_NS(); }

protected:
    // helper used by the default (not-overridden) bodies
    static ELoadError NotSupported()
    {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_SUPPORTED, QStringLiteral("operation not supported by this model"));
    }
};

Q_DECLARE_INTERFACE(CIELoadPlugin, ELoadPlugin_iid)

-- include/ELoadManager.h

/*============================================================================
 *  ELoadManager.h
 *
 *  CELoadManager - the framework front end. Mirrors CPowerSupplyManager:
 *  a QObject singleton that discovers plugins with QPluginLoader, maps a
 *  logical load number to a loaded plugin instance, and forwards every
 *  operation to the plugin that owns that load number.
 *
 *  Usage:
 *      CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
 *      mgr.loadPlugins("plugins");
 *      mgr.createInstance(1, "PLZ4005WH2");
 *      S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig cfg; cfg.setResourceString("USB0::0x0B3E::0x1042::X::INSTR");
 *      mgr.connect(1, cfg);
 *      mgr.setMode(1, 1, Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC);
 *      mgr.setCurrent(1, 1, 2.5);
 *      mgr.enableInput(1, 1, true);
 *
 *  \author  ELoad framework
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef ELOADMANAGER_H
#define ELOADMANAGER_H

#include <QObject>
#include <QMap>
#include <QPluginLoader>
#include <QVector>

#include "IELoadPlugin.h"
#include "ELoadError.h"
#include "ELoadTypes.h"   // provides ELOADCORE_EXPORT

/* Live SCPI trace tap (optional). Direction: 0=TX 1=RX 2=ERR 3=INFO. */
typedef void (*ELoadTraceCallback)(void* in_pvUser, U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber,
                                   int in_iDirection, const QString& in_strText);

class ELOADCORE_EXPORT CELoadManager : public QObject
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    CELoadManager();
    ~CELoadManager();

    static CELoadManager& instance();

    /*==== plugin management ==============================================*/
    void        loadPlugins(const QString& in_strPluginPath);
    QStringList getAvailablePlugins() const;
    S_ELoad_PluginInfo getPluginInfoByName(const QString& in_strPluginName) const;
    S_ELoad_Capabilities getPluginCapabilities(const QString& in_strPluginName) const;
    QString     getCoreVersion() const { return QStringLiteral("1.0.0"); }

    /*==== instance management ============================================*/
    ELoadError createInstance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const QString& in_strPluginName);
    ELoadError destroyInstance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber);
    bool       instanceExists(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const;
    QString    getInstancePlugin(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const;
    S_ELoad_Capabilities getCapabilities(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const;

    /*==== connection =====================================================*/
    ELoadError connect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig& in_sConfig);
    ELoadError disconnect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber);
    bool       isConnected(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const;
    ELoadError reset(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber);
    ELoadError setTimeout(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32TimeoutMs);
    ELoadError clearStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber);
    ELoadError getIdentification(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strIdn);
    ELoadError selfTest(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, S32BIT& out_iResult);
    ELoadError getOptions(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strOptions);
    ELoadError waitOperationComplete(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber);
    ELoadError getScpiVersion(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strVersion);
    ELoadError getParameterRange(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel,
                                 Enum_ELoad_ParamId in_eParam, S_ELoad_ParameterRange& out_sRange);

    /*==== mode ===========================================================*/
    ELoadError setMode(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_Mode in_eMode);
    ELoadError getMode(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_Mode& out_eMode);
    ELoadError setCVOption(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn);
    ELoadError getCVOption(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn);
    ELoadError getOperatingMode(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode& out_eMode);

    /*==== setpoints ======================================================*/
    ELoadError setCurrent(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dAmps);
    ELoadError readbackSetCurrent(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dAmps);
    ELoadError setVoltage(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dVolts);
    ELoadError readbackSetVoltage(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dVolts);
    ELoadError setConductance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dSiemens);
    ELoadError readbackSetConductance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dSiemens);
    ELoadError setResistance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dOhms);
    ELoadError readbackSetResistance(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dOhms);
    ELoadError setPower(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dWatts);
    ELoadError readbackSetPower(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dWatts);
    ELoadError setCRRange(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_CRRange in_eRange);
    ELoadError getCRRange(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_CRRange& out_eRange);
    ELoadError setCVResponse(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed in_eResp);
    ELoadError getCVResponse(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed& out_eResp);
    ELoadError setCRResponse(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed in_eResp);
    ELoadError getCRResponse(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed& out_eResp);
    ELoadError setSlewRate(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dAmpsPerUs);
    ELoadError getSlewRate(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dAmpsPerUs);
    ELoadError setSoftStart(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds);
    ELoadError getSoftStart(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds);
    ELoadError setExtCurrentSum(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn);
    ELoadError getExtCurrentSum(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn);
    ELoadError setExtFullControl(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_Mode in_eMode, bool in_bOn);
    ELoadError getExtFullControl(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_Mode in_eMode, bool& out_bOn);

    /*==== load input =====================================================*/
    ELoadError enableInput(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn);
    ELoadError isInputEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn);
    ELoadError enableInputTimed(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, U32BIT in_u32Seconds);

    ELoadError getExternalInputLogic(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bNegative);

    /*==== measurements ===================================================*/

    ELoadError measureEnergy(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dWattHours);
    ELoadError measureElapsedTime(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds);

    /*==== data logger / integration ======================================*/

    ELoadError getDataFormat(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, QList<Enum_ELoad_DataItem>& out_lstItems);

    ELoadError readDataBuffer(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, QVector<FDOUBLE>& out_vecValues, U32BIT in_u32MaxValues);

    ELoadError getIntegrationGate(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_IntegGate& out_eGate);

    ELoadError startIntegration(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel);
    ELoadError stopIntegration(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel);

    /*==== pulse / sine ===================================================*/

    /*==== ARB ============================================================*/

    /*==== sequence =======================================================*/

    ELoadError readOperationStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, U32BIT& out_u32Status);

    ELoadError getRemoteState(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_RemoteState& out_eState);
