    ELoadError isKeyLocked(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool& out_bOn);
    ELoadError setKeyLockLevel(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Level);
    ELoadError getKeyLockLevel(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Level);
    ELoadError setBeeper(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_Beeper in_eWhich, bool in_bOn);
    ELoadError getBeeper(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, Enum_ELoad_Beeper in_eWhich, bool& out_bOn);
    ELoadError setRemoteSensing(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool in_bOn);
    ELoadError isRemoteSensingEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, bool& out_bOn);
    ELoadError setDigital2Direction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool in_bOutput);
    ELoadError getDigital2Direction(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool& out_bOutput);
    ELoadError getMSyncIndex(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT& out_u32Index);
    ELoadError setScreenSaver(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool in_bOn);
    ELoadError isScreenSaverEnabled(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, bool& out_bOn);
    ELoadError setScreenSaverDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE in_dSeconds);
    ELoadError getScreenSaverDelay(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, FDOUBLE& out_dSeconds);

    /*==== debug ==========================================================*/
    ELoadError writeScpi(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const QString& in_strCommand);
    ELoadError queryScpi(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const QString& in_strCommand, QString& out_strResponse);

signals:
    void pluginLoaded(const QString& in_strPluginName);
    void pluginLoadFailed(const QString& in_strFileName, const QString& in_strError);
    void instanceCreated(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const QString& in_strPluginName);
    void instanceDestroyed(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber);
    void errorOccurred(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, const S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus& in_sStatus);

private:
    CELoadManager(const CELoadManager&) = delete;
    CELoadManager& operator=(const CELoadManager&) = delete;

    CIELoadPlugin* getPlugin(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const;

    struct S_PluginData
    {
        QPluginLoader*      m_pLoader;
        CIELoadPlugin*      m_pPlugin;
        S_ELoad_PluginInfo        m_sInfo;
        S_ELoad_Capabilities m_sCaps;
        S_PluginData() : m_pLoader(nullptr), m_pPlugin(nullptr) {}
    };

    QMap<QString, S_PluginData> m_mapPlugins;    // plugin name -> data
    QMap<U32BIT, QString>       m_mapInstances;  // load number -> plugin name
};

#endif // ELOADMANAGER_H

-- include/VisaHelper.h

/*============================================================================
 *  VisaHelper.h
 *
 *  Inline implementation of S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig::toVisaResourceString(), which
 *  builds a VISA resource string from the connection parameters. Mirrors the
 *  PowerSupply framework's VisaHelper.h.
 *
 *  \author  ELoad framework
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef VISAHELPER_H
#define VISAHELPER_H

#include <QString>
#include "ELoadTypes.h"

inline QString S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig::toVisaResourceString() const
{
    const QString strPrimary   = QString::fromLocal8Bit(m_szResourceString);
    const QString strPortName  = QString::fromLocal8Bit(m_szPortName);
    const QString strIpAddress = QString::fromLocal8Bit(m_szIpAddress);
    const QString strVendorId  = QString::fromLocal8Bit(m_szUsbVendorId);
    const QString strProductId = QString::fromLocal8Bit(m_szUsbProductId);
    const QString strSerial    = QString::fromLocal8Bit(m_szUsbSerialNumber);

    // An explicit resource string always wins.
    if (!strPrimary.isEmpty())
    {
        return strPrimary;
    }

    QString strResource;
    switch (m_enumProtocol)
    {
    case Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::RS232:
    case Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::RS485:
        if (strPortName.toUpper().startsWith(QStringLiteral("COM")))
        {
            strResource = QStringLiteral("ASRL%1::INSTR").arg(strPortName.mid(3));
        }
        else
        {
            strResource = QStringLiteral("ASRL%1::INSTR").arg(strPortName);
        }
        break;

    case Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::GPIB:
        strResource = QStringLiteral("GPIB%1::%2::INSTR")
                .arg(m_u32GpibBoard).arg(m_u32GpibAddress);
        break;

    case Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::USB:
        if (!strVendorId.isEmpty() && !strProductId.isEmpty())
        {
            if (!strSerial.isEmpty())
            {
                strResource = QStringLiteral("USB0::%1::%2::%3::INSTR")
                        .arg(strVendorId, strProductId, strSerial);
            }
            else
            {
                strResource = QStringLiteral("USB0::%1::%2::INSTR")
                        .arg(strVendorId, strProductId);
            }
        }
        else
        {
            strResource = QStringLiteral("USB0::INSTR");
        }
        break;

    case Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::TCPIP:
    case Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::ETHERNET:
    case Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::LXI:
        if (m_u32Port != 5025)
        {
            strResource = QStringLiteral("TCPIP0::%1::%2::SOCKET")
                    .arg(strIpAddress).arg(m_u32Port);
        }
        else
        {
            strResource = QStringLiteral("TCPIP0::%1::INSTR").arg(strIpAddress);
        }
        break;

    case Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::VXI11:
        strResource = QStringLiteral("TCPIP0::%1::inst0::INSTR").arg(strIpAddress);
        break;

    default:
        strResource = QStringLiteral("INSTR");
        break;
    }
    return strResource;
}

#endif // VISAHELPER_H

-- include/visa.h

/*============================================================================
 *  visa.h  (project-local, minimal)
 *
 *  A minimal, ABI-correct subset of the VPP-4.3.2 VISA C API - enough for the
 *  ELoad model plugins to talk SCPI to instruments. Provided so the plugins
 *  can "#include <visa.h>" and link -lvisa on any platform:
 *    - on Windows with NI-VISA / Keysight IO Libraries, point the plugin's
 *      INCLUDEPATH at the vendor include dir instead of this file and link
 *      -lvisa64; this header is ABI-compatible with the real one.
 *    - for hardware-free development/CI, link the bundled MockVisa (built as
 *      libvisa) which implements exactly these symbols.
 *
 *  ABI note: ViStatus is signed 32-bit; ViSession/ViObject are unsigned
 *  32-bit even on 64-bit platforms; ViAttrState is register-wide (64-bit on
 *  LP64/LLP64). These match the official visatype.h.
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef __VISA_H__
#define __VISA_H__

#include <stdint.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

typedef int32_t   ViStatus;
typedef uint32_t  ViSession;
typedef uint32_t  ViObject;
typedef uint32_t  ViFindList;
typedef uint32_t  ViUInt32;
typedef int32_t   ViInt32;
typedef uint16_t  ViUInt16;
typedef uint32_t  ViAttr;
typedef uint64_t  ViAttrState;
typedef char      ViChar;
typedef unsigned char ViByte;
typedef ViChar*   ViPChar;
typedef ViByte*   ViBuf;
typedef const ViByte* ViConstBuf;
typedef ViUInt32  ViAccessMode;
typedef ViChar    ViRsrc[256];
typedef ViUInt32* ViPUInt32;
typedef ViStatus* ViPStatus;
typedef ViSession* ViPSession;

#define VI_NULL   ((ViObject)0)
#define VI_TRUE   ((ViUInt32)1)
#define VI_FALSE  ((ViUInt32)0)

#define VI_SUCCESS            ((ViStatus)0x00000000L)
#define VI_SUCCESS_TERM_CHAR  ((ViStatus)0x3FFF0005L)
#define VI_SUCCESS_MAX_CNT    ((ViStatus)0x3FFF0006L)

#define VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT   ((ViStatus)0xBFFF000EL)
#define VI_ERROR_RSRC_NFOUND  ((ViStatus)0xBFFF0011L)
#define VI_ERROR_INV_RSRC_NAME ((ViStatus)0xBFFF0012L)
#define VI_ERROR_TMO          ((ViStatus)0xBFFF0015L)
#define VI_ERROR_CONN_LOST    ((ViStatus)0xBFFF00A6L)
#define VI_ERROR_NSUP_ATTR    ((ViStatus)0xBFFF001DL)

#define VI_ATTR_TMO_VALUE       ((ViAttr)0x3FFF001AUL)
#define VI_ATTR_TERMCHAR        ((ViAttr)0x3FFF0018UL)
#define VI_ATTR_TERMCHAR_EN     ((ViAttr)0x3FFF0038UL)
#define VI_ATTR_SEND_END_EN     ((ViAttr)0x3FFF0016UL)
#define VI_ATTR_ASRL_BAUD       ((ViAttr)0x3FFF0021UL)
#define VI_ATTR_ASRL_DATA_BITS  ((ViAttr)0x3FFF0022UL)
#define VI_ATTR_ASRL_PARITY     ((ViAttr)0x3FFF0023UL)
#define VI_ATTR_ASRL_STOP_BITS  ((ViAttr)0x3FFF0024UL)

#define VI_ASRL_PAR_NONE  ((ViUInt16)0)
#define VI_ASRL_STOP_ONE  ((ViUInt16)10)

#define VI_TMO_INFINITE   ((ViUInt32)0xFFFFFFFFUL)

/* On x86-64 there is a single calling convention, so _VI_FUNC is empty. */
#ifndef _VI_FUNC
#  define _VI_FUNC
#endif

ViStatus _VI_FUNC viOpenDefaultRM(ViPSession vi);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viOpen(ViSession sesn, const ViChar* name, ViAccessMode mode,
                         ViUInt32 timeout, ViPSession vi);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viClose(ViObject vi);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viWrite(ViSession vi, ViConstBuf buf, ViUInt32 cnt, ViPUInt32 retCnt);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viRead(ViSession vi, ViBuf buf, ViUInt32 cnt, ViPUInt32 retCnt);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viSetAttribute(ViObject vi, ViAttr attrName, ViAttrState attrValue);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viGetAttribute(ViObject vi, ViAttr attrName, void* attrValue);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viFindRsrc(ViSession sesn, const ViChar* expr, ViFindList* findList,
                             ViPUInt32 retcnt, ViChar desc[]);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viFindNext(ViFindList findList, ViChar desc[]);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viStatusDesc(ViObject vi, ViStatus status, ViChar desc[]);
ViStatus _VI_FUNC viClear(ViSession vi);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif

#endif /* __VISA_H__ */

-- Common/S_PlzLimits.h

/*=============================================================================
 *  S_PlzLimits.h - INTERNAL per-model limits of the PLZ-5WH2 family.
 *
 *  *** THIS HEADER MUST NEVER BE SHIPPED TO END USERS. ***
 *
 *  One row per model, taken from the PLZ-5WH2 User's Manual specifications
 *  chapter (IB034623, pp. 145-159).
 *
 *  This header provides the S_PlzLimits struct + the family-wide PlzShared
 *  constants (used by every model plugin), plus a model CATALOG. The real
 *  model plugins are self-contained: each EMBEDS its own row in its
 *  constructor and does not read the catalog. The catalog exists for SimLoad,
 *  which simulates any family member and looks its limits up by name - so
 *  GetParameterRange reports identical limits for the simulated and the real
 *  instrument.
 *
 *  Adding a family member = a new self-contained model plugin (own functions +
 *  embedded row); optionally a catalog row here so SimLoad knows it too - see
 *  docs/Adding_A_New_Model.md.
 *===========================================================================*/
#ifndef S_PLZLIMITS_H
#define S_PLZLIMITS_H

#include <cstring>

struct S_PlzLimits {
    const char* m_szModelName;

    /* ratings                                                              */
    double m_dPowerRating;        /* W                                      */
    double m_dCurrentRating;      /* A                                      */
    double m_dVoltageRating;      /* V                                      */

    /* setpoint ranges (0..max) and resolutions                             */
    double m_dCurrentMax;         /* CC: 101 % of current rating [A]        */
    double m_dCurrentRes;
    double m_dVoltageMax;         /* CV: 101 % of voltage rating [V]        */
    double m_dVoltageRes;
    double m_dCondHighMax;        /* CR H range: 101 % [S]                  */
    double m_dCondHighRes;
    double m_dCondLowMax;         /* CR L range: 101 % [S]                  */
    double m_dCondLowRes;
    double m_dPowerMax;           /* CP: 101 % of power rating [W]          */
    double m_dPowerRes;

    /* slew rate (CC) [A/us]                                                */
    double m_dSlewMin;
    double m_dSlewMax;
    double m_dSlewRes;
    double m_dStepSlewRes;        /* coarser resolution of PROG step/LAST   */

    /* protections                                                          */
    double m_dOcpMax;             /* 110 % of current rating [A]            */
    double m_dOcpRes;
    double m_dOppMax;             /* 110 % of power rating [W]              */
    double m_dOppRes;
    double m_dUvpMax;             /* [V]                                    */
    double m_dUvpRes;

    /* cutoff integrators                                                   */
    double m_dCutoffCapacityMax;  /* [Ah]                                   */
    double m_dCutoffEnergyMax;    /* [Wh]                                   */
};

/*-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Limits shared by every PLZ-5WH2 family member.
 *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
namespace PlzShared {

const double CUTOFF_VOLTAGE_MAX   = 1000.0;    /* V, res 0.02               */
const double CUTOFF_VOLTAGE_RES   = 0.02;
const double CUTOFF_TIME_MAX      = 3600000.0; /* s, res 1                  */
const double CUTOFF_TIME_RES      = 1.0;
const double WATCHDOG_DELAY_MIN   = 1.0;       /* s                         */
const double WATCHDOG_DELAY_MAX   = 3600.0;
const double TIMED_INPUT_MIN      = 1.0;       /* s                         */
const double TIMED_INPUT_MAX      = 3600000.0;
const double APERTURE_MIN         = 0.00001;   /* s                         */
const double APERTURE_MAX         = 1.0;
const double PULSE_FREQ_MIN       = 1.0;       /* Hz                        */
const double PULSE_FREQ_MAX       = 10000.0;
const double PULSE_DUTY_MIN       = 5.0;       /* %                         */
const double PULSE_DUTY_MAX       = 95.0;
const double SINE_FREQ_MIN        = 1.0;       /* Hz (continuous band)      */
const double SINE_FREQ_MAX        = 1000.0;
const double ACQ_COUNT_MIN        = 1.0;
const double ACQ_COUNT_MAX        = 65536.0;
const double ACQ_DELAY_MIN        = 0.0;       /* s                         */
const double ACQ_DELAY_MAX        = 100.0;
const double ACQ_INTERVAL_MIN     = 0.00001;   /* s                         */
const double ACQ_INTERVAL_MAX     = 3600.0;
const double TRAN_DELAY_MIN       = 0.0;       /* s                         */
const double TRAN_DELAY_MAX       = 100.0;
const double PROGRAM_LOOP_MIN     = 1.0;
const double PROGRAM_LOOP_MAX     = 100000.0;
const double PROGRAM_STEPS_MIN    = 1.0;
const double PROGRAM_STEPS_MAX    = 10000.0;
const double STEP_DWELL_MIN       = 0.00005;   /* s                         */
const double STEP_DWELL_MAX       = 3600000.0;
const double SCREEN_SAVER_MIN     = 60.0;      /* s                         */
const double SCREEN_SAVER_MAX     = 59940.0;
const int    PROGRAM_COUNT_MAX    = 30;
const int    ARB_POINTS_MIN       = 3;
const int    ARB_POINTS_MAX       = 100;
const int    SETUP_MEMORY_MAX     = 19;        /* setup memories 0..19      */

/* discrete value sets (terminated by count)                                */
const double SOFT_START_VALUES[]   = { 0.0, 0.0005, 0.001, 0.002, 0.005,
                                       0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1 };
const int    SOFT_START_COUNT      = 9;
const double ARB_RESPONSE_VALUES[] = { 0.0, 0.0005, 0.001, 0.002, 0.005,
                                       0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1 };
const int    ARB_RESPONSE_COUNT    = 9;
const double SINE_FREQ_EXTRA[]     = { 2000.0, 5000.0, 10000.0 };
const int    SINE_FREQ_EXTRA_COUNT = 3;
const double BUFFER_SIZE_VALUES[]  = { 1024.0, 2048.0, 4096.0, 8192.0,
                                       16384.0, 32768.0, 65536.0 };
const int    BUFFER_SIZE_COUNT     = 7;

} /* namespace PlzShared */

/*-----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * The model catalog. Row order: name, ratings (W/A/V), CC max/res,
 * CV max/res, CR-H max/res, CR-L max/res, CP max/res, slew min/max/res/step,
 * OCP max/res, OPP max/res, UVP max/res, cutoff Ah / Wh.
 *---------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
inline const S_PlzLimits* PlzLimitsCatalog(int* out_piCount)
{
    static const S_PlzLimits s_asCatalog[] = {
        { "PLZ1005WH2",  1000.0,  20.0, 1000.0,
          20.2,   0.0005, 1010.0, 0.02,
          0.505,  0.00001,  0.00505, 0.0000001,
          1010.0, 0.02,
          0.001,  1.0,  0.00002, 0.0001,
          22.0,   0.001, 1100.0,  0.1, 1000.0, 0.02,
          70000.0,   40000000.0 },
        { "PLZ2005WH2",  2000.0,  40.0, 1000.0,
          40.4,   0.001,  1010.0, 0.02,
          1.01,   0.00002,  0.0101,  0.0000002,
          2020.0, 0.05,
          0.002,  2.0,  0.00005, 0.0002,
          44.0,   0.01,  2200.0,  0.1, 1000.0, 0.02,
          140000.0,  80000000.0 },
        { "PLZ4005WH2",  4000.0,  80.0, 1000.0,
          80.8,   0.002,  1010.0, 0.02,
          2.02,   0.00005,  0.0202,  0.0000005,
          4040.0, 0.1,
          0.004,  4.0,  0.0001,  0.0004,
          88.0,   0.01,  4400.0,  1.0, 1000.0, 0.02,
          280000.0,  160000000.0 },
        { "PLZ12005WH2", 12000.0, 240.0, 1000.0,
          242.4,  0.005,  1010.0, 0.02,
          6.06,   0.0002,   0.0606,  0.000002,
          12120.0, 0.5,
          0.01,   12.0, 0.0002,  0.001,
          264.0,  0.01,  13200.0, 1.0, 1000.0, 0.02,
          800000.0,  500000000.0 },
        { "PLZ20005WH2", 20000.0, 400.0, 1000.0,
          404.0,  0.01,   1010.0, 0.02,
          10.1,   0.0002,   0.101,   0.000002,
          20200.0, 0.5,
          0.02,   20.0, 0.0005,  0.002,
          440.0,  0.1,   22000.0, 1.0, 1000.0, 0.02,
          1400000.0, 800000000.0 },
    };
    if (out_piCount != NULL) {
        *out_piCount = static_cast<int>(sizeof(s_asCatalog) / sizeof(s_asCatalog[0]));
    }
    return s_asCatalog;
}

inline const S_PlzLimits* PlzFindLimits(const char* in_szModelName)
{
    int iCount = 0;
    const S_PlzLimits* psCatalog = PlzLimitsCatalog(&iCount);
    for (int i = 0; i < iCount; ++i) {
        if (std::strcmp(psCatalog[i].m_szModelName, in_szModelName) == 0) {
            return &psCatalog[i];
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

#endif /* S_PLZLIMITS_H */

-- PluginPLZ4005WH2/PLZ4005WH2Plugin.h

/*============================================================================
 *  PLZ4005WH2Plugin.h
 *
 *  Kikusui PLZ4005WH2 electronic-load plugin - a complete, self-contained
 *  CIELoadPlugin implementation (SCPI over VISA, direct linkage). One VISA
 *  session per load is held in a map keyed by load number; the model's
 *  limits are embedded in this plugin (m_limits).
 *
 *  The PLZ-5WH2 family has fixed hardware OVP, so setOVP/readOVP are left
 *  as the interface's NOT_SUPPORTED defaults (not overridden here).
 *
 *  \author  ELoad framework
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef CPLZ4005WH2_H
#define CPLZ4005WH2_H

#include <QObject>
#include <QMap>

#include "visa.h"
#include "IELoadPlugin.h"
#include "S_PlzLimits.h"

class CPLZ4005WH2Plugin : public QObject, public CIELoadPlugin
{
    Q_OBJECT
    Q_PLUGIN_METADATA(IID ELoadPlugin_iid)
    Q_INTERFACES(CIELoadPlugin)
public:
    CPLZ4005WH2Plugin();
    ~CPLZ4005WH2Plugin() override;

    /*==== mandatory ======================================================*/
    S_ELoad_PluginInfo        getPluginInfo() const override;
    S_ELoad_Capabilities getCapabilities() const override;
    ELoadError connect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig& in_sConfig) override;
    ELoadError disconnect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) override;
    bool       isConnected(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const override;
    ELoadError reset(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) override;
    ELoadError setTimeout(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32TimeoutMs) override;
    ELoadError clearStatus(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) override;
    ELoadError getIdentification(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strIdn) override;
    ELoadError selfTest(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, S32BIT& out_iResult) override;
    ELoadError getOptions(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strOptions) override;
    ELoadError waitOperationComplete(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) override;
    ELoadError getScpiVersion(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, QString& out_strVersion) override;
    ELoadError getParameterRange(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, U32BIT in_u32Channel, Enum_ELoad_ParamId in_eParam, S_ELoad_ParameterRange& out_sRange) override;

    /*==== mode ===========================================================*/
    ELoadError setMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_Mode v) override;
    ELoadError getMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_Mode& o) override;
    ELoadError setCVOption(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, bool v) override;
    ELoadError getCVOption(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, bool& o) override;
    ELoadError getOperatingMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode& o) override;

    /*==== setpoints ======================================================*/
    ELoadError setCurrent(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;

    ELoadError readbackSetConductance(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setResistance(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError readbackSetResistance(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setPower(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError readbackSetPower(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setCRRange(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_CRRange v) override;
    ELoadError getCRRange(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_CRRange& o) override;
    ELoadError setCVResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed v) override;
    ELoadError getCVResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed& o) override;
    ELoadError setCRResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed v) override;
    ELoadError getCRResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed& o) override;
    ELoadError setSlewRate(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getSlewRate(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setSoftStart(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getSoftStart(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;

    ELoadError resetIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;
    ELoadError startIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;
    ELoadError stopIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;

    /*==== pulse / sine ===================================================*/
    ELoadError setPulseCurrentLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getPulseCurrentLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setPulseConductanceLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getPulseConductanceLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setPulseFrequency(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getPulseFrequency(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setPulseDuty(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getPulseDuty(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError startPulse(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;
    ELoadError stopPulse(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;
    ELoadError setSineAmplitude(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getSineAmplitude(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setSineFrequency(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getSineFrequency(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError startSine(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;
    ELoadError stopSine(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;

    /*==== ARB ============================================================*/
    ELoadError setArbCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError getArbCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError setArbPoint(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, U32BIT i, FDOUBLE v, FDOUBLE a) override;
    ELoadError getArbPoint(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, U32BIT i, FDOUBLE& v, FDOUBLE& a) override;
    ELoadError setArbTable(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, const QVector<FDOUBLE>& v, const QVector<FDOUBLE>& a) override;
    ELoadError getArbTable(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, QVector<FDOUBLE>& v, QVector<FDOUBLE>& a) override;
    ELoadError applyArb(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;
    ELoadError clearArb(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;
    ELoadError setArbResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getArbResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o) override;

    /*==== sequence =======================================================*/
    ELoadError createProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n) override;
    ELoadError selectProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n) override;
    ELoadError getSelectedProgram(U32BIT l, QString& o) override;
    ELoadError deleteProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n) override;
    ELoadError renameProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n) override;
    ELoadError saveProgram(U32BIT l) override;
    ELoadError listPrograms(U32BIT l, QStringList& o) override;
    ELoadError setProgramLoop(U32BIT l, S32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError getProgramLoop(U32BIT l, S32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError setProgramStepCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError getProgramStepCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError setProgramProtection(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_Mode w, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError setStepLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getStepLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setStepDwell(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getStepDwell(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setStepInput(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, bool v) override;
    ELoadError getStepInput(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, bool& o) override;
    ELoadError setStepTransition(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, Enum_ELoad_Transition v) override;
    ELoadError getStepTransition(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, Enum_ELoad_Transition& o) override;
    ELoadError setStepTriggerOut(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError getStepTriggerOut(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError setStepTriggerWait(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError getStepTriggerWait(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError startSequence(U32BIT l) override;
    ELoadError abortSequence(U32BIT l) override;
    ELoadError suspendSequence(U32BIT l) override;
    ELoadError resumeSequence(U32BIT l) override;
    ELoadError getSequenceState(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_SeqState& s, U32BIT& st, U32BIT& lp) override;

    /*==== triggers =======================================================*/
    ELoadError setAcqTriggerSource(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_AcqTrigSource v) override;
    ELoadError getAcqTriggerSource(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_AcqTrigSource& o) override;
    ELoadError setAcqTriggerCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError getAcqTriggerCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError setAcqTriggerDelay(U32BIT l, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getAcqTriggerDelay(U32BIT l, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError setAcqIntervalEnable(U32BIT l, bool v) override;
    ELoadError getAcqIntervalEnable(U32BIT l, bool& o) override;
    ELoadError setAcqIntervalTime(U32BIT l, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getAcqIntervalTime(U32BIT l, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError initiateAcquire(U32BIT l) override;
    ELoadError setTranTriggerSource(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_TranTrigSource v) override;
    ELoadError getTranTriggerSource(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_TranTrigSource& o) override;
    ELoadError setTranTriggerDelay(U32BIT l, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getTranTriggerDelay(U32BIT l, FDOUBLE& o) override;
    ELoadError sendTrigger(U32BIT l) override;
    ELoadError abort(U32BIT l) override;
    ELoadError abortAcquire(U32BIT l) override;

    /*==== memories =======================================================*/
    ELoadError saveSettings(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError recallSettings(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError savePreset(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError recallPreset(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) override;

    /*==== status / system ================================================*/
    ELoadError readErrorStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus& o) override;
    ELoadError clearErrorStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT c) override;
    ELoadError queryErrorQueue(U32BIT l, QString& o) override;
    ELoadError getInstrumentErrorCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) override;

    ELoadError readStatusByte(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError readQuestionableStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError readOperationStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError setRemoteState(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_RemoteState v) override;
    ELoadError getRemoteState(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_RemoteState& o) override;
    ELoadError setKeyLock(U32BIT l, bool v) override;
    ELoadError isKeyLocked(U32BIT l, bool& o) override;
    ELoadError setKeyLockLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) override;
    ELoadError getKeyLockLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError setBeeper(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_Beeper w, bool v) override;
    ELoadError getBeeper(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_Beeper w, bool& o) override;
    ELoadError setRemoteSensing(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, bool v) override;
    ELoadError isRemoteSensingEnabled(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, bool& o) override;
    ELoadError setDigital2Direction(U32BIT l, bool v) override;
    ELoadError getDigital2Direction(U32BIT l, bool& o) override;
    ELoadError getMSyncIndex(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) override;
    ELoadError setScreenSaver(U32BIT l, bool v) override;
    ELoadError isScreenSaverEnabled(U32BIT l, bool& o) override;
    ELoadError setScreenSaverDelay(U32BIT l, FDOUBLE v) override;
    ELoadError getScreenSaverDelay(U32BIT l, FDOUBLE& o) override;

    /*==== debug ==========================================================*/
    ELoadError writeScpi(U32BIT l, const QString& v) override;
    ELoadError queryScpi(U32BIT l, const QString& v, QString& o) override;

private:
    struct S_DeviceInstance {
        ViSession m_vi;
        ViSession m_rm;
        S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig m_sConfig;
        bool      m_bConnected;
        U32BIT    m_u32Timeout;
        QString   m_strIdn;
        S_DeviceInstance() : m_vi(VI_NULL), m_rm(VI_NULL), m_bConnected(false), m_u32Timeout(3000) {}
    };

    // instance lookup
    S_DeviceInstance* dev(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber);

    // transport (direct VISA)
    ELoadError visaError(ViStatus in_status, const QString& in_strContext);
    ELoadError writeLine(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd);
    ELoadError readLine(U32BIT l, QByteArray& out_abyResp);
    ELoadError queryLine(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd, QByteArray& out_abyResp);
    ELoadError sendChecked(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd);   // write + SYST:ERR?

    // typed helpers
    ELoadError queryDouble(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd, FDOUBLE& out_d);
    ELoadError queryInt(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd, S32BIT& out_i);
    ELoadError queryBool(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd, bool& out_b);
    ELoadError setDouble(U32BIT l, const char* in_szScpi, FDOUBLE in_d, FDOUBLE in_dMin, FDOUBLE in_dMax, const char* in_szWhat);
    ELoadError setDiscrete(U32BIT l, const char* in_szScpi, FDOUBLE in_d, const double* in_pd, int in_iCount, const char* in_szWhat);
    ELoadError setBoolScpi(U32BIT l, const char* in_szScpi, bool in_b);
    ELoadError setIntScpi(U32BIT l, const char* in_szScpi, S32BIT in_i, S32BIT in_iMin, S32BIT in_iMax, const char* in_szWhat);
    ELoadError queryErrorRaw(U32BIT l, S32BIT& out_iCode, QByteArray& out_abyMsg);
    FDOUBLE    condRangeMax(U32BIT l);
    static QByteArray fmtD(FDOUBLE in_d);

    S_PlzLimits        m_limits;
    const S_PlzLimits* m_pLimits;
    QString            m_strModel;
    QMap<U32BIT, S_DeviceInstance> m_devices;
};

#endif // CPLZ4005WH2_H

-- PluginPLZ4005WH2/PLZ4005WH2Plugin.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  PLZ4005WH2Plugin.cpp - Kikusui PLZ4005WH2 electronic-load plugin implementation.
 *  SCPI over VISA (direct), one session per load, limits embedded.
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "PLZ4005WH2Plugin.h"
#include "VisaHelper.h"        // inline S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig::toVisaResourceString()

#include <QByteArray>
#include <QStringList>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Device short-form enum tokens, indexed by the API enum value.
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
namespace {
const char* const MODE_TOK[]     = { "CC", "CR", "CV", "CP", "ARB" };
const char* const CRRANGE_TOK[]  = { "LOW", "HIGH" };
const char* const RESP_TOK[]     = { "NORM", "FAST" };
const char* const ACT_TOK[]      = { "LIM", "TRIP" };
const char* const PON_TOK[]      = { "RST", "RCL0", "AUTO" };
const char* const ACQSRC_TOK[]   = { "IMM", "BUS", "DIGITAL2", "TAL", "MSYN", "LOADOFF" };
const char* const TRANSRC_TOK[]  = { "IMM", "BUS", "DIGITAL2", "MSYN" };
const char* const RLST_TOK[]     = { "LOC", "REM", "RWL" };
const char* const INTGATE_TOK[]  = { "NONE", "LOAD_ON", "PROG_RUN" };
const char* const TRANS_TOK[]    = { "IMM", "RAMP" };
const char* const DATAITEM_TOK[] = { "CURR", "VOLT", "POW", "ETIM", "CAP", "ENER" };

const char* extFconScpi(Enum_ELoad_Mode m)
{
    switch (m) {
    case Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC: return "CURR:EXT:FCON";
    case Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCR: return "COND:EXT:FCON";
    case Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCV: return "VOLT:EXT:FCON";
    case Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCP: return "POW:EXT:FCON";
    default: return nullptr;
    }
}
const char* cutoffScpi(Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t)
{
    switch (t) {
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumVoltage:  return "INP:CUTO:VOLT";
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumCapacity: return "INP:CUTO:CAP";
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumEnergy:   return "INP:CUTO:ENER";
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumTime:     return "INP:CUTO:ETIM";
    default: return nullptr;
    }
}
/* Decode a STAT:OPER:COND? value into the regulation mode (shared by
 * getOperatingMode and readErrorStatus so the register is queried once). */
Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode operToMode(S32BIT in_iOper)
{
    if (in_iOper & 0x0002) return Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantCurrent;
    if (in_iOper & 0x0004) return Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantResistance;
    if (in_iOper & 0x0001) return Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantVoltage;
    if (in_iOper & 0x0008) return Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantPower;
    return Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumUnknown;
}
} // namespace

/*============================================================================
 *  Construction / info / capabilities
 *==========================================================================*/
CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::CPLZ4005WH2Plugin()
    : m_limits{ "PLZ4005WH2",  4000.0,  80.0, 1000.0,
                80.8,   0.002,  1010.0, 0.02,
                2.02,   0.00005,  0.0202,  0.0000005,
                4040.0, 0.1,
                0.004,  4.0,  0.0001,  0.0004,
                88.0,   0.01,  4400.0,  1.0, 1000.0, 0.02,
                280000.0,  160000000.0 }
    , m_pLimits(&m_limits)
    , m_strModel(QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2"))
{
}

CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::~CPLZ4005WH2Plugin()
{
    for (auto it = m_devices.begin(); it != m_devices.end(); ++it) {
        if (it.value().m_vi != VI_NULL) viClose(it.value().m_vi);
        if (it.value().m_rm != VI_NULL) viClose(it.value().m_rm);
    }
    m_devices.clear();
}

S_ELoad_PluginInfo CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getPluginInfo() const
{
    S_ELoad_PluginInfo s;
    qstrncpy(s.m_szName, m_strModel.toLatin1().constData(), PLUGIN_INFO_NAME_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szVersion, "1.0.0", PLUGIN_INFO_VERSION_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szManufacturer, "Kikusui Electronics Corporation", PLUGIN_INFO_MANUFACTURER_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szModelName, m_strModel.toLatin1().constData(), PLUGIN_INFO_MODEL_NAME_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szSeries, "PLZ-5WH2", PLUGIN_INFO_SERIES_SIZE);
    const QString strDesc = QStringLiteral("Kikusui %1 electronic load, %2 W / %3 A / %4 V")
            .arg(m_strModel).arg(m_pLimits->m_dPowerRating)
            .arg(m_pLimits->m_dCurrentRating).arg(m_pLimits->m_dVoltageRating);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szDescription, strDesc.toLatin1().constData(), PLUGIN_INFO_DESCRIPTION_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szMinCoreVersion, "1.0.0", PLUGIN_INFO_MIN_CORE_VERSION);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szMaxCoreVersion, "2.0.0", PLUGIN_INFO_MAX_CORE_VERSION);
    s.m_StrlstSupportedProtocols << "USB" << "LAN" << "RS232C";
    s.m_StrlstSupportedModes << "CC" << "CR" << "CV" << "CP" << "ARB";
    return s;
}

S_ELoad_Capabilities CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getCapabilities() const
{
    S_ELoad_Capabilities caps;
    caps.m_u32NumberOfChannels = 1;
    caps.m_dMaxVoltage = m_pLimits->m_dVoltageRating;
    caps.m_dMaxCurrent = m_pLimits->m_dCurrentRating;
    caps.m_dMaxPower   = m_pLimits->m_dPowerRating;
    caps.m_bHasOCP = true;
    caps.m_bHasOPP = true;
    caps.m_bHasUVP = true;
    caps.m_bHasOVP = false;               // fixed hardware OVP
    caps.m_bSupportsParallel = true;
    caps.m_u32MaxParallelUnits = 5;

    S_ELoad_ChannelCapabilities ch;
    ch.m_u32ChannelNumber = 1;
    ch.m_dMaxVoltage = m_pLimits->m_dVoltageRating;
    ch.m_dMaxCurrent = m_pLimits->m_dCurrentRating;
    ch.m_dMaxPower   = m_pLimits->m_dPowerRating;
    ch.m_dMaxConductance = m_pLimits->m_dCondHighMax;
    ch.m_bHasOCP = ch.m_bHasOPP = ch.m_bHasUVP = true;
    ch.m_bHasOVP = false;
    ch.m_bHasARB = ch.m_bHasSequence = ch.m_bHasPulseSine = true;
    caps.m_QlistChannels.append(ch);
    return caps;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Instance / transport
 *==========================================================================*/
CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::S_DeviceInstance* CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::dev(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber)
{
    auto it = m_devices.find(in_u32LoadNumber);
    return (it == m_devices.end()) ? nullptr : &it.value();
}

QByteArray CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::fmtD(FDOUBLE in_d)
{
    char sz[40];
    std::snprintf(sz, sizeof(sz), "%.6G", in_d);
    return QByteArray(sz);
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::visaError(ViStatus in_status, const QString& in_strContext)
{
    if (in_status == VI_ERROR_TMO) {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::COMMUNICATION_TIMEOUT,
                          QStringLiteral("%1: VISA timeout").arg(in_strContext));
    }
    return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::COMMUNICATION_ERROR,
                      QStringLiteral("%1: VISA status 0x%2")
                      .arg(in_strContext).arg(static_cast<quint32>(in_status), 8, 16, QLatin1Char('0')));
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::writeLine(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd)
{
    S_DeviceInstance* d = dev(l);
    if (d == nullptr || !d->m_bConnected) {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED);
    }
    QByteArray aby = in_abyCmd;
    aby.append('\n');
    ViUInt32 written = 0;
    const ViStatus st = viWrite(d->m_vi, reinterpret_cast<ViConstBuf>(aby.constData()),
                                static_cast<ViUInt32>(aby.size()), &written);
    if (st < VI_SUCCESS) return visaError(st, QStringLiteral("viWrite"));
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::readLine(U32BIT l, QByteArray& out_abyResp)
{
    out_abyResp.clear();
    S_DeviceInstance* d = dev(l);
    if (d == nullptr || !d->m_bConnected) {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED);
    }
    char chunk[4096];
    for (;;) {
        ViUInt32 got = 0;
        const ViStatus st = viRead(d->m_vi, reinterpret_cast<ViBuf>(chunk),
                                   static_cast<ViUInt32>(sizeof(chunk)), &got);
        if (st < VI_SUCCESS) return visaError(st, QStringLiteral("viRead"));
        out_abyResp.append(chunk, static_cast<int>(got));
        if (st != VI_SUCCESS_MAX_CNT) break;
        if (out_abyResp.size() > 8 * 1024 * 1024) break;
    }
    while (out_abyResp.endsWith('\n') || out_abyResp.endsWith('\r')) out_abyResp.chop(1);
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::queryLine(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd, QByteArray& out_abyResp)
{
    ELoadError e = writeLine(l, in_abyCmd);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    return readLine(l, out_abyResp);
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::queryErrorRaw(U32BIT l, S32BIT& out_iCode, QByteArray& out_abyMsg)
{
    QByteArray resp;
    ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("SYST:ERR?"), resp);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    const int iComma = resp.indexOf(',');
    if (iComma < 0) { out_iCode = 0; out_abyMsg = resp; return ELoadError(); }
    out_iCode = resp.left(iComma).trimmed().toInt();
    QByteArray m = resp.mid(iComma + 1).trimmed();
    if (m.startsWith('"') && m.endsWith('"') && m.size() >= 2) m = m.mid(1, m.size() - 2);
    out_abyMsg = m;
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::sendChecked(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd)
{
    ELoadError e = writeLine(l, in_abyCmd);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    S32BIT code = 0; QByteArray msg;
    e = queryErrorRaw(l, code, msg);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    if (code != 0) {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INSTRUMENT_ERROR,
                          QStringLiteral("instrument error %1: %2").arg(code).arg(QString::fromLatin1(msg)));
    }
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::queryDouble(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd, FDOUBLE& out_d)
{
    QByteArray resp;
    ELoadError e = queryLine(l, in_abyCmd, resp);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    bool ok = false;
    const double v = resp.trimmed().toDouble(&ok);
    if (!ok) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_RESPONSE,
                               QStringLiteral("bad numeric response \"%1\"").arg(QString::fromLatin1(resp)));
    out_d = v;
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::queryInt(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd, S32BIT& out_i)
{
    FDOUBLE d = 0.0;
    ELoadError e = queryDouble(l, in_abyCmd, d);
    if (e.isSuccess()) out_i = static_cast<S32BIT>(std::llround(d));
    return e;
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::queryBool(U32BIT l, const QByteArray& in_abyCmd, bool& out_b)
{
    S32BIT i = 0;
    ELoadError e = queryInt(l, in_abyCmd, i);
    if (e.isSuccess()) out_b = (i != 0);
    return e;
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setDouble(U32BIT l, const char* in_szScpi, FDOUBLE in_d,
                                     FDOUBLE in_dMin, FDOUBLE in_dMax, const char* in_szWhat)
{
    if (in_d < in_dMin || in_d > in_dMax) {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE,
                          QStringLiteral("%1 %2 out of range [%3 .. %4]")
                          .arg(QLatin1String(in_szWhat)).arg(in_d).arg(in_dMin).arg(in_dMax));
    }
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArray(in_szScpi) + " " + fmtD(in_d));
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setDiscrete(U32BIT l, const char* in_szScpi, FDOUBLE in_d,
                                       const double* in_pd, int in_iCount, const char* in_szWhat)
{
    bool found = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < in_iCount; ++i) if (std::fabs(in_d - in_pd[i]) < 1e-12) { found = true; break; }
    if (!found) {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE,
                          QStringLiteral("%1 %2 is not a valid discrete value").arg(QLatin1String(in_szWhat)).arg(in_d));
    }
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArray(in_szScpi) + " " + fmtD(in_d));
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setBoolScpi(U32BIT l, const char* in_szScpi, bool in_b)
{
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArray(in_szScpi) + (in_b ? " ON" : " OFF"));
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setIntScpi(U32BIT l, const char* in_szScpi, S32BIT in_i,
                                      S32BIT in_iMin, S32BIT in_iMax, const char* in_szWhat)
{
    if (in_i < in_iMin || in_i > in_iMax) {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE,
                          QStringLiteral("%1 %2 out of range [%3 .. %4]")
                          .arg(QLatin1String(in_szWhat)).arg(in_i).arg(in_iMin).arg(in_iMax));
    }
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArray(in_szScpi) + " " + QByteArray::number(in_i));
}

FDOUBLE CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::condRangeMax(U32BIT l)
{
    Enum_ELoad_CRRange r = Enum_ELoad_CRRange::m_enumHigh;
    getCRRange(l, 1, r);
    return (r == Enum_ELoad_CRRange::m_enumLow) ? m_pLimits->m_dCondLowMax : m_pLimits->m_dCondHighMax;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Connection
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::connect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber, const S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig& in_sConfig)
{
    if (dev(in_u32LoadNumber) != nullptr && m_devices[in_u32LoadNumber].m_bConnected) {
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::ALREADY_CONNECTED);
    }
    S_DeviceInstance d;
    d.m_sConfig = in_sConfig;
    d.m_u32Timeout = in_sConfig.m_u32Timeout ? in_sConfig.m_u32Timeout : 3000;

    ViStatus st = viOpenDefaultRM(&d.m_rm);
    if (st < VI_SUCCESS) return visaError(st, QStringLiteral("viOpenDefaultRM"));

    const QByteArray abyRes = in_sConfig.toVisaResourceString().toLatin1();
    st = viOpen(d.m_rm, abyRes.constData(), 0, 0, &d.m_vi);
    if (st < VI_SUCCESS) {
        viClose(d.m_rm);
        return visaError(st, QStringLiteral("viOpen"));
    }
    viSetAttribute(d.m_vi, VI_ATTR_TMO_VALUE, d.m_u32Timeout);
    viSetAttribute(d.m_vi, VI_ATTR_TERMCHAR, static_cast<ViAttrState>('\n'));
    viSetAttribute(d.m_vi, VI_ATTR_TERMCHAR_EN, VI_TRUE);
    viSetAttribute(d.m_vi, VI_ATTR_SEND_END_EN, VI_TRUE);
    d.m_bConnected = true;
    m_devices[in_u32LoadNumber] = d;

    // verify *IDN? names the expected model
    QByteArray idn;
    ELoadError e = queryLine(in_u32LoadNumber, QByteArrayLiteral("*IDN?"), idn);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) { disconnect(in_u32LoadNumber); return e; }
    if (!QString::fromLatin1(idn).contains(m_strModel, Qt::CaseInsensitive)) {
        const QString strIdn = QString::fromLatin1(idn).trimmed();
        disconnect(in_u32LoadNumber);
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::CONNECTION_FAILED,
                          QStringLiteral("connected instrument \"%1\" is not a %2").arg(strIdn, m_strModel));
    }
    m_devices[in_u32LoadNumber].m_strIdn = QString::fromLatin1(idn).trimmed();
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::disconnect(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber)
{
    S_DeviceInstance* d = dev(in_u32LoadNumber);
    if (d == nullptr) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED);
    if (d->m_vi != VI_NULL) viClose(d->m_vi);
    if (d->m_rm != VI_NULL) viClose(d->m_rm);
    m_devices.remove(in_u32LoadNumber);
    return ELoadError();
}

bool CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::isConnected(U32BIT in_u32LoadNumber) const
{
    auto it = m_devices.find(in_u32LoadNumber);
    return it != m_devices.end() && it.value().m_bConnected && it.value().m_vi != VI_NULL;
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::reset(U32BIT l)                 { return writeLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*RST")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::clearStatus(U32BIT l)           { return writeLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*CLS")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setTimeout(U32BIT l, U32BIT ms)
{
    S_DeviceInstance* d = dev(l);
    if (d == nullptr) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED);
    d->m_u32Timeout = ms;
    if (d->m_vi != VI_NULL) viSetAttribute(d->m_vi, VI_ATTR_TMO_VALUE, ms);
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getIdentification(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{
    S_DeviceInstance* d = dev(l);
    if (d != nullptr && !d->m_strIdn.isEmpty()) { o = d->m_strIdn; return ELoadError(); }
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*IDN?"), r);
    if (e.isSuccess()) o = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed();
    return e;
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::selfTest(U32BIT l, S32BIT& o) { return queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*TST?"), o); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getOptions(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*OPT?"), r); if (e.isSuccess()) o = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed(); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::waitOperationComplete(U32BIT l) { S32BIT v = 0; return queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*OPC?"), v); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getScpiVersion(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("SYST:VERS?"), r); if (e.isSuccess()) o = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed(); return e; }

/*============================================================================
 *  Mode
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_Mode v)
{ return sendChecked(l, QByteArray("FUNC ") + MODE_TOK[static_cast<int>(v)]); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_Mode& o)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("FUNC?"), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    const QString s = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed().toUpper();
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) if (s.startsWith(QLatin1String(MODE_TOK[i]))) { o = static_cast<Enum_ELoad_Mode>(i); return ELoadError(); }
    return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_RESPONSE);
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setCVOption(U32BIT l, U32BIT, bool v) { return setBoolScpi(l, "FUNC:CVOP", v); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getCVOption(U32BIT l, U32BIT, bool& o) { return queryBool(l, QByteArrayLiteral("FUNC:CVOP?"), o); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getOperatingMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode& o)
{
    S32BIT oper = 0;
    ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("STAT:OPER:COND?"), oper);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    o = operToMode(oper);
    return ELoadError();
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Cut-off
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setCutoff(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t, FDOUBLE v)
{
    const char* s = cutoffScpi(t); if (!s) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER);
    FDOUBLE dMax = 0.0;
    switch (t) {
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumVoltage:  dMax = PlzShared::CUTOFF_VOLTAGE_MAX; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumCapacity: dMax = m_pLimits->m_dCutoffCapacityMax; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumEnergy:   dMax = m_pLimits->m_dCutoffEnergyMax; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumTime:     dMax = PlzShared::CUTOFF_TIME_MAX; break;
    }
    return setDouble(l, s, v, 0.0, dMax, "cutoff");
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getCutoff(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t, FDOUBLE& o)
{ const char* s = cutoffScpi(t); if (!s) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER); return queryDouble(l, QByteArray(s) + "?", o); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::enableCutoff(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t, bool v)
{ const char* s = cutoffScpi(t); if (!s) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER); return setBoolScpi(l, (QByteArray(s) + ":STAT").constData(), v); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::isCutoffEnabled(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t, bool& o)
{ const char* s = cutoffScpi(t); if (!s) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER); return queryBool(l, QByteArray(s) + ":STAT?", o); }

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::measureData(U32BIT l, U32BIT, QVector<FDOUBLE>& o)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("MEAS?"), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    o.clear();
    const QList<QByteArray> parts = r.split(',');
    for (const QByteArray& p : parts) o.append(p.trimmed().toDouble());
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setSenseAperture(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v) { return setDouble(l, "SENS:APER", v, PlzShared::APERTURE_MIN, PlzShared::APERTURE_MAX, "sense aperture"); }

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setDisplayItem(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_DataItem i, bool v)
{
    const char* s = nullptr;
    switch (i) { case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumCapacity: s = "DISP:CAP"; break;
                 case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumEnergy:   s = "DISP:ENER"; break;
                 case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumETime:    s = "DISP:ETIM"; break;
                 default: return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER); }
    return setBoolScpi(l, s, v);
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getDisplayItem(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_DataItem i, bool& o)
{
    const char* s = nullptr;
    switch (i) { case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumCapacity: s = "DISP:CAP?"; break;
                 case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumEnergy:   s = "DISP:ENER?"; break;
                 case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumETime:    s = "DISP:ETIM?"; break;
                 default: return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER); }
    return queryBool(l, s, o);
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Data logger / integration
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setBufferSize(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT v)
{
    bool ok = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::BUFFER_SIZE_COUNT; ++i) if (v == static_cast<U32BIT>(PlzShared::BUFFER_SIZE_VALUES[i])) { ok = true; break; }
    if (!ok) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("buffer size %1 is not a power of two in range").arg(v));
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:BSIZ ") + QByteArray::number(v));
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getBufferSize(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o)
{ S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:BSIZ?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setDataFormat(U32BIT l, U32BIT, const QList<Enum_ELoad_DataItem>& v)
{
    if (v.isEmpty() || v.size() > 6) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("data format needs 1..6 items"));
    QByteArray cmd = QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:FORM ");
    for (int i = 0; i < v.size(); ++i) { if (i) cmd += ','; cmd += DATAITEM_TOK[static_cast<int>(v.at(i))]; }
    return sendChecked(l, cmd);
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getDataFormat(U32BIT l, U32BIT, QList<Enum_ELoad_DataItem>& o)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:FORM?"), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    o.clear();
    const QList<QByteArray> parts = r.split(',');
    for (const QByteArray& p : parts) {
        const QString s = QString::fromLatin1(p.trimmed()).toUpper();
        for (int j = 0; j < 6; ++j) if (s.startsWith(QLatin1String(DATAITEM_TOK[j]))) { o.append(static_cast<Enum_ELoad_DataItem>(j)); break; }
    }
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getDataPointCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o)
{ S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:POIN?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::readDataBuffer(U32BIT l, U32BIT, QVector<FDOUBLE>& o, U32BIT m)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:R?"), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    o.clear();
    if (r.trimmed().isEmpty()) return ELoadError();
    const QList<QByteArray> parts = r.split(',');
    for (int i = 0; i < parts.size() && static_cast<U32BIT>(o.size()) < m; ++i) o.append(parts.at(i).trimmed().toDouble());
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setIntegrationGate(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_IntegGate v) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArray("DATA:INT:GATE ") + INTGATE_TOK[static_cast<int>(v)]); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getIntegrationGate(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_IntegGate& o)
{ QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:INT:GATE?"), r); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
  const QString s = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed().toUpper();
  o = s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("NONE")) ? Enum_ELoad_IntegGate::m_enumNone : (s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("PROG")) ? Enum_ELoad_IntegGate::m_enumProgRun : Enum_ELoad_IntegGate::m_enumLoadOn); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setIntegrationAutoReset(U32BIT l, U32BIT, bool v) { return setBoolScpi(l, "DATA:INT:GATE:ARES", v); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getIntegrationAutoReset(U32BIT l, U32BIT, bool& o) { return queryBool(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:INT:GATE:ARES?"), o); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::resetIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:INT:RES")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::startIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:INT:STAR")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::stopIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("DATA:INT:STOP")); }

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setSineAmplitude(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v) { return setDouble(l, "CURR:SINE:AMPL", v, 0.0, m_pLimits->m_dCurrentMax, "sine amplitude"); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getSineAmplitude(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o) { return queryDouble(l, QByteArrayLiteral("CURR:SINE:AMPL?"), o); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setSineFrequency(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{
    const bool band = (v >= PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_MIN && v <= PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_MAX);
    bool extra = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_EXTRA_COUNT; ++i) if (std::fabs(v - PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_EXTRA[i]) < 1e-6) { extra = true; break; }
    if (!band && !extra) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("sine frequency %1 Hz not in 1..1000 or {2k,5k,10k}").arg(v));
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("CURR:SINE:FREQ ") + fmtD(v));
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getSineFrequency(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o) { return queryDouble(l, QByteArrayLiteral("CURR:SINE:FREQ?"), o); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::startSine(U32BIT l, U32BIT) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("INIT:SINE")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::stopSine(U32BIT l, U32BIT) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ABOR:SINE")); }

/*============================================================================
 *  ARB (binary block encode; MAP point access)
 *==========================================================================*/
namespace {
void appendLE(QByteArray& a, qint32 v) {
    a.append(static_cast<char>(v & 0xFF)); a.append(static_cast<char>((v >> 8) & 0xFF));
    a.append(static_cast<char>((v >> 16) & 0xFF)); a.append(static_cast<char>((v >> 24) & 0xFF));
}
qint32 readLE(const QByteArray& a, int off) {
    return static_cast<qint32>(
        (static_cast<quint32>(static_cast<quint8>(a.at(off)))) |
        (static_cast<quint32>(static_cast<quint8>(a.at(off + 1))) << 8) |
        (static_cast<quint32>(static_cast<quint8>(a.at(off + 2))) << 16) |
        (static_cast<quint32>(static_cast<quint8>(a.at(off + 3))) << 24));
}
} // namespace

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setArbCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT v)
{
    if (v < static_cast<U32BIT>(PlzShared::ARB_POINTS_MIN) || v > static_cast<U32BIT>(PlzShared::ARB_POINTS_MAX))
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB count out of range [3 .. 100]"));
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:COUN ") + QByteArray::number(v));
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getArbCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o)
{ S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:COUN?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setArbPoint(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT i, FDOUBLE vv, FDOUBLE aa)
{
    if (i < 2) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB point 1 is fixed at 0 V / 0 A"));
    if (vv < 0.0 || vv > m_pLimits->m_dVoltageMax || aa < 0.0 || aa > m_pLimits->m_dCurrentMax)
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB point outside limits"));
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:MAP ") + QByteArray::number(i) + "," + fmtD(vv) + "," + fmtD(aa));
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getArbPoint(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT i, FDOUBLE& vv, FDOUBLE& aa)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:MAP? ") + QByteArray::number(i), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    const QList<QByteArray> parts = r.split(',');
    if (parts.size() < 2) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_RESPONSE);
    vv = parts.at(0).trimmed().toDouble(); aa = parts.at(1).trimmed().toDouble();
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setArbTable(U32BIT l, U32BIT, const QVector<FDOUBLE>& v, const QVector<FDOUBLE>& a)
{
    if (v.size() != a.size()) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("ARB vectors differ in size"));
    const int n = v.size();
    if (n < PlzShared::ARB_POINTS_MIN || n > PlzShared::ARB_POINTS_MAX)
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB count out of range [3 .. 100]"));
    if (std::fabs(v[0]) > 1e-9 || std::fabs(a[0]) > 1e-9)
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB point 1 must be 0 V / 0 A"));
    if (std::fabs(v[n - 1] - m_pLimits->m_dVoltageMax) > 1e-6)
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("last ARB voltage must be %1 V").arg(m_pLimits->m_dVoltageMax));
    QByteArray payload;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (v[i] < 0.0 || v[i] > m_pLimits->m_dVoltageMax || a[i] < 0.0 || a[i] > m_pLimits->m_dCurrentMax)
            return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB point %1 outside limits").arg(i + 1));
        appendLE(payload, static_cast<qint32>(std::llround(v[i] * 1e6)));
        appendLE(payload, static_cast<qint32>(std::llround(a[i] * 1e6)));
    }
    const QByteArray len = QByteArray::number(payload.size());
    QByteArray cmd = QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:DATA #") + QByteArray::number(len.size()) + len + payload;
    return sendChecked(l, cmd);
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getArbTable(U32BIT l, U32BIT, QVector<FDOUBLE>& v, QVector<FDOUBLE>& a)
{
    ELoadError e = writeLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:DATA?"));
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    QByteArray resp; e = readLine(l, resp);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    // resp is "#<w><len><binary>" but readLine stops at LF; MockVisa/instrument
    // that returns ASCII pairs is handled generically here for the emulator.
    v.clear(); a.clear();
    if (resp.startsWith('#')) {
        const int w = resp.at(1) - '0';
        const int len = resp.mid(2, w).toInt();
        const QByteArray bin = resp.mid(2 + w, len);
        for (int i = 0; i + 8 <= bin.size(); i += 8) {
            v.append(readLE(bin, i) / 1e6);
            a.append(readLE(bin, i + 4) / 1e6);
        }
    }
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::applyArb(U32BIT l, U32BIT) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:APPL")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::clearArb(U32BIT l, U32BIT) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:CLE")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setArbResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v) { return setDiscrete(l, "ARB:RESP", v, PlzShared::ARB_RESPONSE_VALUES, PlzShared::ARB_RESPONSE_COUNT, "ARB response"); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getArbResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o) { return queryDouble(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ARB:RESP?"), o); }

/*============================================================================
 *  Sequence
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::createProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n)
{ if (n.isEmpty() || n.size() > 255) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER); return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:CRE \"/") + n.toUtf8() + "\""); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::selectProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n)
{ return n.isEmpty() ? sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG \"\"")) : sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG \"/") + n.toUtf8() + "\""); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getSelectedProgram(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG?"), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    QByteArray n = r.trimmed();
    if (n.startsWith('"') && n.endsWith('"') && n.size() >= 2) n = n.mid(1, n.size() - 2);
    if (n.startsWith('/')) n = n.mid(1);
    o = QString::fromUtf8(n);
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::deleteProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:DEL \"/") + n.toUtf8() + "\""); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::renameProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n)
{ if (n.isEmpty() || n.size() > 255) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER); return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:REN \"") + n.toUtf8() + "\""); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::saveProgram(U32BIT l) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:SAVE")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::listPrograms(U32BIT l, QStringList& o)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:LIST?"), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    o.clear();
    const QList<QByteArray> parts = r.split(',');
    for (QByteArray n : parts) {
        n = n.trimmed();
        if (n.startsWith('"') && n.endsWith('"') && n.size() >= 2) n = n.mid(1, n.size() - 2);
        if (n.startsWith('/')) n = n.mid(1);
        if (!n.isEmpty()) o << QString::fromUtf8(n);
    }
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setProgramLoop(U32BIT l, S32BIT v)
{
    if (v == -1) return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:LOOP INF"));
    if (v < 1 || v > 100000) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("loop count out of range [1 .. 100000] or -1"));
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:LOOP ") + QByteArray::number(v));
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getProgramLoop(U32BIT l, S32BIT& o)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:LOOP?"), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    o = r.trimmed().toUpper().startsWith("INF") ? -1 : r.trimmed().toInt();
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setProgramStepCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) { return setIntScpi(l, "PROG:STEP:COUN", static_cast<S32BIT>(v), 1, 10000, "step count"); }

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setProgramProtection(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_Mode w, FDOUBLE v)
{
    switch (w) {
    case Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC: return setDouble(l, "PROG:CURR:PROT", v, 0.0, m_pLimits->m_dOcpMax, "program OCP");
    case Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCP: return setDouble(l, "PROG:POW:PROT", v, 0.0, m_pLimits->m_dOppMax, "program OPP");
    case Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCV: return setDouble(l, "PROG:VOLT:PROT:LOW", v, 0.0, m_pLimits->m_dUvpMax, "program UVP");
    default: return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getStepDwell(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE& o) { return queryDouble(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:STEP") + QByteArray::number(s) + ":DWEL?", o); }

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setStepTransition(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, Enum_ELoad_Transition v)
{ return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:STEP") + QByteArray::number(s) + ":TRAN " + TRANS_TOK[static_cast<int>(v)]); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getStepTransition(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, Enum_ELoad_Transition& o)
{ QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:STEP") + QByteArray::number(s) + ":TRAN?", r); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
  o = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed().toUpper().startsWith(QStringLiteral("RAMP")) ? Enum_ELoad_Transition::m_enumRamp : Enum_ELoad_Transition::m_enumImmediate; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setStepTriggerOut(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT v)
{
    static const struct { int bit; const char* tok; } T[] = { {1,"TRIGOUT"},{2,"DIGITAL0"},{4,"DIGITAL1"},{8,"DIGITAL2"},{16,"TALINK"} };
    QByteArray cmd = QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:STEP") + QByteArray::number(s) + ":TRIG:GEN ";
    if (v == 0) { cmd += "NONE"; }
    else { QStringList toks; for (auto& t : T) if (v & t.bit) toks << QLatin1String(t.tok); cmd += toks.join(QLatin1Char(',')).toLatin1(); }
    return sendChecked(l, cmd);
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getStepTriggerOut(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT& o)
{
    static const struct { int bit; const char* tok; } T[] = { {1,"TRIGOUT"},{2,"DIGITAL0"},{4,"DIGITAL1"},{8,"DIGITAL2"},{16,"TALINK"} };
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:STEP") + QByteArray::number(s) + ":TRIG:GEN?", r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    const QString up = QString::fromLatin1(r).toUpper(); U32BIT f = 0;
    for (auto& t : T) if (up.contains(QLatin1String(t.tok))) f |= t.bit;
    o = f; return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setStepTriggerWait(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT v)
{
    static const struct { int bit; const char* tok; } T[] = { {1,"IMM"},{2,"BUS"},{4,"TRIGIN"},{8,"DIGITAL2"},{16,"MSYN"},{32,"CUTOFF"} };
    const bool imm = (v & 1), bus = (v & 2);
    if (v == 0 || v > 0x3F || (imm && v != 1) || (bus && v != 2))
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("invalid trigger-wait flags (IMM/BUS exclusive)"));
    QStringList toks; for (auto& t : T) if (v & t.bit) toks << QLatin1String(t.tok);
    return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:STEP") + QByteArray::number(s) + ":TRIG:WAIT " + toks.join(QLatin1Char(',')).toLatin1());
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getStepTriggerWait(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT& o)
{
    static const struct { int bit; const char* tok; } T[] = { {1,"IMM"},{2,"BUS"},{4,"TRIGIN"},{8,"DIGITAL2"},{16,"MSYN"},{32,"CUTOFF"} };
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("PROG:STEP") + QByteArray::number(s) + ":TRIG:WAIT?", r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    const QString up = QString::fromLatin1(r).toUpper(); U32BIT f = 0;
    for (auto& t : T) if (up.contains(QLatin1String(t.tok))) f |= t.bit;
    o = f; return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::startSequence(U32BIT l) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("INIT:TRAN:PROG")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::abortSequence(U32BIT l) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("ABOR:TRAN")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::suspendSequence(U32BIT l) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("TRIG:TRAN:SUSP")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::resumeSequence(U32BIT l) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArrayLiteral("TRIG:TRAN:RES")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getSequenceState(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_SeqState& st, U32BIT& step, U32BIT& loop)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("TRIG:TRAN:EXEC?"), r);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    const QList<QByteArray> parts = r.split(',');
    const QString s = QString::fromLatin1(parts.value(0).trimmed()).toUpper();
    if (s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("WTG")))       st = Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumWaiting;
    else if (s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("RUN")))  st = Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumRunning;
    else if (s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("SUSP"))) st = Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumSuspended;
    else if (s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("PULS"))) st = Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumPulse;
    else if (s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("SINE"))) st = Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumSine;
    else st = Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumIdle;
    step = static_cast<U32BIT>(parts.value(1).trimmed().toInt());
    loop = static_cast<U32BIT>(parts.value(2).trimmed().toInt());
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setAcqTriggerSource(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_AcqTrigSource v) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArray("TRIG:ACQ:SOUR ") + ACQSRC_TOK[static_cast<int>(v)]); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getAcqTriggerSource(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_AcqTrigSource& o)
{ QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("TRIG:ACQ:SOUR?"), r); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
  const QString s = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed().toUpper();
  for (int i = 0; i < 6; ++i) if (s.startsWith(QLatin1String(ACQSRC_TOK[i]))) { o = static_cast<Enum_ELoad_AcqTrigSource>(i); return ELoadError(); }
  return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_RESPONSE); }

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setAcqIntervalEnable(U32BIT l, bool v) { return setBoolScpi(l, "TRIG:ACQ:INT:STAT", v); }

/*============================================================================
 *  Status / system
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::readErrorStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT, S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus& o)
{
    S32BIT ques = 0, oper = 0, esr = 0;
    ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("STAT:QUES:COND?"), ques);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
    queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("STAT:OPER:COND?"), oper);
    queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*ESR?"), esr);
    ELoad_DeviceStatus fl = Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::NoError;
    if (ques & 0x0001) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverVoltage;
    if (ques & 0x0002) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverCurrent;
    if (ques & 0x0008) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverPower;
    if (ques & 0x0010) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverTemperature;
    if (ques & 0x0020) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::Watchdog;
    if (ques & 0x0040) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::CurrentLimiting;
    if (ques & 0x0080) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::PowerLimiting;
    if (ques & 0x0200) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::UnderVoltage;
    if (ques & 0x0400) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::ExternalError;
    if (ques & 0x0800) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::ReverseVoltage;
    if (ques & 0x1000) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::ConnectionError;
    if (oper & 0x0100) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::LoadOn;
    o.m_statusFlags = fl;
    o.m_iQuestionableStatus = ques;
    o.m_iOperationStatus = oper;
    o.m_iStandardEventStatus = esr;
    o.m_EnumMode = operToMode(oper);   // reuse the STAT:OPER:COND? read above
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::clearErrorStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT) { return writeLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*CLS")); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::queryErrorQueue(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ S32BIT code = 0; QByteArray msg; ELoadError e = queryErrorRaw(l, code, msg); if (e.isSuccess()) o = QStringLiteral("%1,\"%2\"").arg(code).arg(QString::fromLatin1(msg)); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getInstrumentErrorCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o)
{ S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("SYST:ERR:COUN?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::readStandardEventStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) { S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*ESR?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::readStatusByte(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) { S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("*STB?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::readQuestionableStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o) { S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("STAT:QUES:COND?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::readOperationStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o) { S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("STAT:OPER:COND?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setRemoteState(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_RemoteState v) { return sendChecked(l, QByteArray("SYST:COMM:RLST ") + RLST_TOK[static_cast<int>(v)]); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getRemoteState(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_RemoteState& o)
{ QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, QByteArrayLiteral("SYST:COMM:RLST?"), r); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e;
  const QString s = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed().toUpper();
  o = s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("RWL")) ? Enum_ELoad_RemoteState::m_enumRWLock : (s.startsWith(QStringLiteral("REM")) ? Enum_ELoad_RemoteState::m_enumRemote : Enum_ELoad_RemoteState::m_enumLocal); return ELoadError(); }

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::isKeyLocked(U32BIT l, bool& o) { return queryBool(l, QByteArrayLiteral("SYST:KLOC?"), o); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setKeyLockLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT v) { return setIntScpi(l, "SYST:KLOC:LEV", static_cast<S32BIT>(v), 1, 3, "key lock level"); }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getKeyLockLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o) { S32BIT v = 0; ELoadError e = queryInt(l, QByteArrayLiteral("SYST:KLOC:LEV?"), v); if (e.isSuccess()) o = static_cast<U32BIT>(v); return e; }
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::setBeeper(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_Beeper w, bool v)
{
    const char* s = "SYST:BEEP";
    switch (w) { case Enum_ELoad_Beeper::m_enumKey: s = "SYST:BEEP:KEY"; break; case Enum_ELoad_Beeper::m_enumProtection: s = "SYST:BEEP:PROT"; break;
                 case Enum_ELoad_Beeper::m_enumScpi: s = "SYST:BEEP:SCPI"; break; default: break; }
    return setBoolScpi(l, s, v);
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getBeeper(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_Beeper w, bool& o)
{
    const char* s = "SYST:BEEP?";
    switch (w) { case Enum_ELoad_Beeper::m_enumKey: s = "SYST:BEEP:KEY?"; break; case Enum_ELoad_Beeper::m_enumProtection: s = "SYST:BEEP:PROT?"; break;
                 case Enum_ELoad_Beeper::m_enumScpi: s = "SYST:BEEP:SCPI?"; break; default: break; }
    return queryBool(l, s, o);
}

ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getDigital2Direction(U32BIT l, bool& out)
