/*============================================================================
 *  Debug
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::writeScpi(U32BIT l, const QString& v)
{
    const QByteArray cmd = v.toUtf8().trimmed();
    if (cmd.isEmpty()) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER);
    if (cmd.contains('?')) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("use queryScpi for queries"));
    return writeLine(l, cmd);
}
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::queryScpi(U32BIT l, const QString& v, QString& o)
{
    QByteArray r; ELoadError e = queryLine(l, v.toUtf8().trimmed(), r);
    if (e.isSuccess()) o = QString::fromLatin1(r).trimmed();
    return e;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Parameter ranges (for auto-ranged UIs)
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CPLZ4005WH2Plugin::getParameterRange(U32BIT, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_ParamId in_eParam, S_ELoad_ParameterRange& o)
{
    const S_PlzLimits& s = *m_pLimits;
    o = S_ELoad_ParameterRange();
    switch (in_eParam) {
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCurrent:          o.m_dMax = s.m_dCurrentMax;  o.m_dResolution = s.m_dCurrentRes;  break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumVoltage:          o.m_dMax = s.m_dVoltageMax;  o.m_dResolution = s.m_dVoltageRes;  break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumConductanceHigh:  o.m_dMax = s.m_dCondHighMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dCondHighRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumConductanceLow:   o.m_dMax = s.m_dCondLowMax;  o.m_dResolution = s.m_dCondLowRes;  break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumPower:            o.m_dMax = s.m_dPowerMax;    o.m_dResolution = s.m_dPowerRes;    break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSlewRate:         o.m_dMin = s.m_dSlewMin; o.m_dMax = s.m_dSlewMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dSlewRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSoftStart:        o.m_dMax = 0.1; for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::SOFT_START_COUNT; ++i) o.m_QlistDiscreteValues << PlzShared::SOFT_START_VALUES[i]; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumOcpLevel:         o.m_dMax = s.m_dOcpMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dOcpRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumOppLevel:         o.m_dMax = s.m_dOppMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dOppRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumUvpLevel:         o.m_dMax = s.m_dUvpMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dUvpRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumWatchdogDelay:    o.m_dMin = PlzShared::WATCHDOG_DELAY_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::WATCHDOG_DELAY_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCutoffVoltage:    o.m_dMax = PlzShared::CUTOFF_VOLTAGE_MAX; o.m_dResolution = PlzShared::CUTOFF_VOLTAGE_RES; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCutoffCapacity:   o.m_dMax = s.m_dCutoffCapacityMax; o.m_dResolution = 0.001; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCutoffEnergy:     o.m_dMax = s.m_dCutoffEnergyMax; o.m_dResolution = 0.001; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCutoffTime:       o.m_dMax = PlzShared::CUTOFF_TIME_MAX; o.m_dResolution = PlzShared::CUTOFF_TIME_RES; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumTimedInput:       o.m_dMin = PlzShared::TIMED_INPUT_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::TIMED_INPUT_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumPulseFrequency:   o.m_dMin = PlzShared::PULSE_FREQ_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::PULSE_FREQ_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 0.1; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumPulseDuty:        o.m_dMin = PlzShared::PULSE_DUTY_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::PULSE_DUTY_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 0.1; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSineFrequency:    o.m_dMin = PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0;
        for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_EXTRA_COUNT; ++i) { o.m_QlistDiscreteValues << PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_EXTRA[i]; }
        break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumArbResponse:      o.m_dMax = 0.1; for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::ARB_RESPONSE_COUNT; ++i) o.m_QlistDiscreteValues << PlzShared::ARB_RESPONSE_VALUES[i]; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSenseAperture:    o.m_dMin = PlzShared::APERTURE_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::APERTURE_MAX; o.m_dResolution = PlzShared::APERTURE_MIN; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumBufferSize:       o.m_dMin = 1024; o.m_dMax = 65536; for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::BUFFER_SIZE_COUNT; ++i) o.m_QlistDiscreteValues << PlzShared::BUFFER_SIZE_VALUES[i]; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumAcqTrigCount:     o.m_dMin = PlzShared::ACQ_COUNT_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::ACQ_COUNT_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumAcqTrigDelay:     o.m_dMin = PlzShared::ACQ_DELAY_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::ACQ_DELAY_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1e-7; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumAcqIntervalTime:  o.m_dMin = PlzShared::ACQ_INTERVAL_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::ACQ_INTERVAL_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1e-5; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumTranTrigDelay:    o.m_dMin = PlzShared::TRAN_DELAY_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::TRAN_DELAY_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1e-5; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumProgramLoop:      o.m_dMin = PlzShared::PROGRAM_LOOP_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::PROGRAM_LOOP_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumProgramStepCount: o.m_dMin = PlzShared::PROGRAM_STEPS_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::PROGRAM_STEPS_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumStepDwell:        o.m_dMin = PlzShared::STEP_DWELL_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::STEP_DWELL_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1e-6; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumStepSlewRate:     o.m_dMin = s.m_dSlewMin; o.m_dMax = s.m_dSlewMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dStepSlewRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumScreenSaverDelay: o.m_dMin = PlzShared::SCREEN_SAVER_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::SCREEN_SAVER_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    default: return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("unknown parameter id"));
    }
    return ELoadError();
}

-- PluginSimLoad/PluginSimLoad.pro

QT -= gui

# Virtual (hardware-free) electronic load - no VISA

TEMPLATE = lib
CONFIG += plugin c++11

TARGET = PluginSimLoad
DESTDIR = ../plugins

INCLUDEPATH += ../include ../Common

LIBS += -L../lib -lELoadCore

HEADERS += \
    SimLoadPlugin.h

SOURCES += \
    SimLoadPlugin.cpp

-- PluginSimLoad/SimLoadPlugin.h

/*============================================================================
 *  PluginSimLoad.h
 *
 *  Virtual (hardware-free) electronic-load plugin. Implements the full
 *  CIELoadPlugin surface with no VISA: any resource string is accepted, and
 *  "SIM::<Model>" (or a resource containing a PLZ...WH2 token) selects the
 *  limits row of that family member (default PLZ4005WH2) so parameter ranges
 *  and validation match the real PLZ plugins exactly.
 *
 *  A small electrical model derives V/I/P from the panel state and runs the
 *  integrators, timed load-off and protection comparisons, so the tester and
 *  example programs behave sensibly with no instrument attached.
 *
 *  \author  ELoad framework
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef CSIMLOAD_H
#define CSIMLOAD_H

#include <QObject>
#include <QMap>
#include <QVector>
#include <QElapsedTimer>

#include "IELoadPlugin.h"
#include "S_PlzLimits.h"

/*--- one sequence step of the simulated PROG subsystem ---------------------*/
struct S_SimStep {
    double m_dLevel;
    double m_dDwell;
    double m_dSlewRate;
    bool   m_bInput;
    int    m_iTransition;
    U32BIT m_u32TriggerOut;
    U32BIT m_u32TriggerWait;
    S_SimStep()
        : m_dLevel(0.0), m_dDwell(0.1), m_dSlewRate(0.0), m_bInput(true)
        , m_iTransition(0), m_u32TriggerOut(0), m_u32TriggerWait(0x01) {}
};

/*--- one stored sequence program -------------------------------------------*/
struct S_SimProgram {
    S32BIT             m_iLoop;          /* -1 = infinite */
    QVector<S_SimStep> m_vecSteps;
    S_SimProgram() : m_iLoop(1) {}
};

/*--- the complete instrument panel state (everything *RST resets) ----------*/
struct S_SimState {
    int    m_iMode;
    bool   m_bCVOption;
    double m_dCurrent, m_dVoltage, m_dConductance, m_dPower;
    int    m_iCRRange, m_iCVResponse, m_iCRResponse;
    double m_dSlewRate, m_dSoftStart;
    bool   m_bExtCurrentSum;
    bool   m_bExtFullControl[5];
    bool   m_bInputOn;
    int    m_iTimedInputSeconds;
    int    m_iPowerOnState, m_iExtLogic;
    bool   m_bMSyncAccept;
    double m_dCutoffVoltage;   bool m_bCutoffVoltageEnable;
    double m_dCutoffCapacity;  bool m_bCutoffCapacityEnable;
    double m_dCutoffEnergy;    bool m_bCutoffEnergyEnable;
    double m_dCutoffTime;      bool m_bCutoffTimeEnable;
    double m_dOcpLevel;  int m_iOcpAction;
    double m_dOppLevel;  int m_iOppAction;
    double m_dUvpLevel;  int m_iUvpAction;  bool m_bUvpEnable;
    bool   m_bWatchdogEnable;  double m_dWatchdogDelay;
    double m_dSenseAperture;
    bool   m_bDisplayCapacity, m_bDisplayEnergy, m_bDisplayElapsedTime;
    int    m_iBufferSize;
    QVector<int> m_vecDataFormat;
    int    m_iIntegrationGate;  bool m_bIntegrationAutoReset;
    double m_dPulseCurrentLevel, m_dPulseConductanceLevel, m_dPulseFrequency, m_dPulseDuty;
    double m_dSineAmplitude, m_dSineFrequency;
    QVector<double> m_vecArbVolts, m_vecArbAmps;
    double m_dArbResponse;
    int    m_iAcqTrigSource, m_iAcqTrigCount;
    double m_dAcqTrigDelay;
    bool   m_bAcqIntervalEnable;  double m_dAcqIntervalTime;
    int    m_iTranTrigSource;     double m_dTranTrigDelay;
    int    m_iKeyLockLevel;  bool m_bKeyLock;
    int    m_iRemoteState;
    bool   m_bBeeper[4];
    bool   m_bRemoteSensing;
    int    m_iDigital2Direction;
    bool   m_bScreenSaver;  double m_dScreenSaverDelay;

    S_SimState() { Reset(nullptr); }
    void Reset(const S_PlzLimits* in_psLimits);
};

/*--- per-load simulator instance (panel state + runtime) -------------------*/
struct S_SimInstance {
    S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig m_sConfig;
    bool               m_bConnected;
    const S_PlzLimits* m_pLimits;
    U32BIT             m_u32Timeout;
    S_SimState         m_sState;

    /* runtime (not part of *RST panel state) */
    QElapsedTimer m_timerTimedInput;
    QElapsedTimer m_timerIntegration;
    bool          m_bIntegrating;
    double        m_dIntegBaseSeconds, m_dCapacityAh, m_dEnergyWh;
    U32BIT        m_u32ProtFlags;
    double        m_dMeasVolts, m_dMeasAmps, m_dMeasWatts;

    /* sequence + memories */
    QMap<QString, S_SimProgram> m_mapPrograms;
    QString                     m_strSelectedProgram;
    bool                        m_bSeqRunning, m_bSeqSuspended;
    U32BIT                      m_u32SeqStep, m_u32SeqLoop;
    QMap<U32BIT, S_SimState>    m_mapSetupMemories;
    QMap<U32BIT, S_SimState>    m_mapPresetMemories;

    S_SimInstance()
        : m_bConnected(false), m_pLimits(PlzFindLimits("PLZ4005WH2")), m_u32Timeout(3000)
        , m_bIntegrating(false), m_dIntegBaseSeconds(0.0), m_dCapacityAh(0.0), m_dEnergyWh(0.0)
        , m_u32ProtFlags(0), m_dMeasVolts(0.0), m_dMeasAmps(0.0), m_dMeasWatts(0.0)
        , m_bSeqRunning(false), m_bSeqSuspended(false), m_u32SeqStep(0), m_u32SeqLoop(0) {}
};

    ELoadError getBeeper(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_Beeper w, bool& o) override;

    /*==== debug ==========================================================*/
    ELoadError writeScpi(U32BIT l, const QString& v) override;
    ELoadError queryScpi(U32BIT l, const QString& v, QString& o) override;

private:
    S_SimInstance* dev(U32BIT l);
    const S_SimInstance* dev(U32BIT l) const;
    void updateSimulation(S_SimInstance& d);
    static double simSourceVolts();

    /* range helpers reused across setters */
    ELoadError chkRange(FDOUBLE v, FDOUBLE lo, FDOUBLE hi, const char* what) const;
    ELoadError chkDiscrete(FDOUBLE v, const double* set, int n, const char* what) const;

    QMap<U32BIT, S_SimInstance> m_devices;
};

#endif // CSIMLOAD_H

-- PluginSimLoad/SimLoadPlugin.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  PluginSimLoad.cpp - virtual electronic-load plugin implementation.
 *
 *  Holds per-load in-memory panel state and a small electrical model; no VISA
 *  and no hardware. Range validation reuses the same S_PlzLimits rows as the
 *  real PLZ plugins, so getParameterRange and out-of-range rejection match.
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "SimLoadPlugin.h"
#include "VisaHelper.h"        // inline S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig::toVisaResourceString()

#include <QByteArray>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <cmath>

namespace {
const double SIM_SOURCE_VOLTS = 50.0;    /* simulated DUT source voltage   */
const double SIM_SOURCE_OHMS  = 0.01;    /* simulated DUT source impedance */

/* STAT:QUES condition bits, matching the real plugin's decoding. */
const U32BIT QUES_OC = 0x0002, QUES_OP = 0x0008, QUES_UV = 0x0200;
const U32BIT QUES_CL = 0x0040, QUES_PL = 0x0080;

const S_PlzLimits* limitsFromResource(const QString& in_strRes)
{
    QRegularExpression re(QStringLiteral("PLZ\\d+WH2"));
    const QRegularExpressionMatch m = re.match(in_strRes);
    const S_PlzLimits* p = m.hasMatch()
            ? PlzFindLimits(m.captured(0).toLatin1().constData()) : nullptr;
    return p ? p : PlzFindLimits("PLZ4005WH2");
}
} // namespace

/*============================================================================
 *  S_SimState::Reset
 *==========================================================================*/
void S_SimState::Reset(const S_PlzLimits* in_psLimits)
{
    m_iMode = 0; m_bCVOption = false;
    m_dCurrent = m_dVoltage = m_dConductance = m_dPower = 0.0;
    m_iCRRange = 1; m_iCVResponse = 0; m_iCRResponse = 0;
    m_dSlewRate = in_psLimits ? in_psLimits->m_dSlewMin : 0.0;
    m_dSoftStart = 0.0005;
    m_bExtCurrentSum = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) m_bExtFullControl[i] = false;
    m_bInputOn = false; m_iTimedInputSeconds = 0;
    m_iPowerOnState = 2; m_iExtLogic = 0; m_bMSyncAccept = false;
    m_dCutoffVoltage = 0.0;  m_bCutoffVoltageEnable = false;
    m_dCutoffCapacity = 0.0; m_bCutoffCapacityEnable = false;
    m_dCutoffEnergy = 0.0;   m_bCutoffEnergyEnable = false;
    m_dCutoffTime = 0.0;     m_bCutoffTimeEnable = false;
    m_dOcpLevel = in_psLimits ? in_psLimits->m_dOcpMax : 0.0; m_iOcpAction = 0;
    m_dOppLevel = in_psLimits ? in_psLimits->m_dOppMax : 0.0; m_iOppAction = 0;
    m_dUvpLevel = 0.0; m_iUvpAction = 0; m_bUvpEnable = false;
    m_bWatchdogEnable = false; m_dWatchdogDelay = 60.0;
    m_dSenseAperture = 0.1;
    m_bDisplayCapacity = false; m_bDisplayEnergy = false; m_bDisplayElapsedTime = true;
    m_iBufferSize = 65536;
    m_vecDataFormat.clear(); m_vecDataFormat << 0 << 1 << 2;
    m_iIntegrationGate = 1; m_bIntegrationAutoReset = true;
    m_dPulseCurrentLevel = 0.0; m_dPulseConductanceLevel = 0.0;
    m_dPulseFrequency = 1.0; m_dPulseDuty = 50.0;
    m_dSineAmplitude = 0.0; m_dSineFrequency = 1.0;
    m_vecArbVolts.clear(); m_vecArbAmps.clear();
    const double dArbMaxV = in_psLimits ? in_psLimits->m_dVoltageMax : 1010.0;
    m_vecArbVolts << 0.0 << dArbMaxV / 2.0 << dArbMaxV;
    m_vecArbAmps  << 0.0 << 0.0 << 0.0;
    m_dArbResponse = 0.0005;
    m_iAcqTrigSource = 0; m_iAcqTrigCount = 1; m_dAcqTrigDelay = 0.0;
    m_bAcqIntervalEnable = false; m_dAcqIntervalTime = 0.1;
    m_iTranTrigSource = 0; m_dTranTrigDelay = 0.0;
    m_iKeyLockLevel = 3; m_bKeyLock = false; m_iRemoteState = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) m_bBeeper[i] = true;
    m_bRemoteSensing = false; m_iDigital2Direction = 0;
    m_bScreenSaver = false; m_dScreenSaverDelay = 60.0;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Construction / info
 *==========================================================================*/
CSimLoadPlugin::CSimLoadPlugin() {}
CSimLoadPlugin::~CSimLoadPlugin() { m_devices.clear(); }

S_SimInstance* CSimLoadPlugin::dev(U32BIT l)
{ auto it = m_devices.find(l); return it == m_devices.end() ? nullptr : &it.value(); }
const S_SimInstance* CSimLoadPlugin::dev(U32BIT l) const
{ auto it = m_devices.find(l); return it == m_devices.end() ? nullptr : &it.value(); }

double CSimLoadPlugin::simSourceVolts() { return SIM_SOURCE_VOLTS; }

S_ELoad_PluginInfo CSimLoadPlugin::getPluginInfo() const
{
    S_ELoad_PluginInfo s;
    qstrncpy(s.m_szName, "SimLoad", PLUGIN_INFO_NAME_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szVersion, "1.0.0", PLUGIN_INFO_VERSION_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szManufacturer, "ELoad framework", PLUGIN_INFO_MANUFACTURER_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szModelName, "SimLoad", PLUGIN_INFO_MODEL_NAME_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szSeries, "Virtual", PLUGIN_INFO_SERIES_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szDescription,
             "Virtual PLZ-5WH2-family electronic load (no hardware, no VISA)",
             PLUGIN_INFO_DESCRIPTION_SIZE);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szMinCoreVersion, "1.0.0", PLUGIN_INFO_MIN_CORE_VERSION);
    qstrncpy(s.m_szMaxCoreVersion, "2.0.0", PLUGIN_INFO_MAX_CORE_VERSION);
    s.m_StrlstSupportedProtocols << "SIM";
    s.m_StrlstSupportedModes << "CC" << "CR" << "CV" << "CP" << "ARB";
    return s;
}

S_ELoad_Capabilities CSimLoadPlugin::getCapabilities() const
{
    const S_PlzLimits* p = PlzFindLimits("PLZ4005WH2");
    S_ELoad_Capabilities caps;
    caps.m_u32NumberOfChannels = 1;
    caps.m_dMaxVoltage = p->m_dVoltageRating;
    caps.m_dMaxCurrent = p->m_dCurrentRating;
    caps.m_dMaxPower   = p->m_dPowerRating;
    caps.m_bHasOCP = caps.m_bHasOPP = caps.m_bHasUVP = true;
    caps.m_bHasOVP = false;
    caps.m_bSupportsParallel = false;
    caps.m_u32MaxParallelUnits = 1;
    S_ELoad_ChannelCapabilities ch;
    ch.m_u32ChannelNumber = 1;
    ch.m_dMaxVoltage = p->m_dVoltageRating;
    ch.m_dMaxCurrent = p->m_dCurrentRating;
    ch.m_dMaxPower   = p->m_dPowerRating;
    ch.m_dMaxConductance = p->m_dCondHighMax;
    ch.m_bHasOCP = ch.m_bHasOPP = ch.m_bHasUVP = true;
    ch.m_bHasOVP = false;
    ch.m_bHasARB = ch.m_bHasSequence = ch.m_bHasPulseSine = true;
    caps.m_QlistChannels.append(ch);
    return caps;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Connection
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::connect(U32BIT l, const S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig& c)
{
    if (dev(l) != nullptr && m_devices[l].m_bConnected)
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::ALREADY_CONNECTED);
    S_SimInstance d;
    d.m_sConfig = c;
    d.m_u32Timeout = c.m_u32Timeout ? c.m_u32Timeout : 3000;
    d.m_pLimits = limitsFromResource(c.toVisaResourceString());
    d.m_sState.Reset(d.m_pLimits);
    d.m_bConnected = true;
    m_devices[l] = d;
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::disconnect(U32BIT l)
{
    S_SimInstance* d = dev(l);
    if (d == nullptr) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED);
    m_devices.remove(l);
    return ELoadError();
}

bool CSimLoadPlugin::isConnected(U32BIT l) const
{ const S_SimInstance* d = dev(l); return d != nullptr && d->m_bConnected; }

ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::reset(U32BIT l)
{
    S_SimInstance* d = dev(l);
    if (d == nullptr) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED);
    d->m_sState.Reset(d->m_pLimits);
    d->m_bIntegrating = false;
    d->m_dIntegBaseSeconds = d->m_dCapacityAh = d->m_dEnergyWh = 0.0;
    d->m_u32ProtFlags = 0;
    d->m_bSeqRunning = d->m_bSeqSuspended = false;
    return ELoadError();
}

ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setTimeout(U32BIT l, U32BIT ms)
{ S_SimInstance* d = dev(l); if (!d) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED); d->m_u32Timeout = ms; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::clearStatus(U32BIT l)
{ S_SimInstance* d = dev(l); if (!d) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED); d->m_u32ProtFlags = 0; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getIdentification(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{
    S_SimInstance* d = dev(l);
    if (!d) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED);
    o = QStringLiteral("ELoad,SimLoad(%1),SIM0001,IFO1.00 FPGA1.00 IOC1.00")
            .arg(QString::fromLatin1(d->m_pLimits->m_szModelName));
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::selfTest(U32BIT l, S32BIT& o)
{ if (!dev(l)) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED); o = 0; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getOptions(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ if (!dev(l)) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED); o = QStringLiteral("0"); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::waitOperationComplete(U32BIT l)
{ return dev(l) ? ELoadError() : ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getScpiVersion(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ if (!dev(l)) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED); o = QStringLiteral("1999.0"); return ELoadError(); }

/*============================================================================
 *  Range helpers
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::chkRange(FDOUBLE v, FDOUBLE lo, FDOUBLE hi, const char* what) const
{
    if (v < lo || v > hi)
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE,
                          QStringLiteral("%1 %2 out of range [%3 .. %4]")
                          .arg(QLatin1String(what)).arg(v).arg(lo).arg(hi));
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::chkDiscrete(FDOUBLE v, const double* set, int n, const char* what) const
{
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) if (std::fabs(v - set[i]) < 1e-12) return ELoadError();
    return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE,
                      QStringLiteral("%1 %2 is not a valid discrete value").arg(QLatin1String(what)).arg(v));
}

/*============================================================================
 *  Electrical model
 *==========================================================================*/
void CSimLoadPlugin::updateSimulation(S_SimInstance& d)
{
    S_SimState& st = d.m_sState;
    const S_PlzLimits* lim = d.m_pLimits;

    if (st.m_bInputOn && st.m_iTimedInputSeconds > 0 && d.m_timerTimedInput.isValid() &&
        d.m_timerTimedInput.elapsed() >= qint64(st.m_iTimedInputSeconds) * 1000) {
        st.m_bInputOn = false; st.m_iTimedInputSeconds = 0;
    }

    double dVolts = simSourceVolts();
    double dAmps  = 0.0;
    if (st.m_bInputOn) {
        switch (st.m_iMode) {
        case 0: dAmps = qMin(st.m_dCurrent, lim->m_dCurrentRating); break;
        case 1: dAmps = qMin(dVolts * st.m_dConductance, lim->m_dCurrentRating); break;
        case 2: dVolts = qMin(st.m_dVoltage, simSourceVolts());
                dAmps  = qMin((simSourceVolts() - dVolts) / SIM_SOURCE_OHMS, lim->m_dCurrentRating); break;
        case 3: dAmps = (dVolts > 0.0) ? qMin(st.m_dPower / dVolts, lim->m_dCurrentRating) : 0.0; break;
        case 4: {
            const QVector<double>& vV = st.m_vecArbVolts; const QVector<double>& vA = st.m_vecArbAmps;
            for (int i = 1; i < vV.size(); ++i) {
                if (dVolts <= vV.at(i) || i == vV.size() - 1) {
                    const double span = vV.at(i) - vV.at(i - 1);
                    const double frac = (span > 0.0) ? (dVolts - vV.at(i - 1)) / span : 0.0;
                    dAmps = vA.at(i - 1) + qBound(0.0, frac, 1.0) * (vA.at(i) - vA.at(i - 1));
                    break;
                }
            }
            dAmps = qMin(dAmps, lim->m_dCurrentRating); break;
        }
        default: break;
        }
    }

    if (st.m_bInputOn) {
        if (dAmps > st.m_dOcpLevel) {
            if (st.m_iOcpAction == 1) { d.m_u32ProtFlags |= QUES_OC; st.m_bInputOn = false; }
            else { dAmps = st.m_dOcpLevel; d.m_u32ProtFlags |= QUES_CL; }
        }
        if (dVolts * dAmps > st.m_dOppLevel) {
            if (st.m_iOppAction == 1) { d.m_u32ProtFlags |= QUES_OP; st.m_bInputOn = false; }
            else { dAmps = (dVolts > 0.0) ? st.m_dOppLevel / dVolts : 0.0; d.m_u32ProtFlags |= QUES_PL; }
        }
        if (st.m_bUvpEnable && dVolts < st.m_dUvpLevel) {
            d.m_u32ProtFlags |= QUES_UV;
            if (st.m_iUvpAction == 1) st.m_bInputOn = false;
        }
    }

    const double dWatts = dVolts * dAmps;

    const bool bGateOpen =
            (st.m_iIntegrationGate == 1 && st.m_bInputOn) ||
            (st.m_iIntegrationGate == 2 && d.m_bSeqRunning) || d.m_bIntegrating;
    if (bGateOpen && !d.m_bIntegrating) {
        if (st.m_bIntegrationAutoReset) d.m_dCapacityAh = d.m_dEnergyWh = d.m_dIntegBaseSeconds = 0.0;
        d.m_bIntegrating = true; d.m_timerIntegration.restart();
    } else if (!bGateOpen && d.m_bIntegrating && st.m_iIntegrationGate != 0) {
        d.m_dIntegBaseSeconds += d.m_timerIntegration.elapsed() / 1000.0;
        d.m_bIntegrating = false;
    }
    if (d.m_bIntegrating && d.m_timerIntegration.isValid()) {
        const double dt = d.m_timerIntegration.restart() / 1000.0;
        d.m_dIntegBaseSeconds += dt;
        d.m_dCapacityAh += dAmps * dt / 3600.0;
        d.m_dEnergyWh   += dWatts * dt / 3600.0;
    }

    if (st.m_bInputOn) {
        if ((st.m_bCutoffTimeEnable && st.m_dCutoffTime > 0.0 && d.m_dIntegBaseSeconds >= st.m_dCutoffTime) ||
            (st.m_bCutoffCapacityEnable && st.m_dCutoffCapacity > 0.0 && d.m_dCapacityAh >= st.m_dCutoffCapacity) ||
            (st.m_bCutoffEnergyEnable && st.m_dCutoffEnergy > 0.0 && d.m_dEnergyWh >= st.m_dCutoffEnergy) ||
            (st.m_bCutoffVoltageEnable && dVolts <= st.m_dCutoffVoltage)) {
            st.m_bInputOn = false;
        }
    }

    d.m_dMeasVolts = st.m_bInputOn ? dVolts : simSourceVolts();
    d.m_dMeasAmps  = st.m_bInputOn ? dAmps : 0.0;
    d.m_dMeasWatts = d.m_dMeasVolts * d.m_dMeasAmps;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  getParameterRange (parity with the real PLZ model plugins)
 *==========================================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getParameterRange(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_ParamId in_eParam, S_ELoad_ParameterRange& o)
{
    S_SimInstance* d = dev(l);
    const S_PlzLimits& s = d ? *d->m_pLimits : *PlzFindLimits("PLZ4005WH2");
    o = S_ELoad_ParameterRange();
    switch (in_eParam) {
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCurrent:          o.m_dMax = s.m_dCurrentMax;  o.m_dResolution = s.m_dCurrentRes;  break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumVoltage:          o.m_dMax = s.m_dVoltageMax;  o.m_dResolution = s.m_dVoltageRes;  break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumConductanceHigh:  o.m_dMax = s.m_dCondHighMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dCondHighRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumConductanceLow:   o.m_dMax = s.m_dCondLowMax;  o.m_dResolution = s.m_dCondLowRes;  break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumPower:            o.m_dMax = s.m_dPowerMax;    o.m_dResolution = s.m_dPowerRes;    break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSlewRate:         o.m_dMin = s.m_dSlewMin; o.m_dMax = s.m_dSlewMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dSlewRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSoftStart:        o.m_dMax = 0.1; for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::SOFT_START_COUNT; ++i) o.m_QlistDiscreteValues << PlzShared::SOFT_START_VALUES[i]; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumOcpLevel:         o.m_dMax = s.m_dOcpMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dOcpRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumOppLevel:         o.m_dMax = s.m_dOppMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dOppRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumUvpLevel:         o.m_dMax = s.m_dUvpMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dUvpRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumWatchdogDelay:    o.m_dMin = PlzShared::WATCHDOG_DELAY_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::WATCHDOG_DELAY_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCutoffVoltage:    o.m_dMax = PlzShared::CUTOFF_VOLTAGE_MAX; o.m_dResolution = PlzShared::CUTOFF_VOLTAGE_RES; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCutoffCapacity:   o.m_dMax = s.m_dCutoffCapacityMax; o.m_dResolution = 0.001; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCutoffEnergy:     o.m_dMax = s.m_dCutoffEnergyMax; o.m_dResolution = 0.001; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCutoffTime:       o.m_dMax = PlzShared::CUTOFF_TIME_MAX; o.m_dResolution = PlzShared::CUTOFF_TIME_RES; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumTimedInput:       o.m_dMin = PlzShared::TIMED_INPUT_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::TIMED_INPUT_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumPulseFrequency:   o.m_dMin = PlzShared::PULSE_FREQ_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::PULSE_FREQ_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 0.1; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumPulseDuty:        o.m_dMin = PlzShared::PULSE_DUTY_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::PULSE_DUTY_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 0.1; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSineFrequency:    o.m_dMin = PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0;
        for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_EXTRA_COUNT; ++i) { o.m_QlistDiscreteValues << PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_EXTRA[i]; }
        break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumArbResponse:      o.m_dMax = 0.1; for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::ARB_RESPONSE_COUNT; ++i) o.m_QlistDiscreteValues << PlzShared::ARB_RESPONSE_VALUES[i]; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSenseAperture:    o.m_dMin = PlzShared::APERTURE_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::APERTURE_MAX; o.m_dResolution = PlzShared::APERTURE_MIN; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumBufferSize:       o.m_dMin = 1024; o.m_dMax = 65536; for (int i = 0; i < PlzShared::BUFFER_SIZE_COUNT; ++i) o.m_QlistDiscreteValues << PlzShared::BUFFER_SIZE_VALUES[i]; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumAcqTrigCount:     o.m_dMin = PlzShared::ACQ_COUNT_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::ACQ_COUNT_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumAcqTrigDelay:     o.m_dMin = PlzShared::ACQ_DELAY_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::ACQ_DELAY_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1e-7; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumAcqIntervalTime:  o.m_dMin = PlzShared::ACQ_INTERVAL_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::ACQ_INTERVAL_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1e-5; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumTranTrigDelay:    o.m_dMin = PlzShared::TRAN_DELAY_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::TRAN_DELAY_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1e-5; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumProgramLoop:      o.m_dMin = PlzShared::PROGRAM_LOOP_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::PROGRAM_LOOP_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumProgramStepCount: o.m_dMin = PlzShared::PROGRAM_STEPS_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::PROGRAM_STEPS_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumStepDwell:        o.m_dMin = PlzShared::STEP_DWELL_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::STEP_DWELL_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1e-6; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumStepSlewRate:     o.m_dMin = s.m_dSlewMin; o.m_dMax = s.m_dSlewMax; o.m_dResolution = s.m_dStepSlewRes; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumScreenSaverDelay: o.m_dMin = PlzShared::SCREEN_SAVER_MIN; o.m_dMax = PlzShared::SCREEN_SAVER_MAX; o.m_dResolution = 1.0; break;
    default: return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("unknown parameter id"));
    }
    return ELoadError();
}

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 *  From here on: a NOT_CONNECTED guard macro + terse store/echo bodies.
 *--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
#define SIM_DEV(var) S_SimInstance* var = dev(l); if (var == nullptr) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_CONNECTED)

/*==== mode ================================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_Mode v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_sState.m_iMode = static_cast<int>(v); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_Mode& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = static_cast<Enum_ELoad_Mode>(d->m_sState.m_iMode); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setCVOption(U32BIT l, U32BIT, bool v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_sState.m_bCVOption = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getCVOption(U32BIT l, U32BIT, bool& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_bCVOption; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getOperatingMode(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode& o)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    if (!d->m_sState.m_bInputOn) { o = Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumUnknown; return ELoadError(); }
    switch (d->m_sState.m_iMode) {
    case 0: o = Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantCurrent; break;
    case 1: o = Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantResistance; break;
    case 2: o = Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantVoltage; break;
    case 3: o = Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumConstantPower; break;
    default: o = Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumUnknown; break;
    }
    return ELoadError();
}

/*==== setpoints ===========================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setCurrent(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, d->m_pLimits->m_dCurrentMax, "current"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; d->m_sState.m_dCurrent = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readbackSetCurrent(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_dCurrent; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setVoltage(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, d->m_pLimits->m_dVoltageMax, "voltage"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; d->m_sState.m_dVoltage = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readbackSetVoltage(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_dVoltage; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setConductance(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); const double hi = (d->m_sState.m_iCRRange == 0) ? d->m_pLimits->m_dCondLowMax : d->m_pLimits->m_dCondHighMax;
  ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, hi, "conductance"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; d->m_sState.m_dConductance = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readbackSetConductance(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_dConductance; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setResistance(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE v)
{ if (v <= 0.0) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("resistance must be positive")); return setConductance(l, c, 1.0 / v); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readbackSetResistance(U32BIT l, U32BIT c, FDOUBLE& o)
{ FDOUBLE g = 0.0; ELoadError e = readbackSetConductance(l, c, g); if (e.isSuccess()) o = (g > 0.0) ? 1.0 / g : 0.0; return e; }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setPower(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, d->m_pLimits->m_dPowerMax, "power"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; d->m_sState.m_dPower = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readbackSetPower(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)

/*==== cutoff ==============================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setCutoff(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t, FDOUBLE v)
{
    SIM_DEV(d); S_SimState& st = d->m_sState;
    switch (t) {
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumVoltage:  { ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, PlzShared::CUTOFF_VOLTAGE_MAX, "cutoff voltage"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; st.m_dCutoffVoltage = v; break; }
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumCapacity: { ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, d->m_pLimits->m_dCutoffCapacityMax, "cutoff capacity"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; st.m_dCutoffCapacity = v; break; }
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumEnergy:   { ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, d->m_pLimits->m_dCutoffEnergyMax, "cutoff energy"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; st.m_dCutoffEnergy = v; break; }
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumTime:     { ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, PlzShared::CUTOFF_TIME_MAX, "cutoff time"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; st.m_dCutoffTime = v; break; }
    }
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getCutoff(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t, FDOUBLE& o)
{
    SIM_DEV(d); const S_SimState& st = d->m_sState;
    switch (t) {
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumVoltage:  o = st.m_dCutoffVoltage; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumCapacity: o = st.m_dCutoffCapacity; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumEnergy:   o = st.m_dCutoffEnergy; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumTime:     o = st.m_dCutoffTime; break;
    }
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::enableCutoff(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t, bool v)
{
    SIM_DEV(d); S_SimState& st = d->m_sState;
    switch (t) {
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumVoltage:  st.m_bCutoffVoltageEnable = v; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumCapacity: st.m_bCutoffCapacityEnable = v; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumEnergy:   st.m_bCutoffEnergyEnable = v; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumTime:     st.m_bCutoffTimeEnable = v; break;
    }
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::isCutoffEnabled(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType t, bool& o)
{
    SIM_DEV(d); const S_SimState& st = d->m_sState;
    switch (t) {
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumVoltage:  o = st.m_bCutoffVoltageEnable; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumCapacity: o = st.m_bCutoffCapacityEnable; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumEnergy:   o = st.m_bCutoffEnergyEnable; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumTime:     o = st.m_bCutoffTimeEnable; break;
    }
    return ELoadError();
}

/*==== measurements ========================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::measureVoltage(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o = d->m_dMeasVolts; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::measureCurrent(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o = d->m_dMeasAmps; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::measurePower(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o = d->m_dMeasWatts; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::measureCapacity(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o = d->m_dCapacityAh; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::measureEnergy(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o = d->m_dEnergyWh; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::measureElapsedTime(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o = d->m_dIntegBaseSeconds; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::fetchVoltage(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_dMeasVolts; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::fetchCurrent(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_dMeasAmps; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::fetchPower(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_dMeasWatts; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::measureData(U32BIT l, U32BIT, QVector<FDOUBLE>& o)
{
    SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o.clear();
    for (int item : d->m_sState.m_vecDataFormat) {
        switch (item) {
        case 0: o << d->m_dMeasAmps; break;
        case 1: o << d->m_dMeasVolts; break;
        case 2: o << d->m_dMeasWatts; break;
        case 3: o << d->m_dIntegBaseSeconds; break;
        case 4: o << d->m_dCapacityAh; break;
        case 5: o << d->m_dEnergyWh; break;
        default: break;
        }
    }
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setSenseAperture(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e = chkRange(v, PlzShared::APERTURE_MIN, PlzShared::APERTURE_MAX, "aperture"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; d->m_sState.m_dSenseAperture = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getSenseAperture(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_dSenseAperture; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setDisplayItem(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_DataItem i, bool v)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    switch (i) {
    case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumCapacity: d->m_sState.m_bDisplayCapacity = v; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumEnergy:   d->m_sState.m_bDisplayEnergy = v; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumETime:    d->m_sState.m_bDisplayElapsedTime = v; break;
    default: return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("display item not settable"));
    }
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getDisplayItem(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_DataItem i, bool& o)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    switch (i) {
    case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumCapacity: o = d->m_sState.m_bDisplayCapacity; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumEnergy:   o = d->m_sState.m_bDisplayEnergy; break;
    case Enum_ELoad_DataItem::m_enumETime:    o = d->m_sState.m_bDisplayElapsedTime; break;
    default: return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("display item not settable"));
    }
    return ELoadError();
}

/*==== data / integration ==================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setBufferSize(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e = chkDiscrete(v, PlzShared::BUFFER_SIZE_VALUES, PlzShared::BUFFER_SIZE_COUNT, "buffer size"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; d->m_sState.m_iBufferSize = static_cast<int>(v); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getBufferSize(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = static_cast<U32BIT>(d->m_sState.m_iBufferSize); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setDataFormat(U32BIT l, U32BIT, const QList<Enum_ELoad_DataItem>& v)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    if (v.isEmpty() || v.size() > 6) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("data format needs 1..6 items"));
    d->m_sState.m_vecDataFormat.clear();
    for (Enum_ELoad_DataItem it : v) d->m_sState.m_vecDataFormat << static_cast<int>(it);
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getDataFormat(U32BIT l, U32BIT, QList<Enum_ELoad_DataItem>& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o.clear(); for (int it : d->m_sState.m_vecDataFormat) o << static_cast<Enum_ELoad_DataItem>(it); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getDataPointCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_bIntegrating ? 1u : 0u; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readDataBuffer(U32BIT l, U32BIT, QVector<FDOUBLE>& o, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o.clear(); o << d->m_dMeasAmps << d->m_dMeasVolts << d->m_dMeasWatts; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setIntegrationGate(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_IntegGate v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_sState.m_iIntegrationGate = static_cast<int>(v); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getIntegrationGate(U32BIT l, U32BIT, Enum_ELoad_IntegGate& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = static_cast<Enum_ELoad_IntegGate>(d->m_sState.m_iIntegrationGate); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setIntegrationAutoReset(U32BIT l, U32BIT, bool v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_sState.m_bIntegrationAutoReset = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getIntegrationAutoReset(U32BIT l, U32BIT, bool& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_bIntegrationAutoReset; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::resetIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_dCapacityAh = d->m_dEnergyWh = d->m_dIntegBaseSeconds = 0.0; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::startIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_bIntegrating = true; d->m_timerIntegration.restart(); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::stopIntegration(U32BIT l, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); if (d->m_bIntegrating && d->m_timerIntegration.isValid()) d->m_dIntegBaseSeconds += d->m_timerIntegration.elapsed() / 1000.0; d->m_bIntegrating = false; return ELoadError(); }

ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setSineAmplitude(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e = chkRange(v, 0.0, d->m_pLimits->m_dCurrentMax, "sine amplitude"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; d->m_sState.m_dSineAmplitude = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getSineAmplitude(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_dSineAmplitude; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setSineFrequency(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    bool ok = (v >= PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_MIN && v <= PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_MAX);
    for (int i = 0; !ok && i < PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_EXTRA_COUNT; ++i) if (std::fabs(v - PlzShared::SINE_FREQ_EXTRA[i]) < 1e-9) ok = true;
    if (!ok) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("sine frequency %1 out of range").arg(v));
    d->m_sState.m_dSineFrequency = v; return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getSineFrequency(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_dSineFrequency; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::startSine(U32BIT l, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_sState.m_bInputOn = true; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::stopSine(U32BIT l, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); return ELoadError(); }

/*==== ARB =================================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setArbCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT v)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    if (v < static_cast<U32BIT>(PlzShared::ARB_POINTS_MIN) || v > static_cast<U32BIT>(PlzShared::ARB_POINTS_MAX))
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB count out of range [3..100]"));
    d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts.resize(static_cast<int>(v));
    d->m_sState.m_vecArbAmps.resize(static_cast<int>(v));
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getArbCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = static_cast<U32BIT>(d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts.size()); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setArbPoint(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT i, FDOUBLE v, FDOUBLE a)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    const int idx = static_cast<int>(i);
    if (idx < 0 || idx >= d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts.size())
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB point index out of range"));
    d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts[idx] = v; d->m_sState.m_vecArbAmps[idx] = a;
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getArbPoint(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT i, FDOUBLE& v, FDOUBLE& a)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    const int idx = static_cast<int>(i);
    if (idx < 0 || idx >= d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts.size())
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB point index out of range"));
    v = d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts.at(idx); a = d->m_sState.m_vecArbAmps.at(idx);
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setArbTable(U32BIT l, U32BIT, const QVector<FDOUBLE>& v, const QVector<FDOUBLE>& a)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    if (v.size() != a.size() || v.size() < PlzShared::ARB_POINTS_MIN || v.size() > PlzShared::ARB_POINTS_MAX)
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("ARB table needs 3..100 matching V/I points"));
    d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts = v; d->m_sState.m_vecArbAmps = a;
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getArbTable(U32BIT l, U32BIT, QVector<FDOUBLE>& v, QVector<FDOUBLE>& a)
{ SIM_DEV(d); v = d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts; a = d->m_sState.m_vecArbAmps; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::applyArb(U32BIT l, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_sState.m_iMode = 4; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::clearArb(U32BIT l, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_sState.m_vecArbVolts.clear(); d->m_sState.m_vecArbAmps.clear(); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setArbResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e = chkDiscrete(v, PlzShared::ARB_RESPONSE_VALUES, PlzShared::ARB_RESPONSE_COUNT, "ARB response"); if (!e.isSuccess()) return e; d->m_sState.m_dArbResponse = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getArbResponse(U32BIT l, U32BIT, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_sState.m_dArbResponse; return ELoadError(); }

/*==== sequence ============================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::createProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n)
{ SIM_DEV(d); if (d->m_mapPrograms.size() >= PlzShared::PROGRAM_COUNT_MAX) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("program limit reached")); d->m_mapPrograms.insert(n, S_SimProgram()); d->m_strSelectedProgram = n; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::selectProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n)
{ SIM_DEV(d); if (!d->m_mapPrograms.contains(n)) return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("no such program")); d->m_strSelectedProgram = n; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getSelectedProgram(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_strSelectedProgram; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::deleteProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_mapPrograms.remove(n); if (d->m_strSelectedProgram == n) d->m_strSelectedProgram.clear(); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::renameProgram(U32BIT l, const QString& n)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    if (d->m_strSelectedProgram.isEmpty() || !d->m_mapPrograms.contains(d->m_strSelectedProgram))
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("no selected program"));
    S_SimProgram p = d->m_mapPrograms.take(d->m_strSelectedProgram);
    d->m_mapPrograms.insert(n, p); d->m_strSelectedProgram = n; return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::saveProgram(U32BIT l)
{ SIM_DEV(d); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::listPrograms(U32BIT l, QStringList& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = d->m_mapPrograms.keys(); return ELoadError(); }

// Returns a pointer to the selected program or nullptr (fills err).
static S_SimProgram* selProg(S_SimInstance* d, ELoadError& err)
{
    if (d->m_strSelectedProgram.isEmpty() || !d->m_mapPrograms.contains(d->m_strSelectedProgram)) {
        err = ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMETER, QStringLiteral("no selected program"));
        return nullptr;
    }
    return &d->m_mapPrograms[d->m_strSelectedProgram];
}
static S_SimStep* selStep(S_SimInstance* d, U32BIT s, ELoadError& err)
{
    S_SimProgram* p = selProg(d, err); if (!p) return nullptr;
    const int idx = static_cast<int>(s);
    if (idx < 0 || idx >= p->m_vecSteps.size()) { err = ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("step index out of range")); return nullptr; }
    return &p->m_vecSteps[idx];
}

ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setProgramLoop(U32BIT l, S32BIT v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimProgram* p = selProg(d, e); if (!p) return e; p->m_iLoop = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getProgramLoop(U32BIT l, S32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimProgram* p = selProg(d, e); if (!p) return e; o = p->m_iLoop; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setProgramStepCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT v)
{
    SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimProgram* p = selProg(d, e); if (!p) return e;
    if (v < PlzShared::PROGRAM_STEPS_MIN || v > PlzShared::PROGRAM_STEPS_MAX)
        return ELoadError(Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE, QStringLiteral("step count out of range"));
    p->m_vecSteps.resize(static_cast<int>(v)); return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getProgramStepCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimProgram* p = selProg(d, e); if (!p) return e; o = static_cast<U32BIT>(p->m_vecSteps.size()); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setProgramProtection(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_Mode, FDOUBLE)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; if (!selProg(d, e)) return e; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setStepLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; st->m_dLevel = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getStepLevel(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; o = st->m_dLevel; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setStepDwell(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e;
  ELoadError r = chkRange(v, PlzShared::STEP_DWELL_MIN, PlzShared::STEP_DWELL_MAX, "step dwell"); if (!r.isSuccess()) return r; st->m_dDwell = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getStepDwell(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, FDOUBLE& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; o = st->m_dDwell; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setStepInput(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, bool v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; st->m_bInput = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getStepInput(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, bool& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; o = st->m_bInput; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setStepTransition(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, Enum_ELoad_Transition v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; st->m_iTransition = static_cast<int>(v); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getStepTransition(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, Enum_ELoad_Transition& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; o = static_cast<Enum_ELoad_Transition>(st->m_iTransition); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setStepTriggerOut(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; st->m_u32TriggerOut = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getStepTriggerOut(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; o = st->m_u32TriggerOut; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::setStepTriggerWait(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT v)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; st->m_u32TriggerWait = v; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getStepTriggerWait(U32BIT l, U32BIT s, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; S_SimStep* st = selStep(d, s, e); if (!st) return e; o = st->m_u32TriggerWait; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::startSequence(U32BIT l)
{ SIM_DEV(d); ELoadError e; if (!selProg(d, e)) return e; d->m_bSeqRunning = true; d->m_bSeqSuspended = false; d->m_u32SeqStep = 0; d->m_u32SeqLoop = 0; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::abortSequence(U32BIT l)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_bSeqRunning = false; d->m_bSeqSuspended = false; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::suspendSequence(U32BIT l)
{ SIM_DEV(d); if (d->m_bSeqRunning) d->m_bSeqSuspended = true; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::resumeSequence(U32BIT l)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_bSeqSuspended = false; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getSequenceState(U32BIT l, Enum_ELoad_SeqState& s, U32BIT& st, U32BIT& lp)
{
    SIM_DEV(d);
    s = !d->m_bSeqRunning ? Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumIdle
        : d->m_bSeqSuspended ? Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumSuspended : Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumRunning;
    st = d->m_u32SeqStep; lp = d->m_u32SeqLoop; return ELoadError();
}

/*==== status / system =====================================================*/
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readErrorStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT, S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus& o)
{
    SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d);
    ELoad_DeviceStatus fl = Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::NoError;
    const U32BIT q = d->m_u32ProtFlags;
    if (q & QUES_OC) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverCurrent;
    if (q & QUES_OP) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::OverPower;
    if (q & QUES_UV) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::UnderVoltage;
    if (q & QUES_CL) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::CurrentLimiting;
    if (q & QUES_PL) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::PowerLimiting;
    if (d->m_sState.m_bInputOn) fl |= Enum_ELoad_DeviceStatusFlag::LoadOn;
    o.m_statusFlags = fl;
    Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode m = Enum_ELoad_OperatingMode::m_enumUnknown;
    getOperatingMode(l, 1, m);
    o.m_EnumMode = m;
    o.m_iQuestionableStatus = static_cast<S32BIT>(q);
    o.m_StrErrorMessage = o.hasError() ? ELoadError::deviceStatusToString(fl) : QStringLiteral("No error");
    return ELoadError();
}
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::clearErrorStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT)
{ SIM_DEV(d); d->m_u32ProtFlags = 0; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::queryErrorQueue(U32BIT l, QString& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = QStringLiteral("0,\"No error\""); return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::getInstrumentErrorCount(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = 0; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readStandardEventStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = 0; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readStatusByte(U32BIT l, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); o = 0; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readQuestionableStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o)
{ SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d); o = d->m_u32ProtFlags; return ELoadError(); }
ELoadError CSimLoadPlugin::readOperationStatus(U32BIT l, U32BIT, U32BIT& o)
{
    SIM_DEV(d); updateSimulation(*d);
    U32BIT oper = 0;
    if (d->m_sState.m_bInputOn) {
        switch (d->m_sState.m_iMode) {
        case 0: oper |= 0x0002; break; case 1: oper |= 0x0004; break;
        case 2: oper |= 0x0001; break; case 3: oper |= 0x0008; break; default: break;
        }
        oper |= 0x0100;   /* LOAD on */
    }
    o = oper; return ELoadError();
}
