-- MockVisa/MockVisa.pro

# =============================================================================
#  MockVisa - a fake VISA library (libvisa) for hardware-free dev/CI.
#  Model plugins link -lvisa; on a real bench this is the vendor VISA instead.
# =============================================================================
QT       -= gui
QT       += core

TEMPLATE  = lib
CONFIG   += plugin c++11        # 'plugin' => clean libvisa.so (no version symlinks)

TARGET    = visa
DESTDIR   = ../lib

INCLUDEPATH += ../include ../Common

HEADERS += \
    CMockPlzEmulator.h

SOURCES += \
    CMockPlzEmulator.cpp \
    MockVisa.cpp

-- MockVisa/CMockPlzEmulator.h

/*=============================================================================
 *  CMockPlzEmulator.h - mini PLZ-5WH2 SCPI emulator inside MockVisa.
 *
 *  DEV-ONLY (never shipped). Emulates enough of a PLZ4005WH2 to exercise
 *  the REAL plugin code path (CPlzDevice over CVisaSession over the VISA C
 *  ABI) without hardware: SCPI parsing, value stores with range checks,
 *  the SYST:ERR? queue semantics, NR3 responses, measurements.
 *
 *  The command tables reuse the same S_PlzLimits rows as the plugins, so
 *  range behaviour matches the real instrument description.
 *===========================================================================*/
#ifndef CMOCKPLZEMULATOR_H
#define CMOCKPLZEMULATOR_H

#include <QByteArray>
#include <QMap>
#include <QPair>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>
#include <QVector>

#include "S_PlzLimits.h"

class CMockPlzEmulator
{
public:
    explicit CMockPlzEmulator(const QString& in_strModelName);

    /* Feeds one complete SCPI line; the response (if any, LF-terminated)
     * is appended to out_abyResponse.                                       */
    void HandleLine(const QByteArray& in_abyLine, QByteArray& out_abyResponse);

private:
    struct S_NumericParam {
        double m_dValue;
        double m_dMin;
        double m_dMax;
        double m_dDefault;
        S_NumericParam() : m_dValue(0.0), m_dMin(0.0), m_dMax(0.0), m_dDefault(0.0) {}
        S_NumericParam(double in_dMin, double in_dMax, double in_dDefault)
            : m_dValue(in_dDefault), m_dMin(in_dMin), m_dMax(in_dMax)
            , m_dDefault(in_dDefault) {}
    };
    struct S_EnumParam {
        QString     m_strValue;
        QStringList m_lstAllowed;
        QString     m_strDefault;
        S_EnumParam() {}
        S_EnumParam(const QStringList& in_lstAllowed, const QString& in_strDefault)
            : m_strValue(in_strDefault), m_lstAllowed(in_lstAllowed)
            , m_strDefault(in_strDefault) {}
    };
    struct S_BoolParam {
        bool m_bValue;
        bool m_bDefault;
        S_BoolParam() : m_bValue(false), m_bDefault(false) {}
        explicit S_BoolParam(bool in_bDefault)
            : m_bValue(in_bDefault), m_bDefault(in_bDefault) {}
    };

    void registerCommands();
    void resetValues();
    void pushError(int in_iCode, const char* in_szMessage);
    static QByteArray formatNr3(double in_dValue);
    bool parseNumeric(const QString& in_strArg, const S_NumericParam& in_sParam,
                      double& out_dValue);

    /* handlers                                                             */
    bool handleCommon(const QString& in_strHeader, const QString& in_strArgs,
                      QByteArray& out_abyResponse);
    bool handleMeasurement(const QString& in_strHeader, QByteArray& out_abyResponse);
    bool handleGeneric(const QString& in_strHeader, const QString& in_strArgs,
                       QByteArray& out_abyResponse);

    void computeMeasurement(double& out_dVolts, double& out_dAmps, double& out_dWatts);

    QString                       m_strModelName;
    const S_PlzLimits*            m_psLimits;
    QMap<QString, S_NumericParam> m_mapNumeric;   /* header -> value/range   */
    QMap<QString, S_EnumParam>    m_mapEnum;
    QMap<QString, S_BoolParam>    m_mapBool;
    QList<QPair<int, QByteArray> > m_lstErrors;   /* SYST:ERR? queue         */
};

#endif /* CMOCKPLZEMULATOR_H */

-- MockVisa/CMockPlzEmulator.cpp

#include "CMockPlzEmulator.h"

#include <cstdio>

CMockPlzEmulator::CMockPlzEmulator(const QString& in_strModelName)
    : m_strModelName(in_strModelName)
{
    m_psLimits = PlzFindLimits(in_strModelName.toUtf8().constData());
    if (m_psLimits == NULL) {
        m_psLimits = PlzFindLimits("PLZ4005WH2");
    }
    registerCommands();
    resetValues();
}

void CMockPlzEmulator::pushError(int in_iCode, const char* in_szMessage)
{
    if (m_lstErrors.size() < 16) {
        m_lstErrors.append(qMakePair(in_iCode, QByteArray(in_szMessage)));
    }
}

QByteArray CMockPlzEmulator::formatNr3(double in_dValue)
{
    char szBuffer[40];
    std::snprintf(szBuffer, sizeof(szBuffer), "%+.5E", in_dValue);
    return QByteArray(szBuffer);
}

void CMockPlzEmulator::registerCommands()
{
    const S_PlzLimits& s = *m_psLimits;

    /* numeric parameters: header -> {min, max, default}                    */
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR",               S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dCurrentMax, 0.0));
    /* CV setpoints accept the operating range (up to the rating); the extra
     * headroom of the 101 % setting range is only valid for other uses, so
     * a setpoint above the rating is rejected by the instrument. This gives
     * the test suite a value the local range check passes but SYST:ERR?
     * flags - exercising the after-set error policy.                        */
    m_mapNumeric.insert("VOLT",               S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dVoltageRating, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("COND",               S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dCondHighMax, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("POW",                S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dPowerMax, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR:SLEW",          S_NumericParam(s.m_dSlewMin, s.m_dSlewMax,
                                                             s.m_dSlewMin));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR:SST",           S_NumericParam(0.0, 0.1, 0.0005));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR:PROT",          S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dOcpMax, s.m_dOcpMax));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("POW:PROT",           S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dOppMax, s.m_dOppMax));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("VOLT:PROT:LOW",      S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dUvpMax, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("INP:PROT:WDOG:DEL",  S_NumericParam(1.0, 3600.0, 60.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("INP:CUTO:VOLT",      S_NumericParam(0.0, 1000.0, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("INP:CUTO:CAP",       S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dCutoffCapacityMax, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("INP:CUTO:ENER",      S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dCutoffEnergyMax, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("INP:CUTO:ETIM",      S_NumericParam(0.0, 3600000.0, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR:PULS:LEV",      S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dCurrentMax, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("COND:PULS:LEV",      S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dCondHighMax, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR:PULS:FREQ",     S_NumericParam(1.0, 10000.0, 1.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR:PULS:DCYC",     S_NumericParam(5.0, 95.0, 50.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR:SINE:AMPL",     S_NumericParam(0.0, s.m_dCurrentMax, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("CURR:SINE:FREQ",     S_NumericParam(1.0, 10000.0, 1.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("ARB:RESP",           S_NumericParam(0.0, 0.1, 0.0005));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("SENS:APER",          S_NumericParam(0.00001, 1.0, 0.1));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("TRIG:ACQ:COUN",      S_NumericParam(1.0, 65536.0, 1.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("TRIG:ACQ:DEL",       S_NumericParam(0.0, 100.0, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("TRIG:ACQ:INT:TIME",  S_NumericParam(0.00001, 3600.0, 0.1));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("TRIG:TRAN:DEL",      S_NumericParam(0.0, 100.0, 0.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("SYST:KLOC:LEV",      S_NumericParam(1.0, 3.0, 3.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("SYST:SSAV:DEL",      S_NumericParam(60.0, 59940.0, 60.0));
    m_mapNumeric.insert("DATA:BSIZ",          S_NumericParam(1024.0, 65536.0, 65536.0));

    /* enum parameters (device answers in short form)                       */
    m_mapEnum.insert("FUNC",           S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "CC" << "CR" << "CV" << "CP" << "ARB", "CC"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("COND:RANG",      S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "LOW" << "HIGH", "HIGH"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("VOLT:RESP",      S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "NORM" << "FAST", "NORM"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("COND:RESP",      S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "NORM" << "FAST", "NORM"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("VOLT:PROT:ACT",  S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "LIM" << "TRIP", "LIM"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("INP:PON:STAT",   S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "RST" << "RCL0" << "AUTO", "AUTO"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("INP:EXT:LOG",    S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "POS" << "NEG", "POS"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("TRIG:ACQ:SOUR",  S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "IMM" << "BUS" << "DIGITAL2" << "TAL" << "MSYN" << "LOADOFF",
                     "IMM"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("TRIG:TRAN:SOUR", S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "IMM" << "BUS" << "DIGITAL2" << "MSYN", "IMM"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("SYST:COMM:RLST", S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "LOC" << "REM" << "RWL", "LOC"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("SYST:CONF:DIGITAL2:DIR", S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "INP" << "OUTP", "INP"));
    m_mapEnum.insert("DATA:INT:GATE",  S_EnumParam(QStringList()
                     << "NONE" << "LOAD_ON" << "PROG_RUN", "LOAD_ON"));

    /* boolean parameters                                                   */
    m_mapBool.insert("INP",                 S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("FUNC:CVOP",           S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("CURR:PROT:STAT",      S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("POW:PROT:STAT",       S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("VOLT:PROT:STAT",      S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("INP:PROT:WDOG",       S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("INP:CUTO:VOLT:STAT",  S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("INP:CUTO:CAP:STAT",   S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("INP:CUTO:ENER:STAT",  S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("INP:CUTO:ETIM:STAT",  S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("INP:MSYN:ACC",        S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("CURR:EXT:ACON",       S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("CURR:EXT:FCON",       S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("COND:EXT:FCON",       S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("VOLT:EXT:FCON",       S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("POW:EXT:FCON",        S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("TRIG:ACQ:INT:STAT",   S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("DATA:INT:GATE:ARES",  S_BoolParam(true));
    m_mapBool.insert("DISP:CAP",            S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("DISP:ENER",           S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("DISP:ETIM",           S_BoolParam(true));
    m_mapBool.insert("SYST:KLOC",           S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("SYST:BEEP",           S_BoolParam(true));
    m_mapBool.insert("SYST:BEEP:KEY",       S_BoolParam(true));
    m_mapBool.insert("SYST:BEEP:PROT",      S_BoolParam(true));
    m_mapBool.insert("SYST:BEEP:SCPI",      S_BoolParam(true));
    m_mapBool.insert("SYST:CONF:RSEN",      S_BoolParam(false));
    m_mapBool.insert("SYST:SSAV",           S_BoolParam(false));
}

void CMockPlzEmulator::resetValues()
{
    for (QMap<QString, S_NumericParam>::iterator it = m_mapNumeric.begin();
         it != m_mapNumeric.end(); ++it) {
        it.value().m_dValue = it.value().m_dDefault;
    }
    for (QMap<QString, S_EnumParam>::iterator it = m_mapEnum.begin();
         it != m_mapEnum.end(); ++it) {
        it.value().m_strValue = it.value().m_strDefault;
    }
    for (QMap<QString, S_BoolParam>::iterator it = m_mapBool.begin();
         it != m_mapBool.end(); ++it) {
        it.value().m_bValue = it.value().m_bDefault;
    }
}

bool CMockPlzEmulator::parseNumeric(const QString& in_strArg,
                                    const S_NumericParam& in_sParam,
                                    double& out_dValue)
{
    const QString strArg = in_strArg.trimmed().toUpper();
    if (strArg.startsWith(QStringLiteral("MIN"))) {
        out_dValue = in_sParam.m_dMin;
        return true;
    }
    if (strArg.startsWith(QStringLiteral("MAX"))) {
        out_dValue = in_sParam.m_dMax;
        return true;
    }
    bool bOk = false;
    out_dValue = strArg.toDouble(&bOk);
    return bOk;
}

void CMockPlzEmulator::computeMeasurement(double& out_dVolts, double& out_dAmps,
                                          double& out_dWatts)
{
    const bool   bInputOn = m_mapBool.value("INP").m_bValue;
    const double dSource  = 50.0;
    out_dVolts = dSource;
    out_dAmps  = 0.0;
    if (bInputOn) {
        const QString strMode = m_mapEnum.value("FUNC").m_strValue;
        if (strMode == QStringLiteral("CC")) {
            out_dAmps = qMin(m_mapNumeric.value("CURR").m_dValue,
                             m_psLimits->m_dCurrentRating);
        } else if (strMode == QStringLiteral("CR")) {
            out_dAmps = qMin(dSource * m_mapNumeric.value("COND").m_dValue,
                             m_psLimits->m_dCurrentRating);
        } else if (strMode == QStringLiteral("CV")) {
            out_dVolts = qMin(m_mapNumeric.value("VOLT").m_dValue, dSource);
            out_dAmps  = qMin((dSource - out_dVolts) / 0.01,
                              m_psLimits->m_dCurrentRating);
        } else if (strMode == QStringLiteral("CP")) {
            out_dAmps = qMin(m_mapNumeric.value("POW").m_dValue / dSource,
                             m_psLimits->m_dCurrentRating);
        }
    }
    out_dWatts = out_dVolts * out_dAmps;
}

bool CMockPlzEmulator::handleCommon(const QString& in_strHeader,
                                    const QString& in_strArgs,
                                    QByteArray& out_abyResponse)
{
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*IDN?")) {
        out_abyResponse += QStringLiteral("KIKUSUI,%1,MOCK0001,IFO1.00 FPGA1.00 IOC1.00\n")
                .arg(m_strModelName).toLatin1();
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*RST")) {
        resetValues();
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*CLS")) {
        m_lstErrors.clear();
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*OPC?")) {
        out_abyResponse += "1\n";
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*OPC") ||
        in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*WAI") ||
        in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*TRG")) {
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*TST?")) {
        out_abyResponse += "0\n";
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*OPT?")) {
        out_abyResponse += "0\n";
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*STB?") ||
        in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*ESR?")) {
        out_abyResponse += "0\n";
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*SAV") ||
        in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("*RCL")) {
        bool bOk = false;
        const int iSlot = in_strArgs.trimmed().toInt(&bOk);
        if (!bOk || iSlot < 0 || iSlot > 19) {
            pushError(-222, "Data out of range");
        }
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("SYST:ERR?")) {
        if (m_lstErrors.isEmpty()) {
            out_abyResponse += "+0,\"No error.\"\n";
        } else {
            const QPair<int, QByteArray> sError = m_lstErrors.takeFirst();
            out_abyResponse += QByteArray::number(sError.first) + ",\"" +
                    sError.second + "\"\n";
        }
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("SYST:ERR:COUN?")) {
        out_abyResponse += QByteArray::number(m_lstErrors.size()) + "\n";
        return true;
    }
    if (in_strHeader == QStringLiteral("SYST:VERS?")) {
        out_abyResponse += "1999.0\n";
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

bool CMockPlzEmulator::handleMeasurement(const QString& in_strHeader,
                                         QByteArray& out_abyResponse)
{
    QString strHeader = in_strHeader;
    if (!strHeader.startsWith(QStringLiteral("MEAS:")) &&
        !strHeader.startsWith(QStringLiteral("READ:")) &&
        !strHeader.startsWith(QStringLiteral("FETC:"))) {
        return false;
    }
    strHeader = strHeader.mid(5);

    double dVolts = 0.0, dAmps = 0.0, dWatts = 0.0;
    computeMeasurement(dVolts, dAmps, dWatts);

    if (strHeader == QStringLiteral("VOLT?")) {
        out_abyResponse += formatNr3(dVolts) + "\n";
    } else if (strHeader == QStringLiteral("CURR?")) {
        out_abyResponse += formatNr3(dAmps) + "\n";
    } else if (strHeader == QStringLiteral("POW?")) {
        out_abyResponse += formatNr3(dWatts) + "\n";
    } else if (strHeader == QStringLiteral("CAP?") ||
               strHeader == QStringLiteral("ENER?") ||
               strHeader == QStringLiteral("ETIM?")) {
        out_abyResponse += formatNr3(0.0) + "\n";
    } else {
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool CMockPlzEmulator::handleGeneric(const QString& in_strHeader,
                                     const QString& in_strArgs,
                                     QByteArray& out_abyResponse)
{
    const bool bQuery = in_strHeader.endsWith(QLatin1Char('?'));
    const QString strKey = bQuery ? in_strHeader.left(in_strHeader.size() - 1)
                                  : in_strHeader;

    if (m_mapNumeric.contains(strKey)) {
        S_NumericParam& sParam = m_mapNumeric[strKey];
        if (bQuery) {
            if (in_strArgs.trimmed().compare(QStringLiteral("MIN"),
                                             Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0) {
                out_abyResponse += formatNr3(sParam.m_dMin) + "\n";
            } else if (in_strArgs.trimmed().compare(QStringLiteral("MAX"),
                                                    Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0) {
                out_abyResponse += formatNr3(sParam.m_dMax) + "\n";
            } else {
                out_abyResponse += formatNr3(sParam.m_dValue) + "\n";
            }
            return true;
        }
        double dValue = 0.0;
        if (!parseNumeric(in_strArgs, sParam, dValue)) {
            pushError(-104, "Data type error");
            return true;
        }
        if (dValue < sParam.m_dMin || dValue > sParam.m_dMax) {
            pushError(-222, "Data out of range");
            return true;
        }
        sParam.m_dValue = dValue;
        return true;
    }

    if (m_mapEnum.contains(strKey)) {
        S_EnumParam& sParam = m_mapEnum[strKey];
        if (bQuery) {
            out_abyResponse += sParam.m_strValue.toLatin1() + "\n";
            return true;
        }
        const QString strValue = in_strArgs.trimmed().toUpper();
        for (int i = 0; i < sParam.m_lstAllowed.size(); ++i) {
            if (strValue.startsWith(sParam.m_lstAllowed.at(i))) {
                sParam.m_strValue = sParam.m_lstAllowed.at(i);
                return true;
            }
        }
        pushError(-224, "Illegal parameter value");
        return true;
    }

    if (m_mapBool.contains(strKey)) {
        S_BoolParam& sParam = m_mapBool[strKey];
        if (bQuery) {
            out_abyResponse += sParam.m_bValue ? "1\n" : "0\n";
            return true;
        }
        const QString strValue = in_strArgs.trimmed().toUpper();
        /* INP accepts an optional timed-on second argument                 */
        const QString strFirst = strValue.section(QLatin1Char(','), 0, 0).trimmed();
        if (strFirst == QStringLiteral("ON") || strFirst == QStringLiteral("1")) {
            sParam.m_bValue = true;
        } else if (strFirst == QStringLiteral("OFF") || strFirst == QStringLiteral("0")) {
            sParam.m_bValue = false;
        } else {
            pushError(-224, "Illegal parameter value");
        }
        return true;
    }

    return false;
}

void CMockPlzEmulator::HandleLine(const QByteArray& in_abyLine,
                                  QByteArray& out_abyResponse)
{
    const QString strLine = QString::fromLatin1(in_abyLine).trimmed();
    if (strLine.isEmpty()) {
        return;
    }

    const int iSpace = strLine.indexOf(QLatin1Char(' '));
    const QString strHeader = (iSpace < 0 ? strLine : strLine.left(iSpace)).toUpper();
    const QString strArgs   = (iSpace < 0 ? QString() : strLine.mid(iSpace + 1));

    if (handleCommon(strHeader, strArgs, out_abyResponse)) {
        return;
    }
    if (handleMeasurement(strHeader, out_abyResponse)) {
        return;
    }
    if (handleGeneric(strHeader, strArgs, out_abyResponse)) {
        return;
    }

    pushError(-113, "Undefined header");
    if (strHeader.endsWith(QLatin1Char('?'))) {
        /* a query the emulator does not know: no response -> caller times
         * out, exactly like a real instrument that rejected the query      */
    }
}

-- MockVisa/MockVisa.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  MockVisa.cpp - a fake VISA implementation (built as libvisa) for
 *  hardware-free development and CI.
 *
 *  Exports the standard viXxx symbols declared in <visa.h> so the model
 *  plugins, which link -lvisa exactly like against a real VISA, run with no
 *  instrument attached. Each opened session is backed by a mini PLZ-5WH2
 *  SCPI emulator (CMockPlzEmulator). The model to emulate is taken from the
 *  resource string (the first PLZxxxxWH2 token); a resource containing
 *  "TIMEOUT" never answers a read.
 *==========================================================================*/
#include <QByteArray>
#include <QMap>
#include <QMutex>
#include <QMutexLocker>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>
#include <cstring>

#include "visa.h"
#include "CMockPlzEmulator.h"

#if defined(_WIN32)
#  define MOCKVISA_EXPORT __declspec(dllexport)
#else
#  define MOCKVISA_EXPORT __attribute__((visibility("default")))
#endif

namespace {

const int READ_CAP_BYTES = 1024;

enum ESessionKind { KIND_RM, KIND_INSTR, KIND_FIND };

struct S_Session {
    ESessionKind      m_eKind;
    QString           m_strResource;
    CMockPlzEmulator* m_pEmulator;
    QByteArray        m_abyRx;
    bool              m_bTermcharEn;
    bool              m_bTimeout;
    QStringList       m_lstFind;
    int               m_iFindIdx;

    S_Session()
        : m_eKind(KIND_RM), m_pEmulator(nullptr)
        , m_bTermcharEn(true), m_bTimeout(false), m_iFindIdx(0) {}
    ~S_Session() { delete m_pEmulator; }
};

QMutex g_mtx;
QMap<ViSession, S_Session*> g_sessions;
ViSession g_next = 1000;

QString modelFromResource(const QString& in_strResource)
{
    QRegularExpression re(QStringLiteral("PLZ\\d+WH2"));
    const QRegularExpressionMatch m = re.match(in_strResource);
    return m.hasMatch() ? m.captured(0) : QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2");
}

QStringList mockResourceList()
{
    return QStringList()
            << QStringLiteral("MOCK0::PLZ4005WH2::INSTR")
            << QStringLiteral("MOCK0::PLZ2005WH2::INSTR")
            << QStringLiteral("MOCK0::TIMEOUT::INSTR");
}

S_Session* find(ViSession vi) { return g_sessions.value(vi, nullptr); }

} // namespace

extern "C" {

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viOpenDefaultRM(ViPSession vi)
{
    if (vi == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    S_Session* s = new S_Session();
    s->m_eKind = KIND_RM;
    *vi = g_next++;
    g_sessions.insert(*vi, s);
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viOpen(ViSession sesn, const ViChar* name, ViAccessMode,
                                ViUInt32, ViPSession vi)
{
    if (vi == nullptr || name == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    if (find(sesn) == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;

    const QString strResource = QString::fromLatin1(name);
    S_Session* s = new S_Session();
    s->m_eKind = KIND_INSTR;
    s->m_strResource = strResource;
    if (strResource.contains(QStringLiteral("TIMEOUT"), Qt::CaseInsensitive)) {
        s->m_bTimeout = true;
    } else {
        s->m_pEmulator = new CMockPlzEmulator(modelFromResource(strResource));
    }
    *vi = g_next++;
    g_sessions.insert(*vi, s);
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viClose(ViObject vi)
{
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    S_Session* s = g_sessions.take(static_cast<ViSession>(vi));
    if (s == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    delete s;
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viWrite(ViSession vi, ViConstBuf buf, ViUInt32 cnt, ViPUInt32 retCnt)
{
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    S_Session* s = find(vi);
    if (s == nullptr || s->m_eKind != KIND_INSTR) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    if (retCnt) *retCnt = cnt;
    if (s->m_bTimeout || s->m_pEmulator == nullptr) return VI_SUCCESS;

    const QByteArray aby(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(buf), static_cast<int>(cnt));
    const QList<QByteArray> lines = aby.split('\n');
    for (const QByteArray& line : lines) {
        if (!line.trimmed().isEmpty()) {
            s->m_pEmulator->HandleLine(line, s->m_abyRx);
        }
    }
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viRead(ViSession vi, ViBuf buf, ViUInt32 cnt, ViPUInt32 retCnt)
{
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    S_Session* s = find(vi);
    if (s == nullptr || s->m_eKind != KIND_INSTR) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    if (retCnt) *retCnt = 0;
    if (s->m_bTimeout || s->m_abyRx.isEmpty()) return VI_ERROR_TMO;

    int iToCopy = qMin(static_cast<int>(cnt), qMin(READ_CAP_BYTES, s->m_abyRx.size()));
    ViStatus status;
    if (s->m_bTermcharEn) {
        const int iTerm = s->m_abyRx.indexOf('\n');
        if (iTerm >= 0 && iTerm < iToCopy) {
            iToCopy = iTerm + 1;
            status = VI_SUCCESS_TERM_CHAR;
        } else {
            status = (s->m_abyRx.size() > iToCopy) ? VI_SUCCESS_MAX_CNT : VI_SUCCESS;
        }
    } else {
        status = (s->m_abyRx.size() > iToCopy) ? VI_SUCCESS_MAX_CNT : VI_SUCCESS;
    }
    memcpy(buf, s->m_abyRx.constData(), static_cast<size_t>(iToCopy));
    s->m_abyRx.remove(0, iToCopy);
    if (retCnt) *retCnt = static_cast<ViUInt32>(iToCopy);
    return status;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viSetAttribute(ViObject vi, ViAttr attrName, ViAttrState attrValue)
{
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    S_Session* s = find(static_cast<ViSession>(vi));
    if (s == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    if (attrName == VI_ATTR_TERMCHAR_EN) s->m_bTermcharEn = (attrValue != 0);
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viGetAttribute(ViObject vi, ViAttr attrName, void* attrValue)
{
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    S_Session* s = find(static_cast<ViSession>(vi));
    if (s == nullptr || attrValue == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    if (attrName == VI_ATTR_TERMCHAR_EN) {
        *static_cast<ViUInt32*>(attrValue) = s->m_bTermcharEn ? 1u : 0u;
        return VI_SUCCESS;
    }
    return VI_ERROR_NSUP_ATTR;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viFindRsrc(ViSession sesn, const ViChar*, ViFindList* findList,
                                    ViPUInt32 retcnt, ViChar desc[])
{
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    if (find(sesn) == nullptr || findList == nullptr || retcnt == nullptr || desc == nullptr)
        return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    const QStringList lst = mockResourceList();
    S_Session* s = new S_Session();
    s->m_eKind = KIND_FIND;
    s->m_lstFind = lst;
    s->m_iFindIdx = 1;
    const ViSession fl = g_next++;
    g_sessions.insert(fl, s);
    *findList = fl;
    *retcnt = static_cast<ViUInt32>(lst.size());
    qstrncpy(desc, lst.first().toLatin1().constData(), 256);
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viFindNext(ViFindList findList, ViChar desc[])
{
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    S_Session* s = find(static_cast<ViSession>(findList));
    if (s == nullptr || s->m_eKind != KIND_FIND || desc == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    if (s->m_iFindIdx >= s->m_lstFind.size()) return VI_ERROR_RSRC_NFOUND;
    qstrncpy(desc, s->m_lstFind.at(s->m_iFindIdx).toLatin1().constData(), 256);
    ++s->m_iFindIdx;
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viStatusDesc(ViObject, ViStatus status, ViChar desc[])
{
    if (desc == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    const char* szText = "(MockVisa status)";
    switch (status) {
    case VI_ERROR_TMO:           szText = "Timeout expired (MockVisa)";       break;
    case VI_ERROR_RSRC_NFOUND:   szText = "Resource not found (MockVisa)";    break;
    case VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT:    szText = "Invalid object (MockVisa)";        break;
    case VI_ERROR_INV_RSRC_NAME: szText = "Invalid resource name (MockVisa)"; break;
    default: break;
    }
    qstrncpy(desc, szText, 256);
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

MOCKVISA_EXPORT ViStatus viClear(ViSession vi)
{
    QMutexLocker lock(&g_mtx);
    S_Session* s = find(vi);
    if (s == nullptr) return VI_ERROR_INV_OBJECT;
    s->m_abyRx.clear();
    return VI_SUCCESS;
}

} // extern "C"

-- AutoTest/AutoTest.pro

# =============================================================================
#  AutoTest - console end-to-end test suite for the ELoad framework.
#  Links the deliverable core only; runs hardware-free (MockVisa + SimLoad).
# =============================================================================
QT       -= gui
QT       += core

TARGET    = AutoTest
TEMPLATE  = app
CONFIG   += console c++11
CONFIG   -= app_bundle

DESTDIR   = $$OUT_PWD/../bin

INCLUDEPATH += ../include

LIBS += -L$$OUT_PWD/../lib -lELoadCore
unix: QMAKE_RPATHDIR += $$OUT_PWD/../lib

SOURCES += main.cpp

-- AutoTest/main.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  AutoTest - console end-to-end test suite for the re-architected ELoad
 *  framework (CELoadManager + QPluginLoader plugins).
 *
 *  Uses only the public deliverables (include/ + ELoadCore lib + plugin DLLs)
 *  and runs with zero hardware: the real PLZ plugins talk to MockVisa
 *  (libvisa), SimLoad is fully virtual. Exit code = number of failures.
 *
 *  Usage: AutoTest [pluginDir]
 *==========================================================================*/
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDir>
#include <QTextStream>

#include "ELoadManager.h"

static int g_checks = 0, g_fail = 0;
static QTextStream& out() { static QTextStream s(stdout); return s; }

#define CHECK(cond) do { ++g_checks; if (!(cond)) { ++g_fail; \
    out() << "  FAIL " << __LINE__ << "  " #cond << "\n"; } } while (0)

static void section(const char* t) { out() << "\n=== " << t << " ===\n"; out().flush(); }

static const char* PLZ_RES  = "MOCK0::PLZ4005WH2::INSTR";
static const char* TMO_RES  = "MOCK0::TIMEOUT::INSTR";

static S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig cfgFor(const char* res)
{ S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig c; c.setResourceString(QString::fromLatin1(res)); c.m_u32Timeout = 800; return c; }

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
static void testDiscovery(CELoadManager& mgr)
{
    section("discovery");
    const QStringList a = mgr.getAvailablePlugins();
    out() << "  plugins: " << a.join(", ") << "\n";
    CHECK(a.contains("PLZ1005WH2"));
    CHECK(a.contains("PLZ2005WH2"));
    CHECK(a.contains("PLZ4005WH2"));
    CHECK(a.contains("PLZ12005WH2"));
    CHECK(a.contains("PLZ20005WH2"));
    CHECK(a.contains("SimLoad"));

    const S_ELoad_PluginInfo si = mgr.getPluginInfoByName("PLZ4005WH2");
    CHECK(QString::fromLatin1(si.m_szManufacturer).contains("Kikusui"));
    CHECK(QString::fromLatin1(si.m_szSeries) == "PLZ-5WH2");
    const S_ELoad_Capabilities caps = mgr.getPluginCapabilities("PLZ4005WH2");
    CHECK(caps.m_dMaxCurrent == 80.0);
    CHECK(caps.m_bHasOCP && caps.m_bHasOPP && caps.m_bHasUVP);
    CHECK(!caps.m_bHasOVP);                       // fixed hardware OVP
}

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
static void testInstances(CELoadManager& mgr)
{
    section("instance management");
    CHECK(mgr.createInstance(10, "PLZ4005WH2").isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.instanceExists(10));
    CHECK(mgr.getInstancePlugin(10) == "PLZ4005WH2");
    // duplicate load number rejected
    CHECK(mgr.createInstance(10, "SimLoad").code() == Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::ALREADY_CONNECTED
          || !mgr.createInstance(10, "SimLoad").isSuccess());
    // unknown plugin name rejected
    CHECK(!mgr.createInstance(11, "NoSuchModel").isSuccess());
    // op on non-existent load rejected
    FDOUBLE d = 0.0;
    CHECK(mgr.measureVoltage(999, 1, d).code() == Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::INVALID_LOAD_NUMBER);
    CHECK(mgr.destroyInstance(10).isSuccess());
    CHECK(!mgr.instanceExists(10));
}

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
static void testPlzEndToEnd(CELoadManager& mgr)
{
    section("PLZ4005WH2 end-to-end (MockVisa)");
    CHECK(mgr.createInstance(1, "PLZ4005WH2").isSuccess());
    ELoadError e = mgr.connect(1, cfgFor(PLZ_RES));
    CHECK(e.isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.isConnected(1));

    QString idn;
    CHECK(mgr.getIdentification(1, idn).isSuccess() && idn.contains("PLZ4005WH2"));

    CHECK(mgr.setMode(1, 1, Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC).isSuccess());
    Enum_ELoad_Mode m = Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumUnknown;
    CHECK(mgr.getMode(1, 1, m).isSuccess() && m == Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC);

    CHECK(mgr.setCurrent(1, 1, 12.5).isSuccess());
    FDOUBLE rb = 0.0;
    CHECK(mgr.readbackSetCurrent(1, 1, rb).isSuccess() && qAbs(rb - 12.5) < 1e-6);
    // local range rejection (CC max 80.8 A)
    CHECK(mgr.setCurrent(1, 1, 200.0).code() == Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE);

    CHECK(mgr.setPower(1, 1, 1000.0).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.setConductance(1, 1, 1.0).isSuccess());

    FDOUBLE v = 0.0;
    CHECK(mgr.measureVoltage(1, 1, v).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.enableInput(1, 1, true).isSuccess());
    bool on = false;
    CHECK(mgr.isInputEnabled(1, 1, on).isSuccess() && on);

    // protections + cutoff round-trips
    CHECK(mgr.setOCP(1, 1, 50.0).isSuccess());
    FDOUBLE ocp = 0.0;
    CHECK(mgr.readOCP(1, 1, ocp).isSuccess() && qAbs(ocp - 50.0) < 1e-6);
    CHECK(mgr.setCutoff(1, 1, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumVoltage, 5.0).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.enableCutoff(1, 1, Enum_ELoad_CutoffType::m_enumVoltage, true).isSuccess());

    // NOT_SUPPORTED: PLZ has fixed hardware OVP
    CHECK(mgr.setOVP(1, 1, 100.0).code() == Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::NOT_SUPPORTED);

    // parameter range from the model's limits
    S_ELoad_ParameterRange rng;
    CHECK(mgr.getParameterRange(1, 1, Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCurrent, rng).isSuccess()
          && qAbs(rng.m_dMax - 80.8) < 1e-6);

    CHECK(mgr.reset(1).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.disconnect(1).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.destroyInstance(1).isSuccess());
}

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
static void testWrongModelAndTimeout(CELoadManager& mgr)
{
    section("wrong-model + timeout paths");
    // a PLZ2005WH2 plugin pointed at a PLZ4005WH2 instrument must reject on *IDN?
    CHECK(mgr.createInstance(2, "PLZ2005WH2").isSuccess());
    ELoadError e = mgr.connect(2, cfgFor(PLZ_RES));
    CHECK(!e.isSuccess());
    CHECK(!mgr.isConnected(2));
    mgr.destroyInstance(2);

    // a timeout resource: *IDN? verification times out, connect fails
    CHECK(mgr.createInstance(3, "PLZ4005WH2").isSuccess());
    e = mgr.connect(3, cfgFor(TMO_RES));
    CHECK(e.code() == Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::COMMUNICATION_TIMEOUT || !e.isSuccess());
    mgr.destroyInstance(3);
}

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
static void testSimLoadFull(CELoadManager& mgr)
{
    section("SimLoad full API (virtual)");
    CHECK(mgr.createInstance(4, "SimLoad").isSuccess());
    S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig c; c.setResourceString("SIM::PLZ4005WH2");
    CHECK(mgr.connect(4, c).isSuccess());

    CHECK(mgr.setMode(4, 1, Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.setCurrent(4, 1, 10.0).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.setCurrent(4, 1, 999.0).code() == Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE);
    CHECK(mgr.enableInput(4, 1, true).isSuccess());

    FDOUBLE vi = 0.0, vv = 0.0, vp = 0.0;
    mgr.measureCurrent(4, 1, vi); mgr.measureVoltage(4, 1, vv); mgr.measurePower(4, 1, vp);
    CHECK(qAbs(vi - 10.0) < 1e-6 && vv > 0.0 && qAbs(vp - vv * vi) < 1e-3);

    // sequence
    CHECK(mgr.createProgram(4, "test").isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.setProgramStepCount(4, 3).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.setStepLevel(4, 0, 5.0).isSuccess());
    FDOUBLE sl = 0.0;
    CHECK(mgr.getStepLevel(4, 0, sl).isSuccess() && qAbs(sl - 5.0) < 1e-6);
    QStringList progs;
    CHECK(mgr.listPrograms(4, progs).isSuccess() && progs.contains("test"));

    // ARB
    QVector<FDOUBLE> av { 0.0, 25.0, 50.0 }, aa { 0.0, 5.0, 10.0 };
    CHECK(mgr.setArbTable(4, 1, av, aa).isSuccess());
    QVector<FDOUBLE> gv, ga;
    CHECK(mgr.getArbTable(4, 1, gv, ga).isSuccess() && gv.size() == 3 && qAbs(ga.at(2) - 10.0) < 1e-6);

    // memories
    CHECK(mgr.savePreset(4, 1).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.savePreset(4, 9).code() == Enum_ELoad_ErrorCode::PARAMETER_OUT_OF_RANGE);
    CHECK(mgr.recallPreset(4, 1).isSuccess());

    // status
    S_ELoad_DeviceErrorStatus st;
    CHECK(mgr.readErrorStatus(4, 1, st).isSuccess());

    CHECK(mgr.disconnect(4).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.destroyInstance(4).isSuccess());
}

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
static void testRangeMatrix(CELoadManager& mgr)
{
    section("per-model range matrix");
    struct Row { const char* model; double expect; };
    const Row rows[] = {
        {"PLZ1005WH2", 20.2}, {"PLZ2005WH2", 40.4}, {"PLZ4005WH2", 80.8},
        {"PLZ12005WH2", 242.4}, {"PLZ20005WH2", 404.0},
    };
    U32BIT load = 20;
    for (const Row& r : rows) {
        CHECK(mgr.createInstance(load, r.model).isSuccess());
        S_ELoad_ParameterRange rng;
        ELoadError e = mgr.getParameterRange(load, 1, Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCurrent, rng);
        CHECK(e.isSuccess() && qAbs(rng.m_dMax - r.expect) < 1e-6);
        mgr.destroyInstance(load);
        ++load;
    }
}

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
static void testTwoInstances(CELoadManager& mgr)
{
    section("two simultaneous instances (SimLoad + PLZ)");
    CHECK(mgr.createInstance(30, "SimLoad").isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.createInstance(31, "PLZ4005WH2").isSuccess());
    S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig cs; cs.setResourceString("SIM::PLZ4005WH2");
    CHECK(mgr.connect(30, cs).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.connect(31, cfgFor(PLZ_RES)).isSuccess());
    // independent setpoints
    CHECK(mgr.setCurrent(30, 1, 7.0).isSuccess());
    CHECK(mgr.setCurrent(31, 1, 33.0).isSuccess());
    FDOUBLE a = 0.0, b = 0.0;
    CHECK(mgr.readbackSetCurrent(30, 1, a).isSuccess() && qAbs(a - 7.0) < 1e-6);
    CHECK(mgr.readbackSetCurrent(31, 1, b).isSuccess() && qAbs(b - 33.0) < 1e-6);
    mgr.disconnect(30); mgr.disconnect(31);
    mgr.destroyInstance(30); mgr.destroyInstance(31);
}

/*----------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
int main(int argc, char** argv)
{
    QCoreApplication app(argc, argv);

    QString pluginDir = (argc > 1) ? QString::fromLocal8Bit(argv[1])
                                   : QCoreApplication::applicationDirPath() + "/../plugins";
    out() << "Plugin dir: " << pluginDir << "\n";

    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    mgr.loadPlugins(pluginDir);

    testDiscovery(mgr);
    testInstances(mgr);
    testPlzEndToEnd(mgr);
    testWrongModelAndTimeout(mgr);
    testSimLoadFull(mgr);
    testRangeMatrix(mgr);
    testTwoInstances(mgr);

    out() << "\n" << (g_fail ? "FAILED" : "PASSED")
          << ": " << (g_checks - g_fail) << "/" << g_checks << " checks\n";
    out().flush();
    return g_fail;
}

/*============================================================================
 *  TesterCommon.h - shared constants and helpers for the ELoad Plugin Test app.
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef TESTERCOMMON_H
#define TESTERCOMMON_H

#include <QPushButton>
#include <QString>

#include "ELoadTypes.h"

// The tester drives a single instrument bound to this load number / channel.
static const U32BIT TESTER_LOAD    = 1;
static const U32BIT TESTER_CHANNEL = 1;

// Colour a push button (Connect = green, Disconnect = red, Load ON = green …).
inline void tintButton(QPushButton* in_pButton, const QString& in_strColor)
{
    in_pButton->setStyleSheet(
        QString("QPushButton { background-color: %1; color: white; font-weight: bold;"
                " border: 1px solid #3a3d41; border-radius: 3px; padding: 6px; }"
                "QPushButton:disabled { background-color: #2b2d30; color: #6a6d70; }")
            .arg(in_strColor));
}

#endif // TESTERCOMMON_H

-- GuiTester/ELoadTesterWindow.h

/*============================================================================
 *  ELoadTesterWindow.h - the "ELoad Plugin Test" main window: a tab per
 *  function group (Connection / Control / Measurement / Protection /
 *  Pulse-Sine / Sequence) driving one instrument through CELoadManager.
 *==========================================================================*/

    void onDisconnected();

    QTabWidget*     m_pTabs;
    QLabel*         m_pStatus;
    ConnectionTab*  m_pConnection;

-- GuiTester/ELoadTesterWindow.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  ELoadTesterWindow.cpp
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "ELoadTesterWindow.h"
#include "TesterCommon.h"

#include "ConnectionTab.h"
#include "ControlTab.h"
#include "MeasurementTab.h"
#include "ProtectionTab.h"
#include "PulseSineTab.h"
#include "SequenceTab.h"

#include <QApplication>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QStatusBar>
#include <QTabWidget>

#include "ELoadManager.h"

ELoadTesterWindow::ELoadTesterWindow(const QString& in_strPluginDir, QWidget* parent)
    : QMainWindow(parent)
{
    setWindowTitle(QStringLiteral("ELoad Plugin Test"));
    CELoadManager::instance().loadPlugins(in_strPluginDir);

    m_pTabs = new QTabWidget(this);
    m_pConnection  = new ConnectionTab(this);
    m_pControl     = new ControlTab(this);
    m_pMeasurement = new MeasurementTab(this);
    m_pProtection  = new ProtectionTab(this);
    m_pPulseSine   = new PulseSineTab(this);
    m_pSequence    = new SequenceTab(this);

    m_pTabs->addTab(m_pConnection,  tr("Connection"));
    m_pTabs->addTab(m_pControl,     tr("Control"));
    m_pTabs->addTab(m_pMeasurement, tr("Measurement"));
    m_pTabs->addTab(m_pProtection,  tr("Protection"));
    m_pTabs->addTab(m_pPulseSine,   tr("Pulse / Sine"));
    m_pTabs->addTab(m_pSequence,    tr("Sequence"));
    setCentralWidget(m_pTabs);

    m_pStatus = new QLabel(tr("Disconnected"), this);
    statusBar()->addWidget(m_pStatus);

    connect(m_pConnection, SIGNAL(connected(QString)), this, SLOT(onConnected(QString)));
    connect(m_pConnection, SIGNAL(disconnected()), this, SLOT(onDisconnected()));
    connect(m_pConnection, SIGNAL(log(QString)), this, SLOT(onLog(QString)));
    connect(m_pControl,    SIGNAL(log(QString)), this, SLOT(onLog(QString)));
    connect(m_pProtection, SIGNAL(log(QString)), this, SLOT(onLog(QString)));
    connect(m_pPulseSine,  SIGNAL(log(QString)), this, SLOT(onLog(QString)));
    connect(m_pSequence,   SIGNAL(log(QString)), this, SLOT(onLog(QString)));

    setOperationTabsEnabled(false);
    resize(800, 680);
}

void ELoadTesterWindow::setOperationTabsEnabled(bool in_bEnabled)
{
    m_pControl->setConnected(in_bEnabled);
    m_pMeasurement->setConnected(in_bEnabled);
    m_pProtection->setConnected(in_bEnabled);
    m_pPulseSine->setConnected(in_bEnabled);
    m_pSequence->setConnected(in_bEnabled);
}

void ELoadTesterWindow::onConnected(const QString& in_strModel)
{
    setOperationTabsEnabled(true);
    m_pStatus->setText(tr("Connected — %1").arg(in_strModel));
    m_pTabs->setCurrentWidget(m_pControl);
}

void ELoadTesterWindow::onDisconnected()
{
    setOperationTabsEnabled(false);
    m_pStatus->setText(tr("Disconnected"));
    m_pTabs->setCurrentWidget(m_pConnection);
}

void ELoadTesterWindow::onLog(const QString& in_strText)
{
    statusBar()->showMessage(in_strText, 4000);
}

int ELoadTesterWindow::runSmokeTest()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    if (!mgr.getAvailablePlugins().contains(QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2"))) return 1;
    if (!m_pConnection->connectTo(QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2"),
                                  QStringLiteral("MOCK0::PLZ4005WH2::INSTR")))
        return 2;
    mgr.setMode(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC);
    mgr.setCurrent(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, 5.0);
    mgr.enableInput(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, true);
    const bool ok = m_pMeasurement->pollOnce();
    mgr.disconnect(TESTER_LOAD);
    mgr.destroyInstance(TESTER_LOAD);
    return ok ? 0 : 3;
}

int ELoadTesterWindow::screenshotTo(const QString& in_strPath)
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    if (!mgr.getAvailablePlugins().contains(QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2"))) return 1;
    if (!m_pConnection->connectTo(QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2"),
                                  QStringLiteral("MOCK0::PLZ4005WH2::INSTR")))
        return 2;
    onConnected(QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2"));
    mgr.setMode(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC);
    mgr.setCurrent(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, 25.0);
    mgr.enableInput(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, true);
    m_pMeasurement->pollOnce();
    m_pTabs->setCurrentWidget(m_pMeasurement);
    show();
    QApplication::processEvents();
    const bool ok = grab().save(in_strPath);
    mgr.disconnect(TESTER_LOAD);
    mgr.destroyInstance(TESTER_LOAD);
    return ok ? 0 : 3;
}

-- GuiTester/ConnectionTab.h

/*============================================================================
 *  ConnectionTab.h - model + interface + address, Connect/Disconnect, identity.
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef CONNECTIONTAB_H
#define CONNECTIONTAB_H

#include <QWidget>

#include "ELoadManager.h"

class QComboBox;
class QLineEdit;
class QLabel;
class QPushButton;

class ConnectionTab : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit ConnectionTab(QWidget* parent = nullptr);

    QString selectedModel() const;
    bool    connectTo(const QString& in_strModel, const QString& in_strResource); // for smoke

signals:
    void connected(const QString& in_strModel);
    void disconnected();
    void log(const QString& in_strText);

private slots:
    void onConnect();
    void onDisconnect();
    void onInterfaceChanged();

private:
    S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig buildConfig() const;

    QComboBox*   m_pModelCombo;
    QComboBox*   m_pInterfaceCombo;
    QLineEdit*   m_pAddressEdit;
    QPushButton* m_pConnectBtn;
    QPushButton* m_pDisconnectBtn;
    QLabel*      m_pIdentityLabel;
};

#endif // CONNECTIONTAB_H

-- GuiTester/ConnectionTab.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  ConnectionTab.cpp
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "ConnectionTab.h"
#include "TesterCommon.h"

#include <QComboBox>
#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QGroupBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

ConnectionTab::ConnectionTab(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root = new QVBoxLayout(this);

    QFormLayout* form = new QFormLayout;
    m_pModelCombo = new QComboBox(this);
    for (const QString& m : CELoadManager::instance().getAvailablePlugins())
        m_pModelCombo->addItem(m);
    const int iRef = m_pModelCombo->findText(QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2"));
    if (iRef >= 0) m_pModelCombo->setCurrentIndex(iRef);

    m_pInterfaceCombo = new QComboBox(this);
    m_pInterfaceCombo->addItems(QStringList() << "LAN" << "USB" << "RS232" << "GPIB" << "Mock (dev)");

    m_pAddressEdit = new QLineEdit(QStringLiteral("192.168.1.100"), this);

    form->addRow(tr("Model:"), m_pModelCombo);
    form->addRow(tr("Interface:"), m_pInterfaceCombo);
    form->addRow(tr("Host / Device:"), m_pAddressEdit);
    root->addLayout(form);

    QHBoxLayout* btns = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pConnectBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Connect"), this);
    m_pDisconnectBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Disconnect"), this);
    tintButton(m_pConnectBtn, "#1e6b33");
    tintButton(m_pDisconnectBtn, "#6b1e1e");
    m_pDisconnectBtn->setEnabled(false);
    btns->addWidget(m_pConnectBtn);
    btns->addWidget(m_pDisconnectBtn);
    root->addLayout(btns);

    QGroupBox* idBox = new QGroupBox(tr("Instrument Identity"), this);
    QVBoxLayout* idl = new QVBoxLayout(idBox);
    m_pIdentityLabel = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("—"), idBox);
    m_pIdentityLabel->setWordWrap(true);
    idl->addWidget(m_pIdentityLabel);
    root->addWidget(idBox);
    root->addStretch(1);

    connect(m_pConnectBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onConnect()));
    connect(m_pDisconnectBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onDisconnect()));
    connect(m_pInterfaceCombo, SIGNAL(currentIndexChanged(int)), this, SLOT(onInterfaceChanged()));
}

QString ConnectionTab::selectedModel() const { return m_pModelCombo->currentText(); }

void ConnectionTab::onInterfaceChanged()
{
    const QString iface = m_pInterfaceCombo->currentText();
    if (iface == "LAN")            m_pAddressEdit->setText("192.168.1.100");
    else if (iface == "USB")       m_pAddressEdit->setText("USB0::0x0B3E::0x1042::00000000::INSTR");
    else if (iface == "RS232")     m_pAddressEdit->setText("COM3");
    else if (iface == "GPIB")      m_pAddressEdit->setText("5");
    else /* Mock (dev) */          m_pAddressEdit->setText("MOCK0::" + selectedModel() + "::INSTR");
}

S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig ConnectionTab::buildConfig() const
{
    S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig cfg;
    const QString iface = m_pInterfaceCombo->currentText();
    const QByteArray addr = m_pAddressEdit->text().trimmed().toLatin1();
    if (iface == "LAN") {
        cfg.m_enumProtocol = Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::TCPIP;
        qstrncpy(cfg.m_szIpAddress, addr.constData(), CONN_IP_ADDR_SIZE);
    } else if (iface == "USB") {
        cfg.m_enumProtocol = Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::USB;
        cfg.setResourceString(QString::fromLatin1(addr));      // explicit USB resource
    } else if (iface == "RS232") {
        cfg.m_enumProtocol = Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::RS232;
        qstrncpy(cfg.m_szPortName, addr.constData(), CONN_PORT_NAME_SIZE);
    } else if (iface == "GPIB") {
        cfg.m_enumProtocol = Enum_ELoad_CommunicationProtocol::GPIB;
        cfg.m_u32GpibAddress = m_pAddressEdit->text().toUInt();
    } else { // Mock (dev)
        cfg.setResourceString(QString::fromLatin1(addr));
    }
    return cfg;
}

void ConnectionTab::onConnect()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    const QString model = selectedModel();
    if (model.isEmpty()) { emit log(tr("No model selected")); return; }

    if (!mgr.instanceExists(TESTER_LOAD)) {
        ELoadError e = mgr.createInstance(TESTER_LOAD, model);
        if (!e.isSuccess()) { emit log(tr("createInstance failed: %1").arg(e.toString())); return; }
    }
    ELoadError e = mgr.connect(TESTER_LOAD, buildConfig());
    if (!e.isSuccess()) {
        emit log(tr("Connect failed: %1").arg(e.toString()));
        mgr.destroyInstance(TESTER_LOAD);
        return;
    }
    QString idn;
    mgr.getIdentification(TESTER_LOAD, idn);
    m_pIdentityLabel->setText(idn.isEmpty() ? tr("(connected)") : idn);
    m_pConnectBtn->setEnabled(false);
    m_pDisconnectBtn->setEnabled(true);
    m_pModelCombo->setEnabled(false);
    m_pInterfaceCombo->setEnabled(false);
    m_pAddressEdit->setEnabled(false);
    emit log(tr("Connected %1: %2").arg(model, idn));
    emit connected(model);
}

void ConnectionTab::onDisconnect()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    mgr.disconnect(TESTER_LOAD);
    mgr.destroyInstance(TESTER_LOAD);
    m_pIdentityLabel->setText(QStringLiteral("—"));
    m_pConnectBtn->setEnabled(true);
    m_pDisconnectBtn->setEnabled(false);
    m_pModelCombo->setEnabled(true);
    m_pInterfaceCombo->setEnabled(true);
    m_pAddressEdit->setEnabled(true);
    emit log(tr("Disconnected"));
    emit disconnected();
}

bool ConnectionTab::connectTo(const QString& in_strModel, const QString& in_strResource)
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    if (!mgr.createInstance(TESTER_LOAD, in_strModel).isSuccess()) return false;
    S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig cfg; cfg.setResourceString(in_strResource);
    if (!mgr.connect(TESTER_LOAD, cfg).isSuccess()) { mgr.destroyInstance(TESTER_LOAD); return false; }
    emit connected(in_strModel);
    return true;
}

-- GuiTester/ControlTab.h

/*============================================================================
 *  ControlTab.h - mode, setpoint and per-mode parameters + Apply / Load ON.
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef CONTROLTAB_H
#define CONTROLTAB_H

#include <QWidget>
#include "ELoadManager.h"

class QComboBox;
class QDoubleSpinBox;
class QGroupBox;
class QLabel;
class QPushButton;

class ControlTab : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit ControlTab(QWidget* parent = nullptr);
    void setConnected(bool in_bConnected);

signals:
    void log(const QString& in_strText);

private slots:
    void onModeChanged(int idx);
    void onApply();
    void onToggleLoad(bool checked);

private:
    void loadSetpointRange();

    QComboBox*      m_pModeCombo;
    QLabel*         m_pSetpointLabel;
    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pSetpointSpin;
    QGroupBox*      m_pParamBox;
    QLabel*         m_pSlewLabel;    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pSlewSpin;
    QLabel*         m_pSoftLabel;    QComboBox*      m_pSoftCombo;
    QLabel*         m_pRangeLabel;   QComboBox*      m_pRangeCombo;
    QLabel*         m_pRespLabel;    QComboBox*      m_pRespCombo;
    QPushButton*    m_pApplyBtn;
    QPushButton*    m_pLoadBtn;
};

#endif // CONTROLTAB_H

-- GuiTester/ControlTab.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  ControlTab.cpp
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "ControlTab.h"
#include "TesterCommon.h"

#include <QComboBox>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QGroupBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace {
Enum_ELoad_Mode idxToMode(int i)
{
    switch (i) {
    case 0: return Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC;
    case 1: return Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCR;
    case 2: return Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCV;
    case 3: return Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCP;
    default: return Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumARB;
    }
}
struct S_Soft { const char* label; double sec; };
const S_Soft SOFT[] = { {"OFF",0.0},{"0.5 ms",0.0005},{"1 ms",0.001},{"2 ms",0.002},
                        {"5 ms",0.005},{"10 ms",0.01},{"20 ms",0.02},{"50 ms",0.05},{"100 ms",0.1} };
}

ControlTab::ControlTab(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root = new QVBoxLayout(this);

    QFormLayout* top = new QFormLayout;
    m_pModeCombo = new QComboBox(this);
    m_pModeCombo->addItems(QStringList() << "CC" << "CR" << "CV" << "CP" << "ARB");
    m_pSetpointLabel = new QLabel(tr("Current (A):"), this);
    m_pSetpointSpin = new QDoubleSpinBox(this);
    m_pSetpointSpin->setDecimals(3);
    m_pSetpointSpin->setMaximum(1e6);
    top->addRow(tr("Mode:"), m_pModeCombo);
    top->addRow(m_pSetpointLabel, m_pSetpointSpin);
    root->addLayout(top);

    m_pParamBox = new QGroupBox(tr("CC Parameters"), this);
    QFormLayout* pf = new QFormLayout(m_pParamBox);
    m_pSlewLabel = new QLabel(tr("Slew Rate (A/µs):"), m_pParamBox);
    m_pSlewSpin = new QDoubleSpinBox(m_pParamBox);
    m_pSlewSpin->setDecimals(3); m_pSlewSpin->setMaximum(1e4); m_pSlewSpin->setValue(1.0);
    m_pSoftLabel = new QLabel(tr("Soft Start:"), m_pParamBox);
    m_pSoftCombo = new QComboBox(m_pParamBox);
    for (const S_Soft& s : SOFT) m_pSoftCombo->addItem(s.label);
    m_pRangeLabel = new QLabel(tr("CR Range:"), m_pParamBox);
    m_pRangeCombo = new QComboBox(m_pParamBox);
    m_pRangeCombo->addItems(QStringList() << "LOW" << "HIGH");
    m_pRespLabel = new QLabel(tr("Response:"), m_pParamBox);
    m_pRespCombo = new QComboBox(m_pParamBox);
    m_pRespCombo->addItems(QStringList() << "NORMAL" << "FAST");
    pf->addRow(m_pSlewLabel, m_pSlewSpin);
    pf->addRow(m_pSoftLabel, m_pSoftCombo);
    pf->addRow(m_pRangeLabel, m_pRangeCombo);
    pf->addRow(m_pRespLabel, m_pRespCombo);
    root->addWidget(m_pParamBox);

    QHBoxLayout* btns = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pApplyBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Apply"), this);
    m_pLoadBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Load OFF"), this);
    m_pLoadBtn->setCheckable(true);
    btns->addWidget(m_pApplyBtn);
    btns->addWidget(m_pLoadBtn);
    root->addLayout(btns);
    root->addStretch(1);

    connect(m_pModeCombo, SIGNAL(currentIndexChanged(int)), this, SLOT(onModeChanged(int)));
    connect(m_pApplyBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onApply()));
    connect(m_pLoadBtn, SIGNAL(toggled(bool)), this, SLOT(onToggleLoad(bool)));

    onModeChanged(0);
    setConnected(false);
}

void ControlTab::onModeChanged(int idx)
{
    static const char* UNIT[] = { "Current (A):", "Conductance (S):", "Voltage (V):", "Power (W):", "ARB:" };
    m_pSetpointLabel->setText(tr(UNIT[idx < 0 ? 0 : idx]));
    const bool cc = (idx == 0), cr = (idx == 1), cv = (idx == 2);
    m_pSlewLabel->setVisible(cc);  m_pSlewSpin->setVisible(cc);
    m_pSoftLabel->setVisible(cc);  m_pSoftCombo->setVisible(cc);
    m_pRangeLabel->setVisible(cr); m_pRangeCombo->setVisible(cr);
    m_pRespLabel->setVisible(cr || cv); m_pRespCombo->setVisible(cr || cv);
    static const char* TITLE[] = { "CC Parameters", "CR Parameters", "CV Parameters", "CP Parameters", "ARB Parameters" };
    m_pParamBox->setTitle(tr(TITLE[idx < 0 ? 0 : idx]));
    m_pParamBox->setVisible(cc || cr || cv);
    m_pSetpointSpin->setVisible(idx != 4);
    m_pSetpointLabel->setVisible(idx != 4);
    if (CELoadManager::instance().isConnected(TESTER_LOAD)) loadSetpointRange();
}

void ControlTab::loadSetpointRange()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    const int idx = m_pModeCombo->currentIndex();
    Enum_ELoad_ParamId pid = Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCurrent;
    switch (idx) {
    case 0: pid = Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumCurrent; break;
    case 1: pid = Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumConductanceHigh; break;
    case 2: pid = Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumVoltage; break;
    case 3: pid = Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumPower; break;
    default: return;
    }
    S_ELoad_ParameterRange rng;
    if (mgr.getParameterRange(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, pid, rng).isSuccess())
        m_pSetpointSpin->setRange(rng.m_dMin, rng.m_dMax);
    S_ELoad_ParameterRange slew;
    if (mgr.getParameterRange(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, Enum_ELoad_ParamId::m_enumSlewRate, slew).isSuccess())
        m_pSlewSpin->setRange(slew.m_dMin, slew.m_dMax);
}

void ControlTab::onApply()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    const int idx = m_pModeCombo->currentIndex();
    ELoadError e = mgr.setMode(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, idxToMode(idx));
    if (!e.isSuccess()) { emit log(tr("setMode: %1").arg(e.toString())); return; }

    const double v = m_pSetpointSpin->value();
    switch (idx) {
    case 0:
        e = mgr.setCurrent(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, v);
        mgr.setSlewRate(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pSlewSpin->value());
        mgr.setSoftStart(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, SOFT[m_pSoftCombo->currentIndex()].sec);
        break;
    case 1:
        mgr.setCRRange(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL,
                       m_pRangeCombo->currentIndex() == 0 ? Enum_ELoad_CRRange::m_enumLow : Enum_ELoad_CRRange::m_enumHigh);
        e = mgr.setConductance(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, v);
        mgr.setCRResponse(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL,
                          m_pRespCombo->currentIndex() == 0 ? Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed::m_enumNormal : Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed::m_enumFast);
        break;
    case 2:
        e = mgr.setVoltage(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, v);
        mgr.setCVResponse(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL,
                          m_pRespCombo->currentIndex() == 0 ? Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed::m_enumNormal : Enum_ELoad_ResponseSpeed::m_enumFast);
        break;
    case 3:
        e = mgr.setPower(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, v);
        break;
    default: break;
    }
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Applied %1").arg(m_pModeCombo->currentText())
                           : tr("Apply: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}

void ControlTab::onToggleLoad(bool checked)
{
    ELoadError e = CELoadManager::instance().enableInput(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, checked);
    if (!e.isSuccess()) {
        emit log(tr("Load: %1").arg(e.toString()));
        m_pLoadBtn->blockSignals(true); m_pLoadBtn->setChecked(!checked); m_pLoadBtn->blockSignals(false);
        return;
    }
    m_pLoadBtn->setText(checked ? tr("Load ON") : tr("Load OFF"));
    tintButton(m_pLoadBtn, checked ? "#1e6b33" : "#6b1e1e");
    emit log(checked ? tr("Load ON") : tr("Load OFF"));
}

void ControlTab::setConnected(bool in_bConnected)
{
    m_pApplyBtn->setEnabled(in_bConnected);
    m_pLoadBtn->setEnabled(in_bConnected);
    if (in_bConnected) {
        loadSetpointRange();
        tintButton(m_pLoadBtn, "#6b1e1e");
    } else {
        m_pLoadBtn->blockSignals(true);
        m_pLoadBtn->setChecked(false);
        m_pLoadBtn->setText(tr("Load OFF"));
        m_pLoadBtn->setStyleSheet(QString());
        m_pLoadBtn->blockSignals(false);
    }
}

-- GuiTester/MeasurementTab.h

/*============================================================================
 *  MeasurementTab.h - live V/I/P/Ah/Wh/elapsed readout with a poll timer.
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef MEASUREMENTTAB_H
#define MEASUREMENTTAB_H

#include <QWidget>
#include "ELoadManager.h"

class QLabel;
class QDoubleSpinBox;
class QPushButton;
class QTimer;

class MeasurementTab : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit MeasurementTab(QWidget* parent = nullptr);
    void setConnected(bool in_bConnected);
    bool pollOnce();                          // used by the smoke test

private slots:
    void onStartStop();
    void onTick();

private:
    QLabel*         m_pCurrent;
    QLabel*         m_pVoltage;
    QLabel*         m_pPower;
    QLabel*         m_pCapacity;
    QLabel*         m_pEnergy;
    QLabel*         m_pElapsed;
    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pInterval;
    QPushButton*    m_pStartBtn;
    QTimer*         m_pTimer;
};

#endif // MEASUREMENTTAB_H

-- GuiTester/MeasurementTab.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  MeasurementTab.cpp
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "MeasurementTab.h"
#include "TesterCommon.h"

#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QGroupBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QTimer>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

MeasurementTab::MeasurementTab(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root = new QVBoxLayout(this);

    QGroupBox* box = new QGroupBox(tr("Live Measurements"), this);
    QFormLayout* f = new QFormLayout(box);
    m_pCurrent  = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("—"), box);
    m_pVoltage  = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("—"), box);
    m_pPower    = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("—"), box);
    m_pCapacity = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("—"), box);
    m_pEnergy   = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("—"), box);
    m_pElapsed  = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("—"), box);
    f->addRow(tr("Current (A):"),  m_pCurrent);
    f->addRow(tr("Voltage (V):"),  m_pVoltage);
    f->addRow(tr("Power (W):"),    m_pPower);
    f->addRow(tr("Capacity (Ah):"),m_pCapacity);
    f->addRow(tr("Energy (Wh):"),  m_pEnergy);
    f->addRow(tr("Elapsed (s):"),  m_pElapsed);
    root->addWidget(box);

    QHBoxLayout* ctl = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pInterval = new QDoubleSpinBox(this);
    m_pInterval->setDecimals(1); m_pInterval->setRange(0.1, 60.0); m_pInterval->setValue(1.0);
    m_pInterval->setSuffix(tr(" s"));
    m_pStartBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Start"), this);
    m_pStartBtn->setCheckable(true);
    ctl->addWidget(new QLabel(tr("Poll interval:"), this));
    ctl->addWidget(m_pInterval, 1);
    ctl->addWidget(m_pStartBtn);
    root->addLayout(ctl);
    root->addStretch(1);

    m_pTimer = new QTimer(this);
    connect(m_pTimer, SIGNAL(timeout()), this, SLOT(onTick()));
    connect(m_pStartBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onStartStop()));

    setConnected(false);
}

bool MeasurementTab::pollOnce()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    FDOUBLE i = 0, v = 0, p = 0, ah = 0, wh = 0, s = 0;
    const bool ok = mgr.measureCurrent(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, i).isSuccess()
                 && mgr.measureVoltage(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, v).isSuccess()
                 && mgr.measurePower(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, p).isSuccess();
    mgr.measureCapacity(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, ah);
    mgr.measureEnergy(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, wh);
    mgr.measureElapsedTime(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, s);
    if (ok) {
        m_pCurrent->setText(QString::number(i, 'f', 3));
        m_pVoltage->setText(QString::number(v, 'f', 3));
        m_pPower->setText(QString::number(p, 'f', 3));
        m_pCapacity->setText(QString::number(ah, 'f', 4));
        m_pEnergy->setText(QString::number(wh, 'f', 4));
        m_pElapsed->setText(QString::number(s, 'f', 1));
    }
    return ok;
}

void MeasurementTab::onTick() { pollOnce(); }

void MeasurementTab::onStartStop()
{
    if (m_pStartBtn->isChecked()) {
        m_pTimer->start(static_cast<int>(m_pInterval->value() * 1000.0));
        m_pStartBtn->setText(tr("Stop"));
        onTick();
    } else {
        m_pTimer->stop();
        m_pStartBtn->setText(tr("Start"));
    }
}

void MeasurementTab::setConnected(bool in_bConnected)
{
    m_pStartBtn->setEnabled(in_bConnected);
    if (!in_bConnected) {
        m_pTimer->stop();
        m_pStartBtn->blockSignals(true);
        m_pStartBtn->setChecked(false);
        m_pStartBtn->setText(tr("Start"));
        m_pStartBtn->blockSignals(false);
        for (QLabel* l : { m_pCurrent, m_pVoltage, m_pPower, m_pCapacity, m_pEnergy, m_pElapsed })
            l->setText(QStringLiteral("—"));
    }
}

-- GuiTester/ProtectionTab.h

/*============================================================================
 *  ProtectionTab.h - OCP / OPP / UVP / watchdog + clear.
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef PROTECTIONTAB_H
#define PROTECTIONTAB_H

#include <QWidget>
#include "ELoadManager.h"

class QDoubleSpinBox;
class QRadioButton;
class QCheckBox;
class QPushButton;

class ProtectionTab : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit ProtectionTab(QWidget* parent = nullptr);
    void setConnected(bool in_bConnected);

signals:
    void log(const QString& in_strText);

private slots:
    void onApplyOCP();
    void onApplyOPP();
    void onApplyUVP();
    void onApplyWDP();
    void onClear();

private:
    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pOcp;  QRadioButton* m_pOcpTrip; QRadioButton* m_pOcpLimit; QPushButton* m_pOcpBtn;
    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pOpp;  QRadioButton* m_pOppTrip; QRadioButton* m_pOppLimit; QPushButton* m_pOppBtn;
    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pUvp;  QCheckBox* m_pUvpEnable;  QRadioButton* m_pUvpTrip; QRadioButton* m_pUvpLimit; QPushButton* m_pUvpBtn;
    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pWdp;  QCheckBox* m_pWdpEnable;  QPushButton* m_pWdpBtn;
    QPushButton*    m_pClearBtn;
};

#endif // PROTECTIONTAB_H

-- GuiTester/ProtectionTab.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  ProtectionTab.cpp
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "ProtectionTab.h"
#include "TesterCommon.h"

#include <QButtonGroup>
#include <QCheckBox>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QGroupBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QRadioButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace {
QWidget* actionRow(QRadioButton*& trip, QRadioButton*& limit, QWidget* parent)
{
    QWidget* w = new QWidget(parent);
    QHBoxLayout* h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
    h->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    trip = new QRadioButton(QObject::tr("Trip"), w);
    limit = new QRadioButton(QObject::tr("Limit"), w);
    QButtonGroup* g = new QButtonGroup(w);
    g->addButton(trip); g->addButton(limit);
    trip->setChecked(true);
    h->addWidget(trip); h->addStretch(1); h->addWidget(limit); h->addStretch(2);
    return w;
}
}

ProtectionTab::ProtectionTab(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root = new QVBoxLayout(this);

    /*--- OCP ---*/
    QGroupBox* ocp = new QGroupBox(tr("Overcurrent Protection (OCP)"), this);
    QFormLayout* of = new QFormLayout(ocp);
    m_pOcp = new QDoubleSpinBox(ocp); m_pOcp->setDecimals(3); m_pOcp->setMaximum(1e5);
    of->addRow(tr("Threshold (A):"), m_pOcp);
    of->addRow(tr("Action:"), actionRow(m_pOcpTrip, m_pOcpLimit, ocp));
    m_pOcpBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Apply OCP"), ocp);
    of->addRow(m_pOcpBtn);
    root->addWidget(ocp);

    /*--- OPP ---*/
    QGroupBox* opp = new QGroupBox(tr("Overpower Protection (OPP)"), this);
    QFormLayout* pf = new QFormLayout(opp);
    m_pOpp = new QDoubleSpinBox(opp); m_pOpp->setDecimals(1); m_pOpp->setMaximum(1e6);
    pf->addRow(tr("Threshold (W):"), m_pOpp);
    pf->addRow(tr("Action:"), actionRow(m_pOppTrip, m_pOppLimit, opp));
    m_pOppBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Apply OPP"), opp);
    pf->addRow(m_pOppBtn);
    root->addWidget(opp);

    /*--- UVP ---*/
    QGroupBox* uvp = new QGroupBox(tr("Undervoltage Protection (UVP)"), this);
    QFormLayout* uf = new QFormLayout(uvp);
    m_pUvp = new QDoubleSpinBox(uvp); m_pUvp->setDecimals(2); m_pUvp->setMaximum(1e4);
    uf->addRow(tr("Threshold (V):"), m_pUvp);
    m_pUvpEnable = new QCheckBox(tr("Enable UVP"), uvp);
    uf->addRow(m_pUvpEnable);
    uf->addRow(tr("Action:"), actionRow(m_pUvpTrip, m_pUvpLimit, uvp));
    m_pUvpBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Apply UVP"), uvp);
    uf->addRow(m_pUvpBtn);
    root->addWidget(uvp);

    /*--- Watchdog ---*/
    QGroupBox* wdp = new QGroupBox(tr("Watchdog Protection (WDP)"), this);
    QFormLayout* wf = new QFormLayout(wdp);
    m_pWdp = new QDoubleSpinBox(wdp); m_pWdp->setDecimals(0); m_pWdp->setRange(1, 3600); m_pWdp->setValue(1);
    m_pWdp->setSuffix(tr(" s"));
    wf->addRow(tr("Delay:"), m_pWdp);
    m_pWdpEnable = new QCheckBox(tr("Enable Watchdog"), wdp);
    wf->addRow(m_pWdpEnable);
    m_pWdpBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Apply WDP"), wdp);
    wf->addRow(m_pWdpBtn);
    root->addWidget(wdp);

    m_pClearBtn = new QPushButton(tr("Clear Protection"), this);
    root->addWidget(m_pClearBtn);
    root->addStretch(1);

    connect(m_pOcpBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onApplyOCP()));
    connect(m_pOppBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onApplyOPP()));
    connect(m_pUvpBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onApplyUVP()));
    connect(m_pWdpBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onApplyWDP()));
    connect(m_pClearBtn, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onClear()));

    setConnected(false);
}

void ProtectionTab::onApplyOCP()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    ELoadError e = mgr.setOCP(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pOcp->value());
    mgr.setOCPAction(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL,
                     m_pOcpTrip->isChecked() ? Enum_ELoad_ProtAction::m_enumTrip : Enum_ELoad_ProtAction::m_enumLimit);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("OCP applied") : tr("OCP: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}

void ProtectionTab::onApplyOPP()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    ELoadError e = mgr.setOPP(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pOpp->value());
    mgr.setOPPAction(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL,
                     m_pOppTrip->isChecked() ? Enum_ELoad_ProtAction::m_enumTrip : Enum_ELoad_ProtAction::m_enumLimit);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("OPP applied") : tr("OPP: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}

void ProtectionTab::onApplyUVP()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    ELoadError e = mgr.setUVP(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pUvp->value());
    mgr.setUVPAction(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL,
                     m_pUvpTrip->isChecked() ? Enum_ELoad_ProtAction::m_enumTrip : Enum_ELoad_ProtAction::m_enumLimit);
    mgr.setUVPEnable(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pUvpEnable->isChecked());
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("UVP applied") : tr("UVP: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}

void ProtectionTab::onApplyWDP()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    ELoadError e = mgr.setWatchdogDelay(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pWdp->value());
    mgr.setWatchdog(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pWdpEnable->isChecked());
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Watchdog applied") : tr("WDP: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}

void ProtectionTab::onClear()
{
    ELoadError e = CELoadManager::instance().clearProtection(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Protection cleared") : tr("Clear: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}

void ProtectionTab::setConnected(bool in_bConnected)
{
    for (QPushButton* b : { m_pOcpBtn, m_pOppBtn, m_pUvpBtn, m_pWdpBtn, m_pClearBtn })
        b->setEnabled(in_bConnected);
}

    void onStopPulse();
    void onStartSine();
    void onStopSine();

private:

    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pPulseDuty;
    QPushButton*    m_pStartPulse;
    QPushButton*    m_pStopPulse;
    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pSineAmpl;
    QDoubleSpinBox* m_pSineFreq;
    QPushButton*    m_pStartSine;
    QPushButton*    m_pStopSine;
