-- GuiTester/PulseSineTab.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  PulseSineTab.cpp
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "PulseSineTab.h"
#include "TesterCommon.h"

#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QGroupBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

PulseSineTab::PulseSineTab(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root = new QVBoxLayout(this);

    /*--- Pulse ---*/
    QGroupBox* pulse = new QGroupBox(tr("Pulse Function (CC / CR)"), this);
    QFormLayout* pf = new QFormLayout(pulse);
    m_pPulseLevel = new QDoubleSpinBox(pulse); m_pPulseLevel->setDecimals(3); m_pPulseLevel->setMaximum(1e5);
    m_pPulseFreq = new QDoubleSpinBox(pulse); m_pPulseFreq->setDecimals(1); m_pPulseFreq->setRange(1, 10000); m_pPulseFreq->setValue(10.0);
    m_pPulseFreq->setSuffix(tr(" Hz"));
    m_pPulseDuty = new QDoubleSpinBox(pulse); m_pPulseDuty->setDecimals(1); m_pPulseDuty->setRange(5, 95); m_pPulseDuty->setValue(50.0);
    m_pPulseDuty->setSuffix(tr(" %"));
    pf->addRow(tr("Level:"), m_pPulseLevel);
    pf->addRow(tr("Frequency:"), m_pPulseFreq);
    pf->addRow(tr("Duty Cycle:"), m_pPulseDuty);
    QHBoxLayout* pb = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pStartPulse = new QPushButton(tr("Start Pulse"), pulse);
    m_pStopPulse = new QPushButton(tr("Stop Pulse"), pulse);
    pb->addWidget(m_pStartPulse); pb->addWidget(m_pStopPulse);
    pf->addRow(pb);
    root->addWidget(pulse);

    /*--- Sine ---*/
    QGroupBox* sine = new QGroupBox(tr("Sine Function (CC only)"), this);
    QFormLayout* sf = new QFormLayout(sine);
    m_pSineAmpl = new QDoubleSpinBox(sine); m_pSineAmpl->setDecimals(3); m_pSineAmpl->setMaximum(1e5);
    m_pSineFreq = new QDoubleSpinBox(sine); m_pSineFreq->setDecimals(1); m_pSineFreq->setRange(1, 10000); m_pSineFreq->setValue(10.0);
    m_pSineFreq->setSuffix(tr(" Hz"));
    sf->addRow(tr("Amplitude (A):"), m_pSineAmpl);
    sf->addRow(tr("Frequency:"), m_pSineFreq);
    QHBoxLayout* sb = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pStartSine = new QPushButton(tr("Start Sine"), sine);
    m_pStopSine = new QPushButton(tr("Stop Sine"), sine);
    sb->addWidget(m_pStartSine); sb->addWidget(m_pStopSine);
    sf->addRow(sb);
    root->addWidget(sine);
    root->addStretch(1);

    connect(m_pStartPulse, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onStartPulse()));
    connect(m_pStopPulse, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onStopPulse()));
    connect(m_pStartSine, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onStartSine()));
    connect(m_pStopSine, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onStopSine()));

    setConnected(false);
}

void PulseSineTab::onStartPulse()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    mgr.setPulseCurrentLevel(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pPulseLevel->value());
    mgr.setPulseFrequency(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pPulseFreq->value());
    mgr.setPulseDuty(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pPulseDuty->value());
    ELoadError e = mgr.startPulse(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Pulse started") : tr("Pulse: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}
void PulseSineTab::onStopPulse()
{
    ELoadError e = CELoadManager::instance().stopPulse(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Pulse stopped") : tr("Pulse: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}
void PulseSineTab::onStartSine()
{
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    mgr.setSineAmplitude(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pSineAmpl->value());
    mgr.setSineFrequency(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL, m_pSineFreq->value());
    ELoadError e = mgr.startSine(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Sine started") : tr("Sine: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}
void PulseSineTab::onStopSine()
{
    ELoadError e = CELoadManager::instance().stopSine(TESTER_LOAD, TESTER_CHANNEL);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Sine stopped") : tr("Sine: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}

void PulseSineTab::setConnected(bool in_bConnected)
{
    for (QPushButton* b : { m_pStartPulse, m_pStopPulse, m_pStartSine, m_pStopSine })
        b->setEnabled(in_bConnected);
}

-- GuiTester/SequenceTab.h

/*============================================================================
 *  SequenceTab.h - program list, step table and run control.
 *==========================================================================*/
#ifndef SEQUENCETAB_H
#define SEQUENCETAB_H

#include <QWidget>
#include "ELoadManager.h"

class QLineEdit;
class QListWidget;
class QSpinBox;
class QTableWidget;
class QPushButton;
class QLabel;
class QTimer;

class SequenceTab : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit SequenceTab(QWidget* parent = nullptr);
    void setConnected(bool in_bConnected);

signals:
    void log(const QString& in_strText);

private slots:
    void onCreate();
    void onDelete();
    void onRefresh();
    void onProgramSelected();
    void onAddStep();
    void onRemoveStep();
    void onSaveProgram();
    void onStart();
    void onStop();
    void onPause();
    void onResume();
    void onPollState();

private:
    QString currentProgram() const;

    QLineEdit*    m_pNewName;
    QPushButton*  m_pCreate;  QPushButton* m_pDelete;  QPushButton* m_pRefresh;
    QListWidget*  m_pProgramList;
    QSpinBox*     m_pLoop;
    QTableWidget* m_pSteps;
    QPushButton*  m_pAddStep;  QPushButton* m_pRemoveStep;  QPushButton* m_pSaveProgram;
    QPushButton*  m_pStart;  QPushButton* m_pStop;  QPushButton* m_pPause;  QPushButton* m_pResume;
    QLabel*       m_pState;
    QTimer*       m_pStateTimer;
};

#endif // SEQUENCETAB_H

-- GuiTester/SequenceTab.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  SequenceTab.cpp
 *==========================================================================*/
#include "SequenceTab.h"
#include "TesterCommon.h"

#include <QFormLayout>
#include <QGroupBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QListWidget>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QTableWidgetItem>
#include <QTimer>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace {
const char* seqStateName(Enum_ELoad_SeqState s)
{
    switch (s) {
    case Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumIdle:      return "IDLE";
    case Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumWaiting:   return "WAITING";
    case Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumRunning:   return "RUNNING";
    case Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumSuspended: return "SUSPENDED";
    case Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumPulse:     return "PULSE";
    case Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumSine:      return "SINE";
    default: return "—";
    }
}
}

SequenceTab::SequenceTab(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root = new QVBoxLayout(this);

    /*--- Programs ---*/
    QGroupBox* progBox = new QGroupBox(tr("Programs"), this);
    QVBoxLayout* pv = new QVBoxLayout(progBox);
    QHBoxLayout* nameRow = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pNewName = new QLineEdit(progBox);
    m_pNewName->setPlaceholderText(tr("New program name"));
    m_pCreate = new QPushButton(tr("Create"), progBox);
    m_pDelete = new QPushButton(tr("Delete"), progBox);
    m_pRefresh = new QPushButton(tr("Refresh"), progBox);
    nameRow->addWidget(new QLabel(tr("New name:"), progBox));
    nameRow->addWidget(m_pNewName, 1);
    pv->addLayout(nameRow);
    QHBoxLayout* pbtns = new QHBoxLayout;
    pbtns->addWidget(m_pCreate); pbtns->addWidget(m_pDelete); pbtns->addWidget(m_pRefresh);
    pv->addLayout(pbtns);
    m_pProgramList = new QListWidget(progBox);
    m_pProgramList->setMaximumHeight(120);
    pv->addWidget(m_pProgramList);
    QHBoxLayout* loopRow = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pLoop = new QSpinBox(progBox); m_pLoop->setRange(1, 100000); m_pLoop->setValue(1);
    loopRow->addWidget(new QLabel(tr("Loop count:"), progBox));
    loopRow->addWidget(m_pLoop, 1);
    pv->addLayout(loopRow);
    root->addWidget(progBox);

    /*--- Steps ---*/
    QGroupBox* stepBox = new QGroupBox(tr("Steps"), this);
    QVBoxLayout* sv = new QVBoxLayout(stepBox);
    QHBoxLayout* sbtns = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pAddStep = new QPushButton(tr("Add Step"), stepBox);
    m_pRemoveStep = new QPushButton(tr("Remove Step"), stepBox);
    m_pSaveProgram = new QPushButton(tr("Save Program"), stepBox);
    sbtns->addWidget(m_pAddStep); sbtns->addWidget(m_pRemoveStep); sbtns->addWidget(m_pSaveProgram);
    sv->addLayout(sbtns);
    m_pSteps = new QTableWidget(0, 6, stepBox);
    m_pSteps->setHorizontalHeaderLabels(
        QStringList() << tr("Dwell (s)") << tr("Level") << tr("Slew") << tr("Load") << tr("Transition") << tr("Trig Out"));
    m_pSteps->horizontalHeader()->setStretchLastSection(true);
    sv->addWidget(m_pSteps);
    root->addWidget(stepBox);

    /*--- Run control ---*/
    QGroupBox* runBox = new QGroupBox(tr("Run Control"), this);
    QHBoxLayout* rl = new QHBoxLayout(runBox);
    m_pStart = new QPushButton(tr("Start"), runBox);
    m_pStop = new QPushButton(tr("Stop"), runBox);
    m_pPause = new QPushButton(tr("Pause"), runBox);
    m_pResume = new QPushButton(tr("Resume"), runBox);
    m_pState = new QLabel(QStringLiteral("—"), runBox);
    rl->addWidget(m_pStart); rl->addWidget(m_pStop); rl->addWidget(m_pPause); rl->addWidget(m_pResume);
    rl->addWidget(new QLabel(tr("State:"), runBox)); rl->addWidget(m_pState); rl->addStretch(1);
    root->addWidget(runBox);

    connect(m_pCreate, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onCreate()));
    connect(m_pDelete, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onDelete()));
    connect(m_pRefresh, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onRefresh()));
    connect(m_pProgramList, SIGNAL(itemSelectionChanged()), this, SLOT(onProgramSelected()));
    connect(m_pAddStep, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onAddStep()));
    connect(m_pRemoveStep, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onRemoveStep()));
    connect(m_pSaveProgram, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onSaveProgram()));
    connect(m_pStart, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onStart()));
    connect(m_pStop, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onStop()));
    connect(m_pPause, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onPause()));
    connect(m_pResume, SIGNAL(clicked()), this, SLOT(onResume()));

    m_pStateTimer = new QTimer(this);
    connect(m_pStateTimer, SIGNAL(timeout()), this, SLOT(onPollState()));

    setConnected(false);
}

QString SequenceTab::currentProgram() const
{
    QListWidgetItem* it = m_pProgramList->currentItem();
    return it ? it->text() : QString();
}

void SequenceTab::onCreate()
{
    const QString name = m_pNewName->text().trimmed();
    if (name.isEmpty()) { emit log(tr("Enter a program name")); return; }
    ELoadError e = CELoadManager::instance().createProgram(TESTER_LOAD, name);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Program '%1' created").arg(name) : tr("Create: %1").arg(e.toString()));
    onRefresh();
}

void SequenceTab::onDelete()
{
    const QString name = currentProgram();
    if (name.isEmpty()) return;
    ELoadError e = CELoadManager::instance().deleteProgram(TESTER_LOAD, name);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Program '%1' deleted").arg(name) : tr("Delete: %1").arg(e.toString()));
    onRefresh();
}

void SequenceTab::onRefresh()
{
    QStringList progs;
    CELoadManager::instance().listPrograms(TESTER_LOAD, progs);
    m_pProgramList->clear();
    m_pProgramList->addItems(progs);
}

void SequenceTab::onProgramSelected()
{
    const QString name = currentProgram();
    if (name.isEmpty()) return;
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    mgr.selectProgram(TESTER_LOAD, name);
    S32BIT loop = 1;
    if (mgr.getProgramLoop(TESTER_LOAD, loop).isSuccess() && loop > 0) m_pLoop->setValue(loop);
}

void SequenceTab::onAddStep()
{
    const int row = m_pSteps->rowCount();
    m_pSteps->insertRow(row);
    const char* defs[] = { "0.1", "0.0", "0.0", "ON", "IMM", "0" };
    for (int c = 0; c < 6; ++c)
        m_pSteps->setItem(row, c, new QTableWidgetItem(QString::fromLatin1(defs[c])));
}

void SequenceTab::onRemoveStep()
{
    const int row = m_pSteps->currentRow();
    if (row >= 0) m_pSteps->removeRow(row);
}

void SequenceTab::onSaveProgram()
{
    const QString name = currentProgram();
    if (name.isEmpty()) { emit log(tr("Select a program first")); return; }
    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    mgr.selectProgram(TESTER_LOAD, name);
    mgr.setProgramLoop(TESTER_LOAD, m_pLoop->value());
    const int n = m_pSteps->rowCount();
    mgr.setProgramStepCount(TESTER_LOAD, static_cast<U32BIT>(n));
    for (int r = 0; r < n; ++r) {
        auto cell = [&](int c) { QTableWidgetItem* it = m_pSteps->item(r, c); return it ? it->text() : QString(); };
        mgr.setStepDwell(TESTER_LOAD, r, cell(0).toDouble());
        mgr.setStepLevel(TESTER_LOAD, r, cell(1).toDouble());
        // column 2 (Slew) has no per-step API on this family — left as display only.
        const QString load = cell(3).trimmed().toUpper();
        mgr.setStepInput(TESTER_LOAD, r, load == "ON" || load == "1" || load == "TRUE");
        mgr.setStepTransition(TESTER_LOAD, r,
            cell(4).trimmed().toUpper() == "RAMP" ? Enum_ELoad_Transition::m_enumRamp : Enum_ELoad_Transition::m_enumImmediate);
        mgr.setStepTriggerOut(TESTER_LOAD, r, cell(5).toUInt());
    }
    ELoadError e = mgr.saveProgram(TESTER_LOAD);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Program '%1' saved (%2 steps)").arg(name).arg(n)
                           : tr("Save: %1").arg(e.toString()));
}

void SequenceTab::onStart()
{
    ELoadError e = CELoadManager::instance().startSequence(TESTER_LOAD);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Sequence started") : tr("Start: %1").arg(e.toString()));
    m_pStateTimer->start(500);
}
void SequenceTab::onStop()
{
    ELoadError e = CELoadManager::instance().abortSequence(TESTER_LOAD);
    emit log(e.isSuccess() ? tr("Sequence stopped") : tr("Stop: %1").arg(e.toString()));
    onPollState();
}
void SequenceTab::onPause()
{
    CELoadManager::instance().suspendSequence(TESTER_LOAD); onPollState();
}
void SequenceTab::onResume()
{
    CELoadManager::instance().resumeSequence(TESTER_LOAD); onPollState();
}

void SequenceTab::onPollState()
{
    Enum_ELoad_SeqState st = Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumIdle; U32BIT step = 0, loop = 0;
    if (CELoadManager::instance().getSequenceState(TESTER_LOAD, st, step, loop).isSuccess()) {
        m_pState->setText(QString("%1  (step %2, loop %3)").arg(seqStateName(st)).arg(step).arg(loop));
        if (st == Enum_ELoad_SeqState::m_enumIdle) m_pStateTimer->stop();
    }
}

void SequenceTab::setConnected(bool in_bConnected)
{
    for (QPushButton* b : { m_pCreate, m_pDelete, m_pRefresh, m_pAddStep, m_pRemoveStep,
                            m_pSaveProgram, m_pStart, m_pStop, m_pPause, m_pResume })
        b->setEnabled(in_bConnected);
    if (in_bConnected) { onRefresh(); }
    else { m_pStateTimer->stop(); m_pProgramList->clear(); m_pState->setText(QStringLiteral("—")); }
}

-- GuiTester/main.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  main.cpp - "ELoad Plugin Test" entry point (dark-themed Qt Widgets app).
 *
 *  Usage: GuiTester [--smoke] [--screenshot <file>] [pluginDir]
 *    --smoke      : offscreen self-test (connect PLZ4005WH2 via MockVisa, read).
 *    --screenshot : render a connected window to an image (offscreen).
 *  Default pluginDir = <appDir>/../plugins.
 *==========================================================================*/
#include <QApplication>
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QPalette>
#include <QStyleFactory>

#include "ELoadTesterWindow.h"

static void applyDarkTheme(QApplication& app)
{
    app.setStyle(QStyleFactory::create(QStringLiteral("Fusion")));

    QPalette p;
    const QColor base(0x1e, 0x1f, 0x22), panel(0x2b, 0x2d, 0x30), text(0xd0, 0xd3, 0xd7);
    p.setColor(QPalette::Window, base);
    p.setColor(QPalette::WindowText, text);
    p.setColor(QPalette::Base, panel);
    p.setColor(QPalette::AlternateBase, base);
    p.setColor(QPalette::Text, text);
    p.setColor(QPalette::Button, panel);
    p.setColor(QPalette::ButtonText, text);
    p.setColor(QPalette::Highlight, QColor(0x2d, 0x6c, 0xdf));
    p.setColor(QPalette::HighlightedText, Qt::white);
    p.setColor(QPalette::ToolTipBase, panel);
    p.setColor(QPalette::ToolTipText, text);
    p.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::Text, QColor(0x6a, 0x6d, 0x70));
    p.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::ButtonText, QColor(0x6a, 0x6d, 0x70));
    app.setPalette(p);

    app.setStyleSheet(
        "QGroupBox { border: 1px solid #3a3d41; border-radius: 4px; margin-top: 12px; }"
        "QGroupBox::title { subcontrol-origin: margin; left: 8px; padding: 0 3px;"
        "                   color: #4da6ff; font-weight: bold; }"
        "QTabBar::tab { background: #2b2d30; color: #c0c3c7; padding: 6px 14px; }"
        "QTabBar::tab:selected { color: #4da6ff; border-bottom: 2px solid #4da6ff; }"
        "QTabWidget::pane { border: 1px solid #3a3d41; }"
        "QLineEdit, QComboBox, QDoubleSpinBox, QSpinBox, QPlainTextEdit, QListWidget, QTableWidget"
        "  { background: #26282b; border: 1px solid #3a3d41; border-radius: 3px; padding: 3px; }"
        "QPushButton { background: #2b2d30; border: 1px solid #3a3d41; border-radius: 3px; padding: 6px; }"
        "QPushButton:hover { border-color: #4da6ff; }"
        "QPushButton:disabled { color: #6a6d70; }");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv)
{
    bool bSmoke = false;
    QString strPluginDir, strShot;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        const QString a = QString::fromLocal8Bit(argv[i]);
        if (a == QStringLiteral("--smoke")) bSmoke = true;
        else if (a == QStringLiteral("--screenshot") && i + 1 < argc) strShot = QString::fromLocal8Bit(argv[++i]);
        else strPluginDir = a;
    }

    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    applyDarkTheme(app);
    if (strPluginDir.isEmpty())
        strPluginDir = QCoreApplication::applicationDirPath() + QStringLiteral("/../plugins");

    ELoadTesterWindow w(strPluginDir);
    if (bSmoke)         return w.runSmokeTest();
    if (!strShot.isEmpty()) return w.screenshotTo(strShot);

    w.show();
    return app.exec();
}

-- examples/BasicUsage/BasicUsage.pro

# =============================================================================
#  BasicUsage example - builds against the PACKAGED deliverables only.
#
#  Expected layout when built from inside deliverables/examples/BasicUsage:
#      deliverables/
#        include/   ELoadCore.h, Error.h
#        lib/       ELoadCore import lib + shared library
#        plugins/   model plugin DLLs
#        examples/BasicUsage/BasicUsage.pro
#
#  Run the built binary from a directory that has a 'plugins' folder beside
#  it (or copy the plugins next to the executable).
# =============================================================================
QT       -= gui
QT       += core

TARGET    = BasicUsage
TEMPLATE  = app
CONFIG   += console c++11
CONFIG   -= app_bundle

INCLUDEPATH += $$PWD/../../include

LIBS += -L$$PWD/../../lib -lELoadCore
unix: QMAKE_RPATHDIR += $$PWD/../../lib

SOURCES += main.cpp

-- examples/BasicUsage/main.cpp

/*============================================================================
 *  BasicUsage - minimal ELoad consumer.
 *
 *  Compiles and links against the DELIVERABLES ONLY: the public headers in
 *  include/ and the ELoadCore library; the model plugin DLLs are discovered
 *  at runtime from a 'plugins' folder. No model header or source is used.
 *
 *  It follows the framework's intended usage: load plugins, list models, open
 *  two independent instruments (a real model + the virtual SimLoad) keyed by
 *  load number, configure and measure, then disconnect.
 *==========================================================================*/
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDebug>

#include "ELoadManager.h"

int main(int argc, char* argv[])
{
    QCoreApplication app(argc, argv);

    CELoadManager& mgr = CELoadManager::instance();
    mgr.loadPlugins(QStringLiteral("plugins"));

    const QStringList models = mgr.getAvailablePlugins();
    if (models.isEmpty()) {
        qWarning() << "No plugins found in ./plugins";
        return 1;
    }
    qInfo() << "Available models:";
    for (const QString& name : models) {
        const S_ELoad_PluginInfo si = mgr.getPluginInfoByName(name);
        qInfo().noquote() << "  " << name << "-" << si.m_szManufacturer
                          << "(" << si.m_szDescription << ")";
    }

    /* A real instrument on load 1. Replace the resource with your VISA
     * address. With MockVisa (or no VISA) present this may return a
     * communication error off the bench - which is expected.              */
    const U32BIT REAL = 1;
    if (mgr.createInstance(REAL, QStringLiteral("PLZ4005WH2")).isSuccess()) {
        S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig cfg;
        cfg.setResourceString(QStringLiteral("USB0::0x0B3E::0x1042::XXXXXXXX::INSTR"));
        const ELoadError e = mgr.connect(REAL, cfg);
        if (e.isSuccess()) {
            mgr.setMode(REAL, 1, Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC);
            mgr.setCurrent(REAL, 1, 2.5);
            mgr.enableInput(REAL, 1, true);
            FDOUBLE volts = 0.0;
            mgr.measureVoltage(REAL, 1, volts);
            qInfo() << "PLZ4005WH2 voltage:" << volts << "V";
            mgr.disconnect(REAL);
        } else {
            qInfo().noquote() << "PLZ4005WH2 not connected (" << e.toString()
                              << ") - continuing with the virtual model.";
        }
        mgr.destroyInstance(REAL);
    }

    /* The virtual model on load 2 always works, with no hardware, no VISA.  */
    const U32BIT SIM = 2;
    if (mgr.createInstance(SIM, QStringLiteral("SimLoad")).isSuccess()) {
        S_ELoad_ConnectionConfig cfg;
        cfg.setResourceString(QStringLiteral("SIM::PLZ4005WH2"));
        if (mgr.connect(SIM, cfg).isSuccess()) {
            mgr.setMode(SIM, 1, Enum_ELoad_Mode::m_enumCC);
            mgr.setCurrent(SIM, 1, 3.0);
            mgr.enableInput(SIM, 1, true);
            FDOUBLE v = 0.0, i = 0.0, p = 0.0;
            mgr.measureVoltage(SIM, 1, v);
            mgr.measureCurrent(SIM, 1, i);
            mgr.measurePower(SIM, 1, p);
            qInfo() << "SimLoad reads" << v << "V," << i << "A," << p << "W";
            mgr.disconnect(SIM);
        }
        mgr.destroyInstance(SIM);
    }

    return 0;
}

-- scripts/package_deliverables.sh

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
#  package_deliverables.sh - build and assemble the shippable ELoad deliverables.
#
#  deliverables/
#    include/   public SDK headers (framework + types + error + VISA)
#    lib/       ELoadCore shared library + a VISA library (MockVisa here;
#               replace with the vendor NI-VISA on a real bench)
#    plugins/   model plugin DLLs (+ SimLoad)
#    bin/       AutoTest, GuiTester
#    examples/  BasicUsage
#    docs/      architecture, API reference, integration + add-a-model guides
#
#  The script ABORTS if any internal header (S_PlzLimits.h, CMockPlzEmulator.h)
#  or any model/mock source would leak into the deliverables.
#
#  Usage: scripts/package_deliverables.sh [debug|release]   (default release)
# =============================================================================
set -euo pipefail

BUILD_KIND="${1:-release}"
SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
ROOT_DIR="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/.." && pwd)"
BUILD_DIR="${ROOT_DIR}/build"
OUT_DIR="${ROOT_DIR}/deliverables"

case "$(uname -s)" in
    Linux*)  LIBGLOB="libELoadCore.so* libvisa.so*"; PLUGINGLOB="*.so"; BINGLOB="AutoTest GuiTester" ;;
    *)       LIBGLOB="ELoadCore.dll ELoadCore.a visa*.dll"; PLUGINGLOB="*.dll"; BINGLOB="AutoTest.exe GuiTester.exe" ;;
esac

echo "ELoad packaging: build=${BUILD_KIND}"

# ---- build the whole tree (shadow) -----------------------------------------
rm -rf "${BUILD_DIR}"
mkdir -p "${BUILD_DIR}"
( cd "${BUILD_DIR}" && qmake "CONFIG+=${BUILD_KIND}" "${ROOT_DIR}/ELoad.pro" && make -j"$(nproc 2>/dev/null || echo 2)" )

# ---- fresh output tree ------------------------------------------------------
rm -rf "${OUT_DIR}"
mkdir -p "${OUT_DIR}/include" "${OUT_DIR}/lib" "${OUT_DIR}/plugins" \
         "${OUT_DIR}/bin" "${OUT_DIR}/examples" "${OUT_DIR}/docs"

# ---- public SDK headers (the whole include/ dir) ---------------------------
cp "${ROOT_DIR}/include/"*.h "${OUT_DIR}/include/"

# ---- core + VISA library ---------------------------------------------------
# Split LIBGLOB into patterns WITHOUT pathname expansion (set -f), then glob
# each pattern attached to the lib dir under nullglob.
set -f; read -ra LIB_PATTERNS <<< "${LIBGLOB}"; set +f
shopt -s nullglob
for pattern in "${LIB_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
    for f in "${BUILD_DIR}/lib/"${pattern}; do
        [[ -e "${f}" ]] && cp -a "${f}" "${OUT_DIR}/lib/"
    done
done
# ---- plugins ---------------------------------------------------------------
for f in "${BUILD_DIR}/plugins/"${PLUGINGLOB}; do
    cp -a "${f}" "${OUT_DIR}/plugins/"
done
# ---- testers ---------------------------------------------------------------
for b in ${BINGLOB}; do
    [[ -e "${BUILD_DIR}/bin/${b}" ]] && cp -a "${BUILD_DIR}/bin/${b}" "${OUT_DIR}/bin/"
done
shopt -u nullglob

# ---- examples + docs -------------------------------------------------------
cp -a "${ROOT_DIR}/examples/." "${OUT_DIR}/examples/"
cp -a "${ROOT_DIR}/docs/."     "${OUT_DIR}/docs/"

# ============================================================================
#  LEAK GUARDS
# ============================================================================
FAIL=0

echo "guard: checking include/ for internal headers ..."
FORBIDDEN_HEADERS=(S_PlzLimits.h CMockPlzEmulator.h SimLoadPlugin.h \
                   PLZ1005WH2Plugin.h PLZ2005WH2Plugin.h PLZ4005WH2Plugin.h \
                   PLZ12005WH2Plugin.h PLZ20005WH2Plugin.h)
for h in "${FORBIDDEN_HEADERS[@]}"; do
    if [[ -e "${OUT_DIR}/include/${h}" ]]; then
        echo "  LEAK: internal header ${h} present in include/" >&2
        FAIL=1
    fi
done

echo "guard: checking for internal source/headers anywhere in the package ..."
# The model plugins are self-contained (own .h/.cpp) - none of their sources,
# the SimLoad/MockVisa sources or the internal limits header may ship.
FORBIDDEN_SOURCES=(SimLoadPlugin.cpp SimLoadPlugin.h MockVisa.cpp \
                   CMockPlzEmulator.cpp CMockPlzEmulator.h S_PlzLimits.h \
                   PLZ1005WH2Plugin.cpp PLZ2005WH2Plugin.cpp PLZ4005WH2Plugin.cpp \
                   PLZ12005WH2Plugin.cpp PLZ20005WH2Plugin.cpp \
                   PLZ1005WH2Plugin.h PLZ2005WH2Plugin.h PLZ4005WH2Plugin.h \
                   PLZ12005WH2Plugin.h PLZ20005WH2Plugin.h)
while IFS= read -r -d '' f; do
    base="$(basename "${f}")"
    for s in "${FORBIDDEN_SOURCES[@]}"; do
        if [[ "${base}" == "${s}" ]]; then
            echo "  LEAK: internal file ${f#${OUT_DIR}/} present in deliverables" >&2
            FAIL=1
        fi
    done
done < <(find "${OUT_DIR}" -type f -print0)

if [[ "${FAIL}" -ne 0 ]]; then
    echo "PACKAGING ABORTED: leak guard failed." >&2
    rm -rf "${OUT_DIR}"
    exit 2
fi

echo "OK: deliverables assembled at ${OUT_DIR}"
echo "  include:  $(ls "${OUT_DIR}/include"  | tr '\n' ' ')"
echo "  lib:      $(ls "${OUT_DIR}/lib"      | tr '\n' ' ')"
echo "  plugins:  $(ls "${OUT_DIR}/plugins"  | tr '\n' ' ')"
echo "  bin:      $(ls "${OUT_DIR}/bin"      | tr '\n' ' ')"
echo "  docs:     $(ls "${OUT_DIR}/docs"     | tr '\n' ' ')"
